# Claude context — Elorae

Quick orientation for any new Claude Code session in this repo. Read this first, then `docs/BOUNDARY.md` for the architectural contract.

## What this is

pnpm + Turborepo monorepo for an ERP + Jubelio marketplace integration.

```
apps/web/        Next.js 16 App Router — the ERP UI + NextAuth + node-cron (VPS)
apps/api/        NestJS 11 — Jubelio integration service (token, webhooks, queue)
packages/db/     Prisma 7 schema + generated client + MariaDB adapter (shared)
docs/BOUNDARY.md Service-boundary contract — source of truth for who-writes-what
reference/       Local-only planning artifacts (gitignored). EPIC todos now live on the GitHub board (epic issues).
```

Database: **MariaDB 11.4** self-hosted in the docker-compose stack on the Hostinger VPS. Local dev reaches it through an SSH tunnel — same DB, same data, both environments. Migrated off TiDB Cloud Serverless 2026-06-28 after the free tier exhausted its monthly quota.

## Authoritative docs (read these before changing architecture)

- `docs/BOUNDARY.md` — service responsibilities, data ownership (per-table write owners), communication patterns (sync HTTP vs outbox vs webhooks), auth model, failure modes, anti-patterns, decisions log.
- `docs/INTEGRATION-GUIDE.md` — how to use the Jubelio-touching surface (outbox enqueue, stock adjustments, signed channel).
- `docs/superpowers/specs/` + `docs/superpowers/plans/` — per-feature design specs + implementation plans (local-only, gitignored). Each feature follows brainstorm → spec → plan → implement → PR.
- `apps/web/README.md` — ERP feature list.
- `README.md` (root) — local setup, env layout, dev/prod commands, ngrok demo.

## Code conventions

- **Double quotes** for string literals in all TS/JS source (whole monorepo, including `apps/web`). No ESLint quote rule enforces single — safe.
- **No comments on Prisma schema models.** Field-level `//` comments forbidden; rationale lives in `docs/`. Migration SQL `--` comments are fine.
- **Adding a `DocType` enum member requires updating TWO `Record<DocType, …>` maps, not one.** `apps/web/lib/docNumber.ts` (the generator) AND `apps/web/app/actions/settings/doc-numbers.ts` (the Settings → Document Numbers seeder) each hold their own `DEFAULT_CONFIGS` with identical shape. Missing either is a **build-breaking** type error that `apps/web` type-check would catch — but that check never runs locally here, so it surfaces only in the Docker build inside the deploy workflow, *after* the api image and the database migrations have already succeeded. That is the dangerous shape: prod schema moves forward while the web app silently stays on the old image. It happened on the delivery branch (PR #229 → deploy `31293514640`). The Settings → Nomor dokumen screen no longer has its own 8-member label map: it lists every Prisma `DocType` through `app/backoffice/settings/documents/jenis-dokumen.ts` (a `Partial` map — a new member lands under "Lainnya" instead of breaking the build; `jenis-dokumen.spec.ts`).
- **Branch names + commit messages + PR titles + spec docs are shared artifacts** — no EPIC-XX or L1-L9 labels in them. Use feature names. EPIC-XX labels live only on the GitHub board (epic issue titles) — never in shared code artifacts.
- **One-liner commit messages.** No body. No `Co-Authored-By` trailer. Conventional Commits format (`feat(api): ...`, `fix(db): ...`, etc.).
- **Subpath exports for `@elorae/db` pure helpers.** Client-component imports must use `@elorae/db/color` or `@elorae/db/pantone` (NOT the main barrel) to avoid dragging Prisma/mariadb into the client bundle.

## Workflow conventions

- **Match existing UI patterns before inventing new ones.** Before writing any new page/form/list, open a sibling module and copy the shape. Backoffice CRUD list reference = `apps/web/app/backoffice/purchase-orders/PurchaseOrdersPageClient.tsx` (header row + inline filter row + Card-wrapped table with CardHeader icon+title, empty state inside CardContent, no `p-6` on the server page — layout handles padding). Backoffice form reference = `apps/web/app/backoffice/suppliers/`. PWA reference = `apps/web/app/pwa/HomeShell.tsx` + `apps/web/app/pwa/stores/StoreList.tsx` (icon-prefixed rows, `bg-primary text-primary-foreground` icon circles for contrast on dark theme, `Card`/`Badge`/`Button` from `@/components/ui/*` — never native `<button>`/`<input>`/`<table>`). Grep + read one existing example before drafting.
- **Plan before implementing** any non-trivial feature. Brainstorming → spec doc (`docs/superpowers/specs/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-design.md`) → plan doc (`docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-plan.md`) → implement → PR. The `superpowers:*` skills enforce this when invoked. `docs/superpowers/` is local-only (gitignored) — specs/plans never ship in PRs.
- **TDD for non-trivial logic.** Pure functions, handlers, routers, processors get failing tests first. Module-wiring and infra-glue files are exempt.
- **Never force-push master.** Past divergence with a teammate's monolith branch was resolved by *recreating* their content in the monorepo, not by force-merging. See `project_master_divergence` memory.
- **`git check-ignore -v` before staging any dotfile** to confirm it's actually ignored.
- **Never commit secrets.** Even when staging looks clean, double-check `git diff --cached` for `.env`-shaped content.

## Local services (more detail in `README.md`)

| Port | Service | Start | Stop |
|------|---------|-------|------|
| 3000 | apps/web dev (Next.js) | `pnpm -F @elorae/web dev` | `pkill -f "next dev"` |
| 3001 | apps/api (NestJS, prod-mode for queue work) | `pnpm prod:api` or `pnpm -F @elorae/api prod:start` | `pkill -f "node dist/main"` |
| 6379 | Redis (BullMQ for apps/api) | `docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up -d redis` (first time) or `docker start elorae-dev-redis` | `docker stop elorae-dev-redis` |
| ngrok | Public tunnel to apps/api for Jubelio | `ngrok http --url unclean-noncalumniating-cory.ngrok-free.dev 3001` | `pkill -f "ngrok http"` |

**Restart order:** Redis → api → web. Static ngrok domain is account-bound; Jubelio webhook config keeps working across restarts.

## Production hosts

| Host | Service | Notes |
|------|---------|-------|
| Hostinger VPS (`elorae.cloud`) | apps/web (Next.js, Docker Compose) | Deploy runs automatically on push to master: GitHub Actions builds the image → pushes to `ghcr.io/okejob-elorae/elorae-web` → the VPS **pulls** it (CI-image cutover, PR #106 — the VPS no longer builds). Manual/rollback: `ssh elorae@api.elorae.cloud && cd /srv/elorae && git pull && IMAGE_TAG=<sha> docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml pull web && docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml up -d --no-build web` (omit `IMAGE_TAG` → `:master`), or Actions → Deploy to VPS → Run workflow with an `image_tag`. Caddy auto-SSL. Vercel DECOMMISSIONED 2026-06-18. |
| Hostinger VPS (`api.elorae.cloud`) | apps/api + Redis + Caddy (Docker Compose) | Same CI-image flow (PR #106): GHA builds → `ghcr.io/okejob-elorae/elorae-api` → VPS pulls, no on-VPS build. Manual/rollback: `ssh elorae@api.elorae.cloud && cd /srv/elorae && git pull && IMAGE_TAG=<sha> docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml pull api && docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml up -d --no-build api`. Caddy handles auto-SSL. Webhook URL: `https://api.elorae.cloud/webhooks/jubelio/<event>`. See `README.md §Production deploy` for first-time setup + ops commands. |
| VPS MariaDB (docker `db` service) | MySQL-compatible DB | Port 3306 bound to 127.0.0.1 on the VPS. Local dev tunnels via `ssh -fNL 3306:127.0.0.1:3306 elorae@api.elorae.cloud`. `DATABASE_URL` lives in each platform's env store. |

ngrok stays available as a fallback for local-only demo work (laptop apps/api + temporary public tunnel). VPS is the authoritative prod target.

## Env layout

- `apps/web/.env` — Next.js env. Holds the shared `DATABASE_URL` (single source of truth).
- `apps/api/.env` — api-only keys (`JUBELIO_*`, `SWAGGER_*`, `PORT`, `CORS_ORIGINS`, `REDIS_URL`). No `DATABASE_URL` — `apps/api/src/bootstrap-env.ts` cascades it from `apps/web/.env`.
- Cascade order in api: `apps/api/.env` → `<root>/.env` → `apps/web/.env`. Earlier wins per key (dotenv no-override).
- In prod (VPS + local-with-ngrok): each platform injects env from its own store; cascade is irrelevant.
- **Secrets that have appeared in any chat transcript are compromised.** Rotate `DATABASE_URL` password, `JUBELIO_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `SWAGGER_PASS` if you accidentally paste them.

## Architecture nuances worth flagging

- **Field sales (putus/konsi) is PER-VARIANT as of PR #144 (2026-07-17) — D15 SUPERSEDED.** `InventoryValue` is keyed `(itemId, variantSku)` and Jubelio-ingested variant items are stored entirely per-variant (real `variantSku` rows, no pooled row) — prod-confirmed. The PWA catalog exposes per-variant availability (`CatalogItem.variants[]`); a variant sheet picks variants; putus/konsi order lines carry the **real** `variantSku`; reserve/consume already keyed per line → per-variant `StockReservation`. **Min-qty aggregates per item**; **promos aggregate per item then pro-rate** across variant lines via shared `applyItemAggregatedPromos` (writer + preview → quote == recorded order). **Simple items (no `Item.variants`) stay item-level** (`variants: []`, inline stepper, cart `variantSku ""`, null/"" tolerant lookup). **Canvassing (van load/sale/reconcile) is ALSO per-variant (Track B, PR #146):** `VanStock`/van docs keyed per `variantSku`, backoffice load form picks per-variant (item block), PWA van sell groups by item + variant sheet, sale/reconcile stamp the variant label into `productName`; no schema migration; van stock has no reservation dimension. See `docs/BOUNDARY.md` D15. Caveat: a variant item restocked via ERP GRN/production writes a `null` pooled row the per-variant sale won't see (Jubelio feeds them per-variant, so rare).
- **Putus stock now leaves at DELIVERY, not at approve (branch `feat/field-sales-delivery`, PR pending).** Approve is paperwork: it no longer consumes stock and no longer writes `SalesHistory`. A putus order ships in parts through `FieldSalesDelivery`/`FieldSalesDeliveryLine` (`recordFieldSalesDelivery` in `apps/web/lib/field-sales/delivery/writer.ts`), each delivery consuming its own slice via `consumeFieldSalesOrderPartial` and writing its own `SalesHistory` rows keyed by the delivery `docNo` (an order number would collide on the second delivery of the same variant). `FieldSalesOrder.deliveryStatus` (PENDING/PARTIAL/DELIVERED/CLOSED) plus `FieldSalesOrderLine.deliveredQty`/`cancelledQty` track progress; `closeFieldSalesOrderRemainder` cancels every still-open line at once, releases the leftover reservation and stamps `closedAt`/`closedById`/`closeReason`. **`FieldSalesDelivery.docNo` is NOT always `DLV/`-prefixed** — the backfill migration gave every pre-existing approved putus order one full delivery carrying the ORDER number, so never pattern-match the prefix. Order/line discounts are pro-rated per delivery and the closing delivery absorbs every remainder, including residue stranded on a line that finished in an earlier delivery, so the deliveries sum back to the order total exactly. Konsi never delivers (transfer is still recorded at approve).
- **Every nota tagihan carries exactly one `TaxInvoice` row (PR #239, merged 2026-08-13).** `TaxInvoice` is a satellite table 1:1 with `FieldSalesDelivery` (`deliveryId @unique`, no columns added to the delivery), created inside `recordFieldSalesDelivery`'s existing serializable transaction and backfilled for every pre-existing delivery — so `PENDING` has exactly one spelling and the queue is keyed on the nota being ISSUED, never on anyone printing it. Statuses are `PENDING`/`CREATED` (needs a faktur number)/`NOT_REQUIRED` (needs a reason), and every transition is reversible and writes its `AuditLog` row (with `changes` + `reason`) **in the same transaction as the update** — the row itself is last-write-wins, so the audit trail is the only surviving record of a superseded faktur number. Finance works the queue at `/backoffice/finance/faktur-pajak` gated on `tax_invoices:view`/`tax_invoices:manage`. First print of a nota tagihan stamps `notaPrintedAt` through an atomic compare-and-swap (`updateMany … where notaPrintedAt: null`) and fans out a `TAX_INVOICE_PENDING` notification only when the affected count is 1; reprints still audit but never re-notify. Writers in `apps/web/lib/tax-invoices/`, print action in `apps/web/app/actions/field-sales-deliveries.ts`. **The migration declares NO foreign key** (`relationMode = "prisma"`), so a `TaxInvoice` can outlive its delivery — the queue query skips orphans rather than letting one dangling row blank the whole page.
- **An approving admin can ADD never-sent products to a konsi transfer (branch `feat/konsi-never-sent-suggestions`, PR pending).** `listKonsiSuggestions` (`apps/web/lib/field-sales/queries.ts`) lists goods never sent to this store — exclusion is **item-level** (`sentItemIds`, any variant ever sent kills the whole item, matching the writer's `ALREADY_SENT` check) while each surviving item expands to **one row per real `InventoryValue` variant**, because availability and the writer's `addedLines` are both per-variant; a `null`/`""` dual row dedupes to one suggestion at the **minimum** available (the writer reserves against a single findFirst row, so summing would offer more than it can honor). The panel (`KonsiSuggestionsCard`) stages additions client-side and `approveFieldSalesOrderAction` applies them **inside the approve transaction** — validated (unknown item, no inventory row, bad qty, duplicate, already-sent, not-konsi) before any write, priced by the same konsi gross-up as the salesman's own lines, and stamped `FieldSalesOrderLine.addedById` so the detail page can badge them. An already-APPROVED order **refuses** a payload carrying additions rather than returning its idempotent `ok` (it would report a success that created nothing). **No new permission — reuses `field_sales_orders:approve`, so nothing to seed on prod.** The client's "barang baru" wording is deliberately NOT implemented: only the never-sent criterion ships, because whether "baru" means newly-created, newly-in-season or never-sent is still an open client question (design doc D5).
- **VPS MariaDB is shared between local dev and the VPS-deployed web.** A local destructive query (delete, truncate, bad migration) hits the same data the client demo shows. Run migrations only via `pnpm -F @elorae/db migrate:deploy`; never `migrate dev --create-only` without thinking. **On prod, the deploy workflow's `migrate` job auto-applies pending migrations on every push to master, so no manual `migrate:deploy` post-merge. It does NOT seed** — RBAC/permission rows are seeded by hand (surgical SQL). Don't conflate the two. **`migrate` is gated on the image builds as of PR #240** (`needs: [pull, build-push-web, build-push-api]` + `always()` with per-build result checks): the builds are where `next build`/`tsc` type-check, and they used to RACE the migration as siblings — so a type error let the schema advance and then failed the build, leaving prod on the old image against the new schema (that is what happened on PR #229 → deploy `31293514640`). The `always()` is load-bearing, not decoration: both build jobs are `if:`-gated on the path filter and are legitimately SKIPPED on a single-service change, and a skipped job in `needs` cascade-skips its dependents — without it an api-only push would deploy nothing at all. Skipped is fine; failed is not. No automatic backups — set up daily `mariadb-dump` → R2 separately (TODO).
- **Prisma 7 `updateMany` is NOT an atomic compare-and-swap.** Measured 2026-10-01 with query logging: `updateMany({ where: { id, status: "DIKIRIM" } })` runs `SELECT id … WHERE id = $1 AND status = $2` and then `UPDATE … WHERE id IN (<ids>)` — the state condition is not re-applied at write time, so under READ COMMITTED two concurrent writers both report `count: 1`. A real-DB concurrency test (`lib/receivables/koreksi.test.ts`, "balapan") showed a salesperson's withdrawal and finance's verification BOTH succeeding until both writers ran inside `runSerializable`, where the database rejects one (Postgres 40001 / MariaDB deadlock, retried by `withRetry`). **Any guard of the form "updateMany where state = X, check count" must run inside `runSerializable`,** and needs a `Promise.all` race test to prove it. `putuskanKlaim` and `batalkanKlaim` were moved to `runSerializable` for this; the rest of the codebase is not yet audited (follow-up below).
- **`Item` is dual-owner** (web for ERP forms; api for Jubelio catalog ingest). All api writes go through `@elorae/db/item-writer.ts` helpers, stamping `source = JUBELIO_INGEST`. See `docs/BOUNDARY.md §3.3`.
- **`StockAdjustment` is dual-owner** (web for ERP-driven adjustments; api for Jubelio stock webhooks). Api writes go through `@elorae/db/stock-writer.ts` `applyJubelioStockAdjustment`, stamping `source = JUBELIO_WEBHOOK` + `idempotencyKey`. See `docs/BOUNDARY.md §3.1`.
- **The "admin wildcard" is granted in CODE, not by a DB row.** `apps/web/lib/auth.ts:70` sets `permissions = ['*']` whenever the user's `RoleDefinition.isSystem` is true (ADMIN), and `hasPermission` short-circuits on `'*'`. There is no `*` Permission row, and ADMIN's explicit `RolePermission` links are irrelevant to access — on prod ADMIN is missing 5 of them (`journals:*`, `field_sales_orders:*`, `canvassing:manage`) with no effect whatsoever. So a post-merge permission seed does NOT unblock admin access, which already works; it makes the permission a real row so it can be granted to NON-admin roles and appear in role management. Seeding a new role with `isSystem = 1` would hand it full admin — the seeds correctly use `0`.
- **Hand-run SQL against prod needs `--skip-ssl`.** MariaDB 11.4's client requires TLS by default and the VPS server has none configured, so a bare `mariadb`/`mariadb-dump` fails with `error 2026: TLS/SSL error: SSL is required, but the server does not support it`. In a pipeline that failure is silent — `mariadb-dump ... | gzip` returns gzip's exit status, and produces a valid 123-byte empty archive. Always verify a dump with its size and a `Dump completed on` trailer. Also set `SET SESSION max_statement_time = <seconds>` before any aggregate over `SalesOrder`: a client-side timeout leaves the query running server-side, which wedged the prod tunnel twice on 2026-08-08.
- **Jubelio webhook signature is HMAC-SHA256 with header `Sign`** — not plain SHA256 with `webhook-signature`. Jubelio's docs *text* is wrong; their code example is correct. See `docs/BOUNDARY.md §9 Q1`.
- **Before using or adding ANY Jubelio endpoint, check the OpenAPI spec FIRST** (`reference/jubelio-openAPI.yaml`; memory `reference_jubelio_openapi`) — grep the path for its documented params/request/response schema. Don't guess or reverse-engineer from live probing. BUT the spec lags/misdescribes the live API (same class as the webhook-sig gotcha), so **verify the real shape live** before relying on it. Concrete gotcha found 2026-07-26 (salesorder `no→id` resolve): the spec's purpose-built `POST /wms/order/getOrderByNo/` is **pick-flow-scoped** — returns HTTP 200 with an *empty body* for completed/settled orders, so it's unusable for settlement backfill. The `q` search on `/sales/orders/completed|cancel|failed/` (what `findSalesOrderIdByNo` uses) is itself **undocumented** (spec lists only `page`/`pageSize`) and only covers those 3 status buckets → misses in-flight orders. The only lookup that resolved every status live is the (also undocumented) `GET /sales/orders/?q=<salesorder_no>` general list. Net: consult the spec, then confirm live.
- **Catalog ingest pulls from Jubelio** via `POST /jubelio/catalog/sync` (apps/api). Product push (ERP → Jubelio) is wired via the outbox — see sub-3 below.
- **Inbound webhook pipeline ships in BullMQ-backed queue.** `JubelioWebhookEvent` carries authoritative status (`RECEIVED → PROCESSING → PROCESSED / SKIPPED / DEAD`). Worker runs in apps/api process, concurrency 4 by default (`JUBELIO_WORKER_CONCURRENCY`). Sweeper rescues stuck rows every 10 min. Note: shared-state writers (e.g. `InventoryValue` aggregates) must use atomic Prisma `increment`/`decrement`, never read-modify-write — the worker is concurrent and the ERP-ship path races the ship-webhook across processes.
- **Outbound push pipeline ships in `JubelioOutbox` table + `outbox-poller` + `outbox-processor`.** Handlers in `apps/api/src/jubelio/outbox/handlers/`: `product_push`, `stock_push`, `salesorder_pick`, `salesorder_pack`, `salesorder_ship`. Already-in-state Jubelio responses are skipped (not retried). New push types add: handler file + payload builder + spec + router case.
- **Marketplace stock is reserve-at-ingest, consume-at-ship, release-on-cancel.** `StockReservation` ledger (one row per `salesorderDetailId`) + aggregate `InventoryValue.reservedQty`, driven by `reserveOrder`/`consumeOrder`/`releaseOrder` in `@elorae/db/reservation-writer.ts`. `available = qtyOnHand - reservedQty`, derived at read time — never stored. `consumeOrder` deducts `qtyOnHand` via a `StockAdjustment` stamped `source = FULFILLMENT_CONSUME`, and is idempotent so the Jubelio ship webhook and the ERP Ship button can both trigger it safely. Jubelio stock push sends `available`, not raw `qtyOnHand`. See `docs/BOUNDARY.md` D6.
- **PWA service worker gotchas** (cost hours 2026-07-06). (1) `apps/web/proxy.ts` IS the Next 16 middleware — `proxy.ts` is Next 16's rename of `middleware.ts` (compiles to `.next/server/middleware.js`, runs on every request); it is NOT dead code. Its allow-list gates `/pwa/*`; the Serwist SW script `/pwa/sw.js` is currently gated (307 → /login unauth) — a SW script behind auth is wrong (breaks update fetches). (2) The `/pwa` Serwist SW (`app/pwa/sw.ts` → `public/pwa/sw.js`) only exists in a **production build** (`next build --webpack`; disabled in dev, and Turbopack skips the plugin per PR #92) → offline behavior is testable **only on prod** (local `next start` over http has a NextAuth secure-cookie login quirk). (3) **Never commit next-pwa/serwist generated files** (`public/sw.js`, `public/workbox-*.js`, `public/pwa/sw.js`, gitignored) — a stale committed `workbox-<hash>.js` rides the Docker `COPY` into the build, poisons the SW precache manifest, then 404s at runtime → SW install fails → offline dinos (root cause of the #113 field-test failure; fixed #115). (4) Root `/` next-pwa SW (scope `/`, caches `/^https?.*/`) and the `/pwa` Serwist SW coexist.

Client-feedback batch (meeting 26/07/2026, tracked on GitHub issue **#206** — not an EPIC). Field-sales features shipped outside the EPIC tables; key surfaces so a future session finds them:

- **Price appeal (#207)** — putus is now **server-priced** (`computeStorePrice` PUTUS at create, `field-sales/writer.ts`); the salesman can only *appeal* (`requestedUnitPrice` + `appealReason` on a line), the owner sets the final price at approve via `finalPrices` in `approveFieldSalesOrderAction`. Konsi unchanged.
- **SPG role (#208)** — new seeded `SPG` RoleDefinition + `User.assignedStoreId` (one fixed store). NEW `SpgSale`/`SpgSaleLine` (`SPGSALE` doc type) — a terminal sale (server-priced at the STORE's terms since 2026-10-06: in-store gross-up price at KONSI stores, `SalesHistory` OFFLINE, idempotencyKey). It never touches main `InventoryValue`; since 2026-09-29 it writes one JUAL row per line into the store stock book (`StoreStockMovement`, see the store-stock follow-up). Check-in/sale server-scoped to `assignedStoreId` + active-visit-required. `recordSpgSale` writer; `/backoffice/spg-sales` register (`spg_sales:view`). Prod RBAC seed `seed-spg-role-permissions.sql` — run 2026-08-08 (role + 2 permissions + links). Accounts, passwords and the SPG's fixed store are set in Settings → Pengguna (`/backoffice/settings/users`).
- **Pack-ratio (#210)** — global size→qty pack config in `SystemSetting["putus.packRatio"]` (Settings → Rasio Pack). Pure `@elorae/db/pack-ratio` `expandPack`/`parsePackRatio`/`validatePackRatio` (client-safe subpath). Perms `settings_pack_ratio:*` (`seed-pack-ratio-permissions.sql`, run on prod 2026-08-08).
- **Smart request (#211)** — PWA putus **cart builder** (`/pwa/stores/[id]/smart-request`): categories + packs → pure `planSmartRequest` (`lib/field-sales/smart-request/plan.ts`) ranks (never-ordered-first → APPROVED qty) + walks 1-pack-per-article via `expandPack`, drops-and-continues on short/missing, → normal putus order. `createFieldSalesOrder` gained a **server-set `skipMinQty`** (smart-request submit passes true; manual catalog cart still enforces min-qty). `getSmartRequestHistory`, `loadSmartRequestCandidates`, `buildSmartRequestAction`/`submitSmartRequestOrder`. **Superseded 2026-10-01 by "Rencana kirim" on the same route** (owner: "isi jenis barang, 6/12 pcs, barang yang belum pernah dikirim … sehingga bisa open order otomatis"): PUTUS **and KONSI**; criteria = categories, quantity per article (1 pack via `expandPack` / 6 / 12 / N pcs split by pack-ratio proportions with largest remainder, never above per-variant available, shortfall reported per size), sources in priority order never-sent → sells here (store book/titipan 60-day sales vs shelf+in-transit, or approved order history for PUTUS without a book) → sells at similar stores (`bahanSaranTawar`, pcs + store count only) → new goods (`konsi.newItemDays`), skip-if-shelf ≥ X, max articles (round-robin across categories), PUTUS-only budget; unpriced PUTUS goods are skipped with a reason. The result is ALWAYS a draft the salesman edits and sends through `createFieldSalesOrder` (one idempotency key per draft). Pure logic `lib/field-sales/smart-request/rencana.ts`, loader `rencana-queries.ts`, phone payload `rencana-draf.ts` (KONSI drafts carry NO price/total/budget keys — JSON-tested), client-safe constants `kriteria.ts`. **Client components may value-import only `kriteria.ts`/`saran-tawar.ts`; `rencana*.ts` is `import type` only** (it pulls Prisma via `lib/konsinyasi/stok-di-toko.ts`). `nilaiStokToko` reads consignment/receivables tables that exist only as Postgres migrations, so on MariaDB this route fails for stores with a stock book/titipan until those migrations exist. `plan.ts` is now used only by its test (kept for the `PlanHistory` type); `pwa.smartRequest.*` keys are unused.
- **Putus delivery (branch `feat/field-sales-delivery`, PR pending)** — stock leaves at delivery instead of approval; orders ship in parts; nota gudang + nota tagihan print per delivery (`DeliveriesCard` on the order detail); a backfill migration gives every pre-existing approved putus order one full delivery. New permission `field_sales_orders:deliver` — **run `packages/db/prisma/seed-field-sales-delivery-permissions.sql` on prod post-merge.** See the architecture nuance above.
- **Faktur pajak tracking (PR #239, merged 2026-08-13)** — one `TaxInvoice` per nota tagihan, worked from `/backoffice/finance/faktur-pajak`; see the architecture nuance above. **Both post-merge steps are DONE — do not re-run them blindly:**
  1. ✅ DONE 2026-08-13 — ran `packages/db/prisma/seed-tax-invoice-permissions.sql` on prod (`tax_invoices:view` + `tax_invoices:manage` + the ADMIN links) — the deploy pipeline migrates but never seeds.
  2. ✅ NO-OP 2026-08-13 — prod had **0 `FieldSalesDelivery` rows**, so the backfill had nothing to insert and the deploy window could not have stranded anything. The step and its reasoning stay recorded because it becomes real the next time this table is migrated with data in it. Originally: **after the new web image is live**, re-run the backfill `INSERT` from `packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260813120000_add_tax_invoice/migration.sql` once. The deploy workflow applies migrations BEFORE the new image goes up, so any delivery recorded by the OLD image in that window gets no `TaxInvoice` at all: it never appears in the finance queue, and its first-print compare-and-swap matches zero rows so finance is never notified either. **Both surfaces fail closed and silent** — nothing anywhere reports the missing row. The `INSERT` is `NOT EXISTS`-guarded (and the whole migration file is now `IF NOT EXISTS`/re-runnable), so re-running it is safe and idempotent.
- **Konsi "baru" (new product) is an ASSUMPTION, not a client answer.** The konsi client story asks the admin to substitute "barang-barang yang **baru** dan belum pernah kirim" when the salesman's requested SKUs are short. Never-sent is answerable today (`sentItemIds`); **new is not defined anywhere**. Working assumption pending client confirmation: new = `Item.createdAt` within N days, N held in `SystemSetting["konsi.newItemDays"]` (default **90** — roughly one apparel season — with an absent row falling back in code, same shape as `checkin.radiusMeters`). Open questions recorded on card #22: whether "baru" means a new *product* or a new *production run*, whether 90 days is the right window, and whether a new product should be suggested even to a store that has had it before. Build the never-sent half first; it is unaffected by the answer.
- **Store price-rule** — still blocked (decision doc in #206 comments; options A–E, awaiting client pick). Reminder: the **Promo engine (#111)** already covers per-store/per-item PERCENT/FIXED/TIERED discounts — don't rebuild it; the gap is standing base-price granularity.

|----|-------|--------|
| **11-01** | Settlement upload + parse — `POST /api/finance/settlements/upload` → `parseSettlement()`; Shopee reads all four sheets (Summary/Income/Adjustment/Seller Fee), 43 Income columns; parse failures return 422 with `{sheet, row, message}[]` | ✅ shipped. Error *display* was broken until `fix/settlement-parse-error-display` (the client typed the payload as `string[]` and rendered objects into JSX → React "Objects are not valid as a React child" crash on any malformed upload) |
| **11-02** | Match orders — `matchSettlement` + `salesorderNoForSettlement` (`match-key.ts`, prefix-aware: Shopee `salesorder_no`, TikTok/Tokopedia `ref_no` OR `salesorder_no`); unmatched list + per-line resync | ✅ shipped (PRs #165/#166) |
| **11-03** | Profit per order — `cogsSnapshot` + `profit = netIncome − cogs` per `SettlementLine`; reconciling KPI trio (`matchedNetIncome − totalCogs = totalProfit`) + `matchRatePct`, `unmatchedCount`, `profitPendingCount` | ✅ shipped |
| **11-04** | Reconcile + journal — `checksumOk` gate returns `CHECKSUM_BLOCKED` before any posting; `postSettlementJournal` then posts DR Bank + per-fee-category DR + CR AR | ✅ shipped (PR #157; fee split + sign fix in the financial-reports PR) |
| **15-01** | Shopee adapter — `shopee-settlement-parser.ts` → `ParsedSettlement` | ✅ shipped |
| **15-02** | TikTok adapter — `tiktok-settlement-parser.ts`, same `ParsedSettlement` shape; zeroes the four Shopee fee columns (TikTok itemizes nothing per line), derives period dates by scanning Date cells, normalizes `Total Biaya` with `Math.abs()` | ✅ shipped (PR #168, validated on a real 18-line TikTok income export) |
| **15-03** | Marketplace selection on upload — `SUPPORTED_MARKETPLACES` dropdown (SHOPEE/TIKTOK) → `parseSettlement()` switch; unknown value → 400, wrong-format file → 422 parse errors (never silent corruption) | ✅ shipped |

Caveat on 15's "identical normalized schema" AC: the *shape* is identical, but fee itemization is not — Shopee fills four fee columns, TikTok fills none, so a TikTok Laba Rugi shows all fees under `MARKETPLACE_FEE_OTHER`. Downstream matching/profit/reconciliation are genuinely marketplace-blind. `TOKOPEDIA` resolves in `match-key.ts` but has **no parser** and is absent from the dropdown — out of scope for EPIC-15 (Shopee + TikTok only).

EPIC-13 (Journal Engine) decomposition:

| Story | Scope | Status |
|----|-------|--------|
| **Engine** | Balance-enforced `postJournal` (cents-rounded, idempotent by `(sourceType, sourceId)`) + posting-role→account mapping (`JournalAccountMapping`, `resolveAccount`, `POSTING_ROLES`) + manual journal entry (13-07) + ledger list/detail view (13-08) | ✅ shipped (PR #155 core + PR #156 mapping, merged 2026-07-23) |
| **13-04** | Auto-journal: settlement — DR Bank + DR Fee, CR AR (Piutang); checksum-gated | ✅ shipped (PR #157 merged 2026-07-24) |
| **13-06** | Auto-journal: opname adjustment — surplus DR Persediaan / CR Selisih (shrinkage reversed); net from `StockMovement` (FG+accessories+fabric); best-effort post-approve + `JOURNAL_PENDING` flag + retriable "Post journal" button (gated on non-zero delta). Introduced the generic `generateAutoJournal(client, sourceType, sourceId, lines, meta)` seam all future auto-journals reuse. | ✅ shipped (PR #159 merged 2026-07-25) |
| **13-01** | Auto-journal: GRN — DR Persediaan, CR Hutang; best-effort on create + reverse (DR Hutang / CR Persediaan) on owner-decline of an over-receive GRN; value = `totalAmount`, dated `grnDate` | ✅ shipped (PR #160 merged 2026-07-26) |
| **13-02** | Auto-journal: FG receipt — DR Persediaan FG (`INVENTORY_FG`) / CR Persediaan (`INVENTORY`, raw), value = `FGReceipt.totalCostValue`, dated `receivedAt`; create-only, best-effort on `receiveFG`; adds the `INVENTORY_FG` role (+ mapping i18n). Credit = raw inventory (transfer), NOT WIP/HPP — material issue isn't journaled | ✅ shipped (PR #161 merged 2026-07-26) |
| **13-03** | Auto-journal: sales (marketplace) — revenue (DR AR / CR SALES_REVENUE @ `grandTotal`) + COGS (DR COGS / CR INVENTORY @ Σ `SalesOrderItem.cogs`); posted at ship by a **5-min web cron sweep** (`lib/finance/sales/sweep.ts` + `jobs.ts`) over un-journaled shipped orders (raw `NOT EXISTS`; eligible = `status IN (SHIPPED,COMPLETED) OR fulfillmentStatus=SHIPPED`), idempotent by source, read-only badges on the order detail. Cross-service (ship fires from api webhook + web button via `consumeOrder`; sweep is the web-side bridge) | ✅ shipped (PR #163 merged 2026-07-26) |
| **13-05** | Auto-journal: retur — reverse of 13-03 on a finalized return (DR SALES_REVENUE / CR AR @ Σ accepted subtotal + DR INVENTORY / CR COGS @ Σ restock cost); best-effort at `submitReturnDecision`, aggregating ACCEPTED items; web-only (no cron), retriable. Also fixed a pre-existing Decimal server→client leak in `getSalesReturnById` | ✅ shipped (PR #164 merged 2026-07-26) |

**EPIC-13 (Journal Engine) COMPLETE** — all six auto-journals shipped (13-01 GRN, 13-02 FG receipt, 13-03 sales/COGS, 13-04 settlement, 13-05 retur, 13-06 opname adjustment) on the shared `generateAutoJournal` seam + role→account mapping. Any future auto-journal reuses the seam — add: role lines + source hook + spec + test. **Adding a NEW `POSTING_ROLES` entry also requires its `financeAccountMapping.role.<ROLE>` + `.desc.<ROLE>` i18n in BOTH locales** (else the account-mapping row renders a raw key — the 13-02 final-review catch) **and an entry in `POSTING_ROLE_ACCOUNT_TYPES`** (`apps/web/lib/finance/journals/role-account-types.ts`) — the `Record<PostingRole, …>` type would normally catch a missing entry, but `apps/web` type-check never runs here and vitest strips types at runtime, so the completeness test plus this doc line are the only real nets. Requires the roles mapped in the CoA; unmapped → flagged (`JOURNAL_PENDING`) + retriable, never blocks the source op.

Already done before sub-1: 01-01 (token + cron + alert), 01-04 (API call audit log + 429 + admin dashboard), catalog ingest (`POST /jubelio/catalog/sync`), category sync (2026-06-05).

**Maintenance rule:** When a sub-project, EPIC, or independently-shipped story merges to master, update the relevant table row here in the same session (status → ✅, append PR # + merge date). Stale decomposition tables caused at least one false-start ("sub-2 next" when sub-2 had shipped months earlier). Treat the table as part of the merge checklist, not an afterthought. Same for the **Follow-ups / tech debt** checklist below — on merge, tick any shipped items (append the PR #) and move any new follow-ups there (never leave them only in PR bodies). **Also tick the matching story on the GitHub board** (the epic issue, `EPIC-NN` → #`NN+4`) — that's the canonical cross-EPIC status tracker now that `reference/todo/` is retired.

EPIC-14 (Financial Reports) decomposition:

| Story | Scope | Status |
|----|-------|--------|
| **14-01** | Trial Balance — period-range debit/credit/saldo over postable leaves; balance check framed as a corruption tripwire (`postJournal` already enforces per-journal balance) | ✅ shipped (PR #215 merged 2026-08-05). Prod permission seeded 2026-08-08. |
| **14-02** | Laba Rugi — Pendapatan − HPP = Laba Kotor − Beban = Laba Bersih; sections by `AccountType` (never account code); GL-coverage banner naming the un-journaled sources | ✅ shipped (PR #215 merged 2026-08-05). Prod permission seeded 2026-08-08. |
| **14-03** | Neraca — as-of, cumulative; balances via an injected "Laba (Rugi) Belum Ditutup" line standing in for the missing fiscal close; opening-balance warning when no journal predates the ledger | ✅ shipped (PR #215 merged 2026-08-05). Prod permission seeded 2026-08-08. |
| **14-04** | Export — Excel via `xlsx` server actions returning `{base64, filename}`; PDF via `/print/finance/*` + `print.css` browser print | ✅ shipped (PR #215 merged 2026-08-05). Prod permission seeded 2026-08-08. |
| **Fee split** | Settlement journal posts one line per fee category (`MARKETPLACE_FEE_ADMIN`/`_SERVICE`/`_COMMISSION`/`_PROCESSING`) plus a `_OTHER` residual computed from `totalPengeluaran` so the journal still balances; unmapped category roles fall back to the legacy `MARKETPLACE_FEE` account. TikTok zeroes all four columns → all its fees land in `_OTHER`. Zero schema migration (`role` is a String). | ✅ shipped (PR #215 merged 2026-08-05) |

EPIC-16 (Cash Flow) decomposition:

| Story | Scope | Status |
|----|-------|--------|
| **16-01** | Cash flow generation — indirect method from journal data; net change derived from every non-cash account's period delta, so `ΔKas = LabaBersih + ΔLiabilitas + ΔEkuitas − ΔAsetNonKas` holds identically and the reconciliation is a corruption tripwire, not a real check. Section comes from a nullable `ChartAccount.cashFlowSection` override falling back to a read-time deriver (`BANK`/`CASH` ⇒ KAS, `AR`/`AP`/`INVENTORY*` ⇒ OPERASIONAL, `EKUITAS` ⇒ PENDANAAN, everything else unclassified). Unclassified accounts stay inside the total and render in their own bucket, so classification affects presentation only — **except for `KAS`, which defines what "cash" means**, and is therefore restricted to `ASET` leaves at three chokepoints (action guard, disabled picker option, leaf-only section map). | ✅ shipped (PR #235 merged 2026-08-11) |
| **16-02** | Period comparison — immediately-preceding equal-length window, per-line delta over the union of both periods' accounts. Hidden when the range is since-inception. | ✅ shipped (PR #235 merged 2026-08-11) |
| **16-03** | Export — Excel via `exportCashFlowExcel`, PDF via `/print/finance/cash-flow`. Print is single-period by design; three amount columns do not fit the portrait sheet. All three surfaces refuse to render when no account is classified `KAS`, rather than emitting zeros. | ✅ shipped (PR #235 merged 2026-08-11) |
| **Klasifikasi** | Finance → Klasifikasi Arus Kas, reusing `journals:view`/`journals:manage` — **no new permission rows, nothing to seed on prod**. The section map is leaf-only but deliberately RETAINS inactive leaves: `getAccountBalances` keeps an inactive account that still has movement, so dropping it from the map would lose its balance from `kasAwal` while its rows still reached the engine — understating closing cash with no visible signal. | ✅ shipped (PR #235 merged 2026-08-11) |

**Dormant until the GL cutover is armed** (see the GL blocker in Finance follow-ups): with no journals posting, the statement renders honest and empty, and the reconciliation criterion cannot be verified against real numbers.

## Piutang, Retur & Konsinyasi

Modul untuk toko-toko **Elorae**. Aturan dan alurnya meniru program lain milik
pemilik ("SSG Pelunasan", untuk usaha distribusi cat — BUKAN Elorae); brief
aslinya ada di `~/Desktop/PROGRAM PELUNASAN/BRIEF-BANGUN-ULANG.md` dan boleh
dibaca untuk alasan di balik sebuah aturan, karena hampir setiap penolakan di
sini menjawab satu kejadian nyata di lapangan. **Elorae tidak memuat data SSG
apa pun dan tidak akan** (keputusan pemilik 2026-10-06: "fokus untuk Elorae").
Syarat brief "tidak ada data yang keluar dari komputer ini" milik program SSG,
tidak berlaku untuk Elorae.

**Dua aturan uang yang tidak boleh dilonggarkan.** Keduanya murni di
`apps/web/lib/receivables/rules.ts`:
1. **Klaim bukan lunas.** Piutang hanya berkurang setelah admin keuangan
   mencocokkan BKM dengan mutasi rekening. Status `DIKIRIM` mengakui nol.
2. **Retur diakui sebesar fisik, bukan sebesar klaim.** Sebelum gudang menutup
   hitungan, seluruh potongan ditahan; sesudahnya tinggal selisihnya sampai ada
   yang ditetapkan menanggung.

**Sisa piutang TIDAK PERNAH diketik dan tidak pernah dikurangi bertahap.**
Setiap penulis memanggil `hitungUlangPiutang`, yang menghitung dari nol atas
seluruh klaim yang menyentuh piutang itu. Kolom `outstanding` adalah cache untuk
penyaringan, bukan kebenaran — `daftarPiutang` pun menghitung ulang.

**Satu penerjemah, bukan tiga.** `lib/receivables/deductions.ts` adalah
satu-satunya tempat bentuk Prisma bertemu aturan retur. Pernah ada tiga salinan
(layar piutang, layar BKM, penulis) dan yang ketiga diam-diam salah begitu tabel
retur ada. Jangan bikin salinan keempat.

**Penunjuk retur menggantung ditahan penuh.** `relationMode = "prisma"` tidak
memasang foreign key, jadi potongan bisa menunjuk nota retur yang tak ada.
`PILIH_POTONGAN` **wajib** ikut memilih `storeReturnId` — tanpa itu kasus
menggantung tidak bisa dibedakan dari "bukan retur", dan uangnya lepas diam-diam.

**Retur adalah DUA dokumen.** `StoreReturn` (NRT) bernomor **milik toko**
(`NRT-4471`, unik per toko — bukan nomor yang kita hasilkan), `WarehouseReturn`
(NRI) bernomor milik kita, dan `storeReturnId`-nya **nullable**: barang datang
lewat ekspedisi mendahului notanya. NRI `DRAFT` tidak melepas apa pun.

**NRI menambah stok** (`STORE_RETURN_RECEIVE`, `POSITIVE`) — arah ini pernah
salah dan dikoreksi pemilik. Hanya baris ber-`itemId` yang bergerak, dan harga
rata-rata TIDAK digeser: retur bukan pembelian.

**Toko konsinyasi tidak pernah muncul di piutang.** Disaring di `daftarPiutang`,
bukan di layar. Baris uji `INV/KONSI/TES` di basis data dev adalah kenari.

**Impor Excel:** `XLSX.read` WAJIB memakai `raw: true`. Tanpanya SheetJS membaca
`"900.000"` sebagai sembilan ratus pada berkas CSV — hanya angka bertitik SATU
yang kena, jadi kerusakannya tepat mengenai nilai di bawah sejuta. Dikunci tes
di `impor/sheetjs.test.ts`. Kolom `sisa` berisi `0` berarti **lunas**, berbeda
dari sel kosong dan dari kolom yang tidak ada.

**Pemisahan tugas ada di `lib/receivables/izin.ts`**, bukan tersebar. Melapor =
`pwa:access` (sales yang menyaksikan), memverifikasi = `journals:manage`,
menghitung = `inventory_opname:count`, menetapkan beban = `receivables:manage`.
Menyatukannya meruntuhkan kedua aturan uang.

**Layar gudang ada di `/gudang-retur`, di luar `/pwa`.** Pohon `/pwa` digerbangi
`pwa:access` milik sales; memberikannya ke gudang membuka semua layar sales.

**Konsinyasi:** satu siklus = satu toko × satu bulan, dan **tiga nomor
berdampingan** (konsi kita, nota admin penjualan, faktur pajak admin pajak).
Tahap `bayar_spg` **dilewati** kalau toko tidak punya SPG. Mengunci butuh KEDUA
laporan; membuka kunci **ditolak** begitu ada gaji dibayar. Tarif komisi
disalin dari master barang saat mengunci. Stok titipan dihitung, tidak disimpan,
dan sisa negatif dipertahankan.

**Foto disimpan di database** (`Foto` + `/api/foto/[id]`), bukan R2 — mengikuti
rancangan brief dan syaratnya bahwa foto tidak keluar dari mesin. Kolom
`photoUrl`/`transferPhotoUrl` tetap bertipe URL, jadi pemanggilnya tidak
bercabang.

**Migrasi MariaDB modul ini** (dilengkapi 2026-10-05): tabel induk
`20260827090000_piutang_pelunasan_modul`, lalu
`20261005140000_receivables_offline_giro_replacement` (14 kolom + 5 indeks unik
yang dulu hanya ada di Postgres `13`/`17`/`22`) dan
`20261005150000_long_text_columns`. Seed izin MariaDB ada di
`packages/db/prisma/seed-{receivables,consignment,creative,production-palette}-permissions.sql`
— jalankan receivables SEBELUM consignment (consignment menurunkan tautannya dari
pemegang `receivables:*`). `packages/db/prisma/migrasi-mariadb.test.ts`
mencocokkan setiap kolom/indeks/DEFAULT skema dengan rantai migrasi MariaDB,
menolak sintaks Postgres di SQL MariaDB, dan menuntut setiap seed Postgres punya
kembaran MariaDB — jalankan dari `packages/db` (`npx vitest run
prisma/migrasi-mariadb.test.ts`) sebelum PR, karena tidak ada CI.

**Setiap keputusan yang bisa dibalik meninggalkan jejak.** `lib/receivables/jejak.ts`
— nama tindakan terkumpul di `JEJAK`, ditulis DI DALAM transaksinya, membawa
pasangan sebelum/sesudah. Barisnya hanya menyimpan keadaan terakhir; keputusan
yang tergantikan hanya hidup di jejaknya. Jangan menulis nama tindakan sebagai
string bebas, dan jangan memakai nama yang artinya lain — keduanya membuat jejak
tidak bisa dicari, yang sama saja dengan tidak ada.

**Salah toko dibetulkan, tidak pernah dihapus** (`lib/receivables/koreksi.ts`,
rancangan `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-01-koreksi-salah-toko-design.md`).
Sales MENARIK laporannya sendiri dari HP selama belum disentuh — BKM selama
`DIKIRIM`, NRT sebelum gudang menghitung dan selama tidak dipakai potongan di
BKM hidup — dengan alasan; jejaknya mencatat `olehSales` dan kantor menerima
notifikasi (`KLAIM_DIBATALKAN_SALES` → `journals:manage`,
`RETUR_DIBATALKAN_SALES` → `receivables:manage`), jadi menerima tunai lalu
menarik laporan tidak pernah diam-diam. KANTOR memindahkan NRT ke toko lain
(`pindahkanReturKeToko`): NRI pasangannya dan beban TERBUKA ikut, stok tidak
bergerak, ditolak kalau beban sudah diputuskan atau nota dipakai potongan di BKM
hidup; hitungan gudang yatim dipindah/ditetapkan tokonya lewat
`pindahkanNriKeToko`. BKM tidak pernah dipindah toko — dibatalkan lalu
"Laporkan ulang"/"Buat ulang" (`?salin=<id>`) yang menyalin tanggal, cara bayar,
giro, dan foto. `batalkanRetur` kini MENOLAK nota yang sudah dihitung
(`SUDAH_DIHITUNG`), dan `lepasPasangan` tidak pernah menghidupkan nota `BATAL`.
**Nomor NRT dari nota BATAL dilepas** (`lepaskanNomorBatal`): saat nomor yang sama
dicatat ulang atau dipindah ke toko yang punya nota batal bernomor itu, yang
batal diberi akhiran " (batal)" / " (batal 2)" dan nomor aslinya dicatat di jejak
`STORE_RETURN_NUMBER_RELEASED` — tanpa ini salah ketik nilai tidak bisa
dibetulkan karena nomornya tetap dipegang nota batal. Tes:
`lib/receivables/koreksi.test.ts` (basis data sungguhan, termasuk balapan).
Lanjutannya (hari yang sama): **laporan pengganti tertaut** lewat kolom UNIK
`PaymentClaim.replacesClaimId` / `StoreReturn.replacesReturnId` (migrasi Postgres
`22_pengganti_laporan`, belum ada migrasi MariaDB untuk modul ini) — satu laporan
lama hanya punya satu pengganti, jadi uang/barang yang sama tidak bisa
dilaporkan ulang dua kali; `menggantikanId` dibawa formulir `?salin=` dan antrean
HP, diperiksa `lib/receivables/pengganti.ts`, dan izin lintas-pelapor ditetapkan
SERVER dari `receivables:manage` sesudah isian HP. **"Minta koreksi"** untuk
laporan yang sudah diproses (BKM terverifikasi, NRT yang sudah dihitung):
`mintaKoreksi` menulis jejak `CORRECTION_REQUESTED` + notifikasi
(`KOREKSI_DIMINTA_BKM`/`_RETUR`); permintaan dianggap tertunda selama dibuat
SESUDAH `updatedAt` dokumennya, jadi selesai sendiri begitu kantor bertindak.
**"Kabari toko"**: pesan WhatsApp "tanda terima dibatalkan karena salah catat"
untuk BKM batal (`pesanPembatalan`, tidak pernah menyebut lunas). **Riwayat**
dokumen di rincian BKM dan rincian retur (`riwayatDokumen`). Memindah hitungan
gudang ke/dari bulan yang siklus konsinyasinya sudah DIKUNCI tidak ditolak tapi
butuh konfirmasi (`SIKLUS_TERKUNCI` → `setujuSiklusTerkunci`), dicatat di jejak.

**Kueri punya tesnya sendiri** (`queries.test.ts`, `dashboard-queries.test.ts`,
`konsinyasi/queries.test.ts`), terhadap basis data sungguhan, teardown lewat
`seededId()`. Ada karena dua kesalahan nyata hidup di kueri, bukan di aturan, dan
keduanya lolos type-check serta seluruh tes murni. Fixture piutang sengaja
menyeed kolom `outstanding` dengan angka SALAH: kalau kuerinya membaca kolom itu
alih-alih menghitung ulang, tesnya gagal.

**Potongan dipilih dari master `DeductionType`, bukan diketik.** Jenis
`DARI_RETUR` MEMAKSA potongan ditautkan ke nota retur — sebelumnya penautan
sepenuhnya bergantung ingatan orang yang mengetik, dan yang lupa ditautkan lolos
sebagai pengurang biasa yang tidak pernah ditahan. Jenis dinonaktifkan, tidak
pernah dihapus: yang pernah dipakai menempel pada BKM lama.

**Laporan lapangan bisa dikirim tanpa sinyal.** BKM dan NRT dari PWA masuk
antrean Dexie (`pendingClaims`/`pendingReturns`, `lib/pwa/offline/laporan-*`)
kalau tidak ada sinyal, fotonya disimpan sebagai berkas di HP dan diunggah saat
sinkron. `idempotencyKey` (unik, migrasi Postgres `13_antrean_luring`) dibuat HP
sekali per formulir; penulis memeriksanya PALING DULU — sebelum validasi sisa,
karena faktur bisa sudah lunas sejak kiriman pertama — dan mengembalikan baris
yang sudah ada. Klaim yang masih di antrean belum tercatat sama sekali, jadi
Aturan 1 tidak tersentuh. Formulir kantor tidak memakai jalur ini (`antreLuring`).

**Belum dikerjakan:** tujuh peran brief (admin penjualan & admin pajak belum ada
di Elorae — aksi konsinyasi sementara memakai `receivables:manage`), penyempitan
menu per peran, OCR luring, pembatalan
impor (id yang dibuat sudah disimpan di metadata jejaknya — itu bahan yang
dibutuhkan), penomoran atomik (sekarang `findFirst`+1 dengan `@unique` sebagai
penjaga), dan penyambungan beban sales ke komisi konsinyasinya.

**Diputuskan 2026-10-06:** modul ini hanya untuk data Elorae (server VPS
Hostinger, MariaDB). Data SSG tidak dimasukkan ke Elorae; program SSG di
`~/Desktop/PROGRAM PELUNASAN/ssg-pelunasan` adalah aplikasi terpisah yang hanya
berisi data contoh.

## Complete salesman app (built 2026-09-30 → 2026-10-01, uncommitted on `dev/timotius`)

The owner asked for EVERY feature from the salesman audit and the SFA research, not a menu of options. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-salesman-lengkap-design.md` (stages 1–5). **Every policy is a default the owner changes in Settings → Salesman** (`app/backoffice/settings/salesman/page.tsx`), stored as `SystemSetting` JSON with an in-code fallback: `salesman.kredit`, `salesman.hasilKunjungan`, `salesman.checkin` (+ `checkin.radiusMeters`), `salesman.kelasToko`, `salesman.komisi`, `salesman.peringkat`, `salesman.biaya`, `salesman.absensi`, `salesman.pesananJarakJauh`, `salesman.portalToko`, `putus.minOrderQty` / `putus.minOrderQtyReorder`.

- **No new permission codes — nothing to seed.** Everything reuses `pwa:access`, `field_sales_orders:view|approve|deliver`, `stores:view|manage`, `receivables:view|manage`, `journals:manage`, `inventory:manage`. Pages that two groups open (travel expenses: sales OR finance; goods exchange: sales OR warehouse) are deliberately NOT in `ROUTE_PERMISSIONS` and guard themselves; the sidebar's `NavChild.permission` now accepts a list meaning ANY-of (`bolehAnak` in `BackofficeShell.tsx`). New backoffice group **Tim Lapangan**.
- **Migrations:** Postgres `16_kredit_toko` … `20_besar` (applied locally); MariaDB `20260930100000_store_credit` … `20260930140000_big_features` (never run). `17_lapangan`'s receivables columns have NO MariaDB counterpart (that module is Postgres-only).
- **Notifications to one person:** `kabariPengguna` (`lib/notifications/kabar-pengguna.ts`) — `NotificationQueue` + FCM, called AFTER the transaction, never throws, skipped under `VITEST`. A new `JENIS_KABAR` member needs its link in `tautanKabar` (`Record<JenisKabar, …>`; `tautan-kabar.test.ts` sweeps every member). Push uses the existing `/pwa/sw.js` registration (`lib/pwa/push.ts`) — there is no `firebase-messaging-sw.js`; push only works in a production build, and iPhone only for an installed PWA. Office-side heads-ups use `AdminNotification` + `fanOutAdminNotification`; a new category needs its recipient permission in `CATEGORY_PERMISSION` AND a case in `lib/notifications/navigation.ts`, or it reaches nobody / links nowhere. Writers that create one inside a `runSerializable` callback must reset the captured row at the top of the callback (a retried attempt's row must never be fanned out).
- **KONSI prices and totals never reach the salesman app** — not on screen, not in payloads (`nilaiTampil` in `lib/field-sales/pwa-queries.ts` returns null for KONSI always). Same rule on the store portal, tested by asserting the serialized payload has no price keys.
- **Credit** (`lib/salesman/kredit.ts`, pure): PERINGATAN (default) / TAHAN (`FieldSalesOrder.kreditDitahan`, approver must tick "checked the bills") / BLOKIR (`KreditDiblokirError` at create). PUTUS only. Per-store `Store.creditLimit`/`maxOpenInvoices` override the defaults.
- **Visit outcome:** check-out without a transaction REQUIRES a reason by default (`checkOut` → `HASIL_WAJIB`); SPG exempt. Check-in radius PERINGATAN (default) / TOLAK; anomaly flags in `StoreVisit.checkinFlags` — a web app cannot detect GPS spoofing apps, only anomalies.
- **Remote orders:** `FieldSalesOrder.remoteChannel` = `WA` | `TELEPON` | `LAINNYA` (salesman, no visit, own store by default) or `PORTAL` (store self-order). `PORTAL` is NOT in `SALURAN_JARAK_JAUH`: the salesman submit action's zod enum rejects it (tested), so the WA/phone owner rule cannot be bypassed; the writer's PORTAL branch only checks `salesmanId === Store.salesmanId`.
- **Store self-order portal** (`app/toko/[token]/`, public, `/toko/` in the `proxy.ts` allow-list): token = 32 random bytes base64url, only its sha256 is stored (`StorePortalLink`); every request re-resolves the link (revoked/expired/inactive store/no holder/portal off). Orders land as `PENDING_APPROVAL` under the store's HOLDER, rate-limited per WIB day, idempotency key scoped to the link (`portal:<linkId>:<key>`); stock shown as buckets only; `referrer: no-referrer` because the token is in the URL.
- **Collection tasks** (`CollectionTask`, `lib/receivables/tugas-tagih*`): `daftarPiutang({ penagihId })` ALSO returns invoices in OPEN tasks assigned to that person — without it a non-holder assignee could see the task but never report the payment. Assignment adds a collector; the holder still collects too. Audit names live in `JEJAK` (`TUGAS_TAGIH_*`).
- **WhatsApp messages to stores** (`lib/receivables/pesan-toko.ts`): a pending report only ever produces a RECEIPT, which never contains "lunas" (test on the real text); only a verified one produces the confirmation.
- **Goods exchange** (`StoreExchange`, `lib/salesman/tukar-*`): PUTUS stores only, value must match at server PUTUS prices; approve = `field_sales_orders:approve`, complete = `inventory:manage`; stock moves ONLY at completion (`STORE_EXCHANGE_OUT`/`_IN`, idempotency `tukar:<lineId>`); receivables never touched.
- **Proof of delivery** (`FieldSalesDelivery.receivedByName/receivedAt/podPhotoUrl`, `lib/field-sales/delivery/bukti-terima*`): photo of the signed nota OR an on-screen signature, stored via `/api/foto` (DB, not R2); first record is compare-and-swap; correction is office-only with a reason; offline queue on the phone (Dexie v5 `pendingBuktiTerima`).
- **Pre-order / inden** (`PreorderWindow` + `PreorderWindowItem`, `lib/field-sales/inden*`, `/backoffice/inden`): window state is DERIVED (BELUM_BUKA/BUKA/TUTUP/DIRILIS/NONAKTIF), never stored. A pre-order is a normal putus `FieldSalesOrder` with `preorderWindowId`: PUTUS only, every item must be in the window, the window is re-checked inside the create transaction, and it reserves NO stock at create or approve. "Rilis" (`rilisJendelaInden`, `field_sales_orders:approve`) is a compare-and-swap on `releasedAt` (idempotent, cannot be undone) that reserves every approved order; orders approved after release reserve at approve. Delivery before release is refused (`INDEN_BELUM_RILIS`). Release needs a stock row per (item, variant) — a pooled `null` row on a variant item blocks it and names the products. An offline-queued pre-order whose window closed before it synced is TERMINAL (`INDEN_DITOLAK` in `classify.ts`), not retried forever.
- **Stage 6 (built 2026-10-01 after the owner's gap list)** — migration Postgres `21_lengkap_lanjut` / MariaDB `20261001100000_salesman_extras` (never run), still no new permission codes:
  - **Cancel / edit own pending order** (`lib/field-sales/ganti-pesanan.ts`, `cancelFieldSalesOrderBySalesman`, `createFieldSalesOrder({ replacesOrderId })`): a cancelled order stays `status = REJECTED` (every "live order" query uses `<> REJECTED`) and is told apart by `cancelledById`/`cancelledAt` — counts of "rejected" must add `cancelledById: null`. Editing = catalog `?ganti=<id>` → a NEW order whose writer cancels the old one and releases its stock INSIDE the same transaction, so every pricing/promo/min/credit/inden rule runs once, in one place. After a successful edit the catalog redirects to the new order (the catalog `key` changes when the old one is cancelled, so an in-place success screen would be lost).
  - **WhatsApp copy of an order** (`lib/field-sales/pesan-pesanan-wa.ts`): always Indonesian; KONSI carries no price, no total, no "Rp" (tested on the real text).
  - **Office visit schedule** (`VisitSchedule`, `lib/salesman/jadwal*`, `/backoffice/jadwal-kunjungan`, `/pwa/jadwal`): one row per salesperson × store × WIB day; past days read-only; compliance = any check-in that WIB day. Approved leave turns an unvisited row into status `IZIN`, which is excluded from the "N of M visited" denominator.
  - **Announcements** (`Announcement`/`AnnouncementRead`, `lib/pengumuman/*`, `/backoffice/pengumuman`, `/pwa/pengumuman`): state derived from dates; optional "Saya mengerti" acknowledgement with a read report; scheduled ones are pushed by a 5-minute cron that records `ANNOUNCEMENT_SENT` in `AuditLog` (no column for it) and skips anything older than 3 days.
  - **Leave (izin/sakit/cuti)** (`LeaveRequest`, `lib/salesman/izin*`, `/backoffice/izin`): working days Mon–Sat (`HARI_TIDAK_KERJA`); cuti quota per WIB year in `salesman.absensi.kuotaCutiTahunan` (default 12, counts pending); overlap refused; nobody decides their own; office cancel of approved leave sends `IZIN_DIBATALKAN`.
  - **Targets per category / product** (`SalesTarget`, `lib/salesman/target-rinci*`, `/backoffice/target-sales`, `/pwa/target`): achievement uses exactly `penjualanSalesman`'s sources (a DB test proves the per-category sum equals the home "Bulan ini" figure). On the PHONE, product targets in rupiah exclude konsi (`capaianUntukHp`) — konsi value ÷ the pcs he sent would be the konsi unit price.
  - **Store notes** (`StoreNote`, `lib/stores/catatan*`): pinned notes show in the home visit plan; edit/delete own, `stores:manage` any; soft delete.
- **Phone layout rules learned in the 2026-10-01 UX pass** (the same three bugs were in a dozen screens): `components/ui/card.tsx` `Card` is `flex flex-col gap-6 py-6` — (1) when its `CardContent` sets its own vertical padding (`p-4`, `py-4`…), give the `Card` `py-0`, or the top gap doubles; (2) spacing BETWEEN children of a `Card` is `gap-N`, never `space-y-N` (it fights `gap-6`); (3) spacing inside a `CardContent` is `space-y-N`, never `gap-N` (`CardContent` is not flex, so `gap` silently does nothing and fields touch). Buttons in a 2-up row must not `truncate` their label — use wrapping tiles (see `app/pwa/stores/[id]/AksiKunjungan.tsx`). Money on the phone is never truncated: give it its own row. A screen with a `sticky bottom-0` action bar needs `min-h-dvh` on its root and `mt-auto` on the bar, or the bar floats mid-screen when the content is short (the pattern of `SpgSaleShell`). Headings are sentence case ("Pelunasan saya"), the user is "Anda", never "Kamu". **Never use viewport breakpoints (`sm:`/`md:`/`lg:`) inside the phone app.** On a laptop or tablet the app is a phone-width column (`max-w-xl`, `app/pwa/layout.tsx`) inside a wide window, so `sm:` fires on the WINDOW's width and lays out three columns inside a 576 px column — the BKM form's fields spilled out of its card in Safari on a laptop (2026-10-01), invisible at 375 px where every earlier check ran. Phone-only screens simply have no breakpoints; a component shared with the backoffice (e.g. `KlaimBaruClient`) puts `@container` on its root and uses container variants (`@2xl:`), so it follows its OWN width in both places. Overlays that cover the window (dialog, sheet) are the exception and keep `sm:`. Check new screens at laptop width too, not only 375. **`.beranda-kartu` / `.beranda-kartu-daftar` are plain CSS outside the Tailwind layers** (`app/globals.css`), so they beat any padding utility on the SAME element (`beranda-kartu p-2` gets 1rem, `beranda-kartu-daftar p-2` gets 0) — put the padding on an inner element. **A submit button that can be disabled says why**, with `app/pwa/KeteranganKirim.tsx` under it naming the first missing field in form order — a pale button with no reason left the owner guessing.
- **Daily summary** cron 18:30 WIB (`kirimRingkasanHarian`, `lib/cron/jobs.ts`): one kabar per active salesman + one `RINGKASAN_SALES_HARIAN` note to approvers; quiet days write nothing; refuses to write twice per date.
- Also built: own-orders screen, store summary card, declining-store tasks, payment promises + collection notes from the phone (office confirms into a kontrabon), giro tracking + 07:00 reminder, cash receipts, new-store proposals (`StoreProposal`), what-to-offer suggestions, WhatsApp catalogue, store classes A/B/C with per-class visit gaps, tiered minimums, BONUS promos (bonus lines excluded from reorder/suggestions), consignment stock on the phone + per-size targets, commission estimate + leaderboard, travel expense claims, attendance (selfie + GPS, off by default), daily route (nearest-next + 2-opt, Google Maps links), display checklist + score, competitor prices, display guides.

## SPG lengkap (built 2026-10-01, uncommitted on `dev/timotius`)

Owner: "what should an SPG do — maybe send goods to another store, handle returns, and more". Researched (Indonesian SPG duties + SPG/retail-execution apps), audited, then built by parallel agents. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-01-spg-lengkap-design.md`. Migration Postgres `23_spg_mutasi_retur` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261001120000_spg_transfer_returns` (never run). Still no new permission codes.

- **An SPG is detected by `User.assignedStoreId` (`ambilTokoTetap`, `lib/pwa/peran.ts`), never by role name.** Salesman-only server actions now refuse it, not just their pages: `submitFieldSalesOrder`, smart-request build/submit, `ajukanTukarAction` (`UNAUTHORIZED`/`TANPA_AKSES`), and `absenMasukAction` (SPG attendance comes from check-in). `catatReturTokoAction` forces the store on the SERVER: SPG → own store; own-store lock on → stores the user holds; `receivables:manage` exempt; else `TOKO_TIDAK_DIIZINKAN` (before 2026-10-01 any `pwa:access` user could file an NRT for any store).
- **Store stock book kinds:** `TERIMA`, `JUAL`, `OPNAME`, `MUTASI_KELUAR`, `MUTASI_MASUK`, `RETUR_KELUAR`, `RETUR_PEMBELI` (`JENIS_GERAKAN_STOK`, `lib/spg/kantor-queries.ts`). Never reuse `JUAL`/`TERIMA` for something that is not a sale/receipt — `stok-di-toko.ts` reads `JUAL` as sales speed (it now nets `RETUR_PEMBELI`). A new kind needs its badge colour in `StokTokoCard.tsx` and `stores.stockCard.kind.*` in both locales.
- **Store transfers (`StoreTransfer`, `lib/spg/mutasi-*`, `/pwa/spg/mutasi`, `/backoffice/spg-transfers`):** DIAJUKAN → DISETUJUI (`field_sales_orders:approve`) → DIKIRIM (`MUTASI_KELUAR`) → DITERIMA (destination SPG via the existing receive screen, source `MUTASI`, writes `MUTASI_MASUK`; or the office with `stores:manage` when the destination has no SPG or is past `hariPengingat`). Rules in `SystemSetting["spg.mutasi"]` (Settings → Salesman): default only KONSI→KONSI (needs approval); every direction involving a PUTUS store is refused because those goods belong to the store, and is never `LANGSUNG`. Shortfall → `selisihStatus TERBUKA`, office decides `HILANG` (audit only) or `KEMBALI` (`MUTASI_MASUK` at source). Main `InventoryValue` never moves. An office receipt at a store without a stock book writes only the `StoreReceipt`; the titipan calculation reads it. Numbers `MTS/<WIB year>/NNNNN`. Audit names in `JEJAK_MUTASI`; reminder sweeps are scoped (`transferIds`/`storeIds`) in tests. Daily 08:00 WIB cron: stale transfers + `KIRIMAN_MENUNGGU` kabar.
- **SPG returns:** the NRT carries lines (item, variant, condition LAYAK/RUSAK/EXPIRED), `reportedBySpg`, no prices on the phone; KONSI note number may be blank → `TRK/YY/MM/NNNN`, value computed server-side (consignment price, unpriced items count 0); PUTUS keeps the typed note total. SPG notes write `RETUR_KELUAR` −qty, skipped when the store has no book yet or the note's WIB **day** is before the last opname's WIB day (compared per day: a date-only note vs a timed opname on the same day must still write — this was a real bug caught in the browser 2026-10-01). Withdraw/cancel writes a reversal (`balikkanBukuRetur`, nets existing rows only), never a delete; moving a book-linked NRT is refused (`ADA_BUKU_TOKO`). Warehouse counts per line; RUSAK/EXPIRED lines never enter sellable stock; `nilaiDariRincian`: when every line price is 0 the typed amount wins.
- **Shopper returns at the counter (`SpgSaleReturn`, `lib/spg/retur-pembeli.ts`, `/pwa/spg/<saleId>/kembali`):** refund is done by the store cashier, we record goods only. `qty ≤ line.qty − returnedQty` under `runSerializable`; LAYAK lines write `RETUR_PEMBELI` +qty, RUSAK lines write no book row; `SalesHistory` returned/net quantities adjusted; commission, consignment SPG report, targets, ranking and the SPG home "Bulan ini" all use NET quantities.
- **SPG people features:** check-in/out at the fixed store = clock-in/out when attendance is on (`lib/salesman/absensi-writer.ts`, one row per WIB day, recorded "tanpa swafoto" rather than blocking sales). SPG working days are `SystemSetting["spg.hariKerja"]` (default Mon–Sun) — deliberately NOT inside `salesman.absensi`, because `AbsensiCard` rewrites that whole JSON. Targets (`JenisDokumen` `"spg"`, `capaianRinciSpg`) and an SPG-only ranking; colleagues' rupiah never reaches the phone.
- **SPG access & UX:** "Toko saya" opens the SPG-aware store page (checklist, guides, competitor prices, photos, notes; salesman order/exchange tiles hidden). Bottom menu hidden on SPG form screens (`lib/pwa/menu-spg.ts`). After check-in the SPG returns to the screen that asked for it (`/pwa?kembali=`, `lib/pwa/kembali-setelah-checkin.ts`, internal `/pwa/` paths only).

## Salesman sales history & store analysis (built 2026-10-01)

`/pwa/penjualan` (12 WIB months, putus/konsi/van split, target, vs last month and same month last year on equal days, top stores/products) uses `penjualanSalesman` directly — a DB test pins the month total to the home card. `/pwa/stores/[id]/analisa` (3/6/12 months vs the previous equal period): basis BELI for putus (delivery lines), LAKU for konsi (store book net of shopper returns, else `ConsignmentSold`), KIRIM as a disclosed fallback; konsi carries no rupiah at all. `Item.variants` may be a JSON string with mixed-case keys — read it with `bacaVarian`. Old local test deliveries (`DLV/2026/11,12,21`) have NO lines, so they show money with 0 pcs and an empty analysis — a data artifact, not a code path real deliveries hit.

## SPG ↔ salesman: analysis, requests, recommendations (built 2026-10-01)

Owner: SPG scans become analysis the salesman sees; the SPG asks the salesman for goods (selling at other stores / often sent / new); the salesman reads absorption per article and recommends goods to the store; plus "Rencana kirim" (see Smart request above). Design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-01-analisa-permintaan-rekomendasi-design.md`. Migration Postgres `24_saran_rekomendasi` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261001150000_spg_requests_recommendations` (never run). No new permission codes.

- **Cart prefill from a document:** `/pwa/stores/<id>/catalog?isi=permintaan:<id>|rekomendasi:<id>` (`lib/field-sales/isi-keranjang*.ts`). A request fills the cart only for the store's HOLDER while BARU/DIPROSES and not yet ordered (`fieldSalesOrderId: null` — reopening the old address must not make a second order); a recommendation only for its sender while DITERIMA. After the order is recorded `tautkanSumberPesanan` links it under `runSerializable` with an `AuditLog` row (`STOCK_REQUEST_STATUS` / `STORE_RECOMMENDATION_ORDERED`): request → DIPROSES + kabar to the SPG, recommendation → SELESAI. The catalog toast names the source.
- **SPG analysis** `/pwa/spg/analisa` (`lib/spg/analisa*.ts`): pcs only, no rupiah even at PUTUS stores. "Sold" = `SpgSaleLine.qty − returnedQty` by receipt date (NOT the book's JUAL rows — the book only exists since 2026-09-29 and damaged shopper returns write no book row); shelf = book balance. Speed and slow-movers use a fixed 30-day window; "hampir habis" ≤ 7 days of cover; suggested ask = 14 days of cover − shelf.
- **SPG requests** (`StockRequestLine.alasan`, `lib/spg/saran-minta*.ts`): suggestions in three tabs (often sent / selling at similar stores — pcs and store count only, never names or rupiah / new goods), only goods with warehouse stock. `mintaKiriman` checks the idempotency key FIRST and sends `PERMINTAAN_SPG_MASUK` to the holder once. Salesman inbox `/pwa/permintaan` (`?status=`, `?toko=`): make order, mark done, reject with reason (holder or `stores:manage`). `?tambah=` uses `~` separators that are escaped by hand — `encodeURIComponent` does not encode `~`.
- **Absorption** (`lib/salesman/penyerapan*.ts`, card on `/pwa/stores/[id]/analisa`): sell-through = sold ÷ (opening stock + received + positive opname difference), deliberately NOT sold ÷ sent (that exceeds 100% for goods already on the shelf); locked by tests. Fast ≥ 50%, slow < 20% after 30 days; PUTUS stores without a book say "tidak melaporkan penjualan". Plus a "Dari SPG" card (lost sales + open requests).
- **Recommendations** (`StoreRecommendation`, `lib/salesman/rekomendasi*.ts`, form `/pwa/stores/[id]/analisa/rekomendasi`, lists `/pwa/rekomendasi` and `/pwa/spg/rekomendasi`): created by the holder (or anyone while the store has no holder and the own-store lock is off); kabar to every SPG of the store; SPG accepts/rejects (reason), or the salesman marks "toko setuju" for stores without an SPG; WhatsApp text carries NO price at all. Every transition `runSerializable` + audit. General suggestions (`saran-tawar`) are filtered out for goods already in the store's absorption list (`saringSaranUmum`) — otherwise goods that arrived by opening opname or a transfer were offered as "barang baru, belum pernah dikirim" on the same screen that marks them slow.
- **Client import boundary:** client screens may only `import type` from `*-queries.ts`, `rencana*.ts`, `permintaan-salesman.ts` (they pull Prisma). Client-safe values live in `saran-minta.ts` (incl. `STATUS_PERMINTAAN`), `rekomendasi-aturan.ts`, `kriteria.ts`. A value import of `STATUS_PERMINTAAN` from the query module was caught before it reached the browser — it would have broken every page with "Can't resolve 'dns'".

## Dashboard rework & sign-in (2026-10-01)

- Review verdict "each card tidy, page not understandable"; fixed. **One definition of a late PO: `overduePOWhere` (`lib/purchase-orders/overdue.ts`)** — ETA before the start of today WIB, status SUBMITTED or PARTIAL. **SUBMITTED means approved and sent to the supplier** (only `purchase_orders:approve` can submit, `changePOStatus`); what waits for approval is a **DRAFT** — the action row `draftPOs`, the KPI and the nav badge count DRAFT, and `statuses.poSubmitted` reads "Dikirim ke pemasok".
- **A client component must never import `lib/dashboard/action-items.ts`** (it pulls Prisma into the browser bundle and breaks every page with "Can't resolve 'dns'"); use `action-items-shared.ts`.
- "Bahan kurang" = not-yet-issued material (planned − `issuedQty`) over DRAFT/ISSUED/IN_PRODUCTION/PARTIAL vs available stock (`lib/dashboard/kekurangan-bahan.ts`). Dashboard "today/this week" use WIB (`lib/dashboard/hari-wib.ts`). The trend includes SPG sales (net). Every dashboard link carries its filter; the PO list reads `?status=` (incl. `overdue`), piutang reads `?keadaan=`.
- Layout (owner decisions): the sales trend chart sits at the very top under the greeting, then the action list (open, not folded); then Penjualan → Piutang → Produksi & pembelian → Stok; duplicates removed; audit feed removed; report tabs moved to `/backoffice/laporan` ("Laporan operasional").
- **Sales trend by channel** (owner, 2026-10-01): a channel dropdown on the trend card — all (marketplace vs field), one group (a line per channel), or one channel (Shopee / TikTok / Tokopedia / "Jubelio lain" = `SalesOrder.channel` OTHER/OFFLINE; putus / van / SPG). State lives in `?saluran=` (written with `history.replaceState`, so switching never refetches the page and the link can be shared); the legend shows each line's total for the period and clicking it drills down. Every `TitikTren` carries the per-channel split (`lib/dashboard/tren.ts`: `susunTren(baris, …)` takes rows tagged with `saluran`, `bandingkanPeriode(tren, h, filter)`); `marketplace`/`lapangan` stay as the group sums. The analysis tool `ringkasan_penjualan` returns `perSaluran` too. Local dev has 0 `SalesOrder` rows, so the marketplace split was checked with temporary seeded orders (removed afterwards).
- **Post-login 3D show "HAUS ELORAÉ"** (owner pick 2026-10-01, Gentle Monster–style, ±11 s): `components/login/HausElorae.tsx`, mounted by `TiraiLanjutan` after the login hand-off. Chrome sculpture + kinetic rings, 3D letters orbit then assemble the name, gallery captions in the four corners (no greeting or date — removed at the owner's request), then the camera passes through the ring into the dashboard. **Colours follow the user's theme at play time** (`--background`/`--foreground`/`--primary` + `dark` class, re-read for the first 6 s because a first login on a new device gets its theme from the server mid-show; pure mapping `warnaRuang` in `lib/login/haus-elorae.ts`, tested). The ELORAÉ wordmark (owner, 2026-10-01) is **black glossy chrome on a light theme** (`HURUF_TERANG`) and **the theme's primary colour on a dark theme** (semi-metallic, "anodised"); on a dark gallery a too-dark primary is lightened just enough for 3:1 contrast, keeping its hue (`warnaHurufTema`, tested). `three@0.160.0` is a dependency loaded ONLY by dynamic import inside that component; the 3D font is self-hosted at `public/vendor/three-0.160.0/helvetiker_bold.typeface.json` (it has no "É" — the accent is a separate chrome bar). Tap/Esc/Enter/space skips; reduced-motion keeps the old 300 ms fade; any failure (no WebGL, offline) ends the show at once. **There is no black screen anywhere** (owner, 2026-10-01): the login page navigates straight away after a successful sign-in (no fade-to-ink), and the destination is covered by a PLAIN layer in the dashboard's `--background` (`LayarMasuk`, no wordmark) until the first 3D frame paints; the 3D scene's first frame is that same colour (`warnaRuang().awal`), so nothing jumps. The login form calls `handleSubmit` inside its `onSubmit` handler (not during render) because a successful submit now reaches `pergi()`, which writes a ref and `window.location` — the React Compiler lint rejects that path from render. rAF pauses in a hidden tab, so a backgrounded show resumes and ends at its real time. The rejected attempts (thread animation, four Gen Z 2D styles, NOIR/DUNE 3D) were removed; their previews live only in the session scratchpad.
- **Sign-in:** the joke story was removed at the owner's request (do not bring humour back); the post-login transition is the 3D show above (reduced motion: a 300 ms fade of the plain cover). `proxy.ts` now sends `?callbackUrl=<path>` to `/login`, and `/login/redirect` honours it only inside the user's own area (`computePostLoginRedirect`, tested): phone users `/pwa…`, office users `/backoffice…` or `/gudang-retur…`; anything else (other hosts, `//`, backslash, `/login`, `/api`) falls back to the area home.

## SPG tahap 2 (built 2026-10-03, uncommitted on `dev/timotius`)

Owner asked for: offline selling, counter promos/discounts, daily closing report, shopper data + "titip pesan", shifts, product info, commission at PUTUS stores, damage recorded on delivery, lost goods with a decided bearer, damaged-goods warehouse, offline stock work, SPG performance page, printable/PDF surat jalan, store book vs konsi report reconciliation, visitor counter, barcode labels. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-03-spg-tahap-dua-design.md`. Migrations Postgres `25_spg_lanjutan` … `28_luring_tukar` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261003100000_spg_stage_two` … `20261003160000_offline_stock_exchange` (never run). **No new permission codes.** Every policy is a `SystemSetting` default changed in Settings → SPG (`/backoffice/settings/spg`, `lib/spg/pengaturan.ts`): `spg.diskonManual` (off by default; `maksPersen`, `alasanWajib`, `bagianTokoPersen`), `spg.shift`, `spg.komisiPutus` (`PER_SPG` default), `spg.titipPesan`, `spg.tutupHari`, `spg.barangHilang` (`HARGA_TERIMA`, `maksPotongPersen` 50, `dariOpname` on).

- **Counter promos** (`CounterPromo`/`CounterPromoTarget`, pure engine `lib/spg/promo-konter.ts`): one item promo per piece (priority, then best per-unit discount, then BELI_GRATIS on full groups), one MIN_BELANJA promo on the net after item promos, manual discount last (capped by `maksPersen`), all in sen with largest-remainder allocation so Σ line net = total. **`SpgSaleLine.lineTotal`/`SpgSale.total` are NET; `unitPrice`/`subtotal` are gross.** Every reader of SPG sale value must use `lineTotal` (the dashboard trend, commission, kinerja do). `storeDiscount` = the konsi store's share per line (`CounterPromo.storeSharePercent`, manual `bagianTokoPersen`); Elorae's share (`discount − storeDiscount`) reduces the konsi bill, frozen at lock as `ConsignmentCycle.promoBorneByElorae`.
- **Offline selling:** Dexie `pendingSpgSales`, `/pwa/spg/antrean`; the action takes `luring` + `waktuJual` (refused when older than 14 days) and binds to the visit the sale happened in (`visitId`, or `visitClientId` for an offline check-in — `cariKunjunganAntrean`), which may already be checked out. `capturedOffline`/`syncedAt` on the sale. A piece sold offline before an opname that already counted it writes no JUAL row.
- **Offline check-in/out and stock work** (`lib/pwa/offline/luring-*`, Dexie v7 `pendingCheckins`/`pendingCheckouts`/`pendingStoreReceipts`/`pendingStoreOpnames`/`pendingSpgReturns`): check-in, check-out, receiving a delivery (with damage photos), opname and shopper returns are queued on the phone and sent in order — check-in → orders → photos → reports → proof of delivery → SPG receipts → stock work (by capture time) → check-out last (`kirimSemuaAntrean`). A check-in still on the phone is a **local visit** (`kunjunganAktifLokal`/`useKunjunganLokal`, states `antre`/`ditolak`/`terkirim`); `ikatanKunjungan(server, lokal)` picks the visit that applies (the newer one).
