Work done under it carries `visitClientId` and is queued EVEN WITH signal (the server does not know the visit yet); it waits for its check-in (`keadaanCheckinTertahan`) and fails on the phone with `KUNJUNGAN_DITOLAK` if the check-in is refused. The SPG Jual screen, the SPG home sell button, the salesman catalogue, the salesman home card (`app/pwa/beranda/KunjunganBeranda.tsx`) and the store-page visit card all follow the local visit. Server side: every writer checks its idempotency key FIRST and again INSIDE the transaction (a serializable retry after a race must find the winner); offline opname/receipt/shopper return are dated at capture time and judged against the book AS OF that time — goods already counted by a NEWER opname get no book row (same rule as offline sales). Offline check-in older than 14 days is refused (`BATAS_UMUR_CHECKIN_LURING_HARI`, equal to offline sales/stock — it was 12 hours, which would have failed a whole day's receipts after a day without signal; the owner confirmed 14 days on 2026-10-03); clocks running ahead are clamped to now; a check-in arriving after a newer visit is recorded already closed. Offline only works for screens opened once with signal (SW cache: `/pwa`, store pages, `/pwa/spg/sale`, `/pwa/spg/terima` and each delivery, `/pwa/spg/opname`, each receipt's `/nota` and `/kembali`, `/pwa/spg/antrean`). A server action redirect (e.g. `checkIn`) REJECTS the client promise — never swallow it; use `unstable_rethrow`. **Visit photos under a check-in still on the phone** are queued with `visitId: ""` + `visitClientId` (`FotoAntrean`, `photo-queue.ts`), wait for the check-in like other tied work, and the upload route resolves the visit from `StoreVisit.clientId` (owner-scoped) and returns `visitId`. **The mandatory-photo rule at OFFLINE check-out** counts photos the phone knows reached the server (`foto-kunjungan.ts`, `localStorage["elorae.pwa.fotoTerkirim"]`, fed by the store page's synced photos, `bacaSyaratCheckOut` and each queued upload; never lowered) plus the visit's queued photos; unknown counts as zero, so taking one more photo always satisfies it. The receive list (`/pwa/spg/terima`) opens under an offline check-in and marks deliveries whose receipt is already held on the phone (sorted last). **Store transfers offline** (Dexie v8 `pendingMutasiAjuan` / `pendingMutasiKirim`): the SPG can file a request and mark an approved transfer shipped with no signal; both go through the same capture-time-ordered stock step as receipts/opname/returns. The ship key lives in `AuditLog.metadata.kunciKirim` of `STORE_TRANSFER_SHIPPED` (no column; matched in JS) — online ships send it too, so a resend after a lost response is recognised. The DISETUJUI → DIKIRIM guard runs in `runSerializable`; `MUTASI_KELUAR` is dated at capture time and skipped for goods a newer opname already counted (`terhitungSesudah`, exported from `stok-writer.ts`). Codes `MUTASI_DITOLAK`/`MUTASI_DIBATALKAN`/`SUDAH_DIKIRIM` come only from the queue path. For a LANGSUNG direction a ship can be queued against a request still on the phone (`ajuanLocalId`), re-pointed to the server id once the request arrives.
- **NO size exchange at the counter — owner rule, do not build or ask again** (2026-10-03: "SPG ini tidak bisa menerima retur pembeli yang mau tuker size"). A counter size-exchange flow was built and removed the same day; plain shopper returns (`/pwa/spg/<saleId>/kembali`) stay, and their screen says size exchanges are not handled. The salesman's goods exchange with PUTUS stores (`StoreExchange`, `/pwa/tukar`) is a different feature and stays.
- **Lost goods** (`StockLoss`/`StockLossLine`/`StockLossShare`/`StockLossDeduction`, `lib/spg/hilang*.ts`, `/backoffice/barang-hilang`): sources OPNAME (konsi store opname short), MUTASI (decided HILANG, recorded at the SOURCE store), PENERIMAAN (non-mutasi receipt short), MANUAL. Deciding/changing/cancelling = `receivables:manage`; viewing also `spg_sales:view` — the page guards itself and is deliberately NOT in `ROUTE_PERMISSIONS`. Bearers TOKO / SPG / SALES / ELORAE, splittable, Σ must equal the total. ELORAE settles at once (write-off); TOKO at a konsi store is billed when the cycle locks (`ConsignmentCycle.lossChargedToStore`, `bebanHilangUntukSiklus`, released by unlock); TOKO at a putus store is marked billed by hand; SALES is marked deducted per month on the salesman commission report. **SPG shares are deducted from pay when it is fixed:** `tetapkanKomisiToko` (putus, `SpgCommission`) and `kunciAngka` (konsi, `ConsignmentCommission`) call `lepasPotonganHilang` then `potongBebanHilang` per row and store the result in `deduction`; re-setting / unlocking releases first, so the figure is stable and never double-deducted; rows deleted on re-set release theirs. **`commission`/`total` stay GROSS** (that is what `sudahBerbeda` compares); paid out = gross − `deduction`. Cap per payment = `maksPotongPersen` × that payment, oldest share first; the rest carries to the next payment. Once a row is paid it can no longer be re-set, so its deduction is permanent. `StockLossDeduction` (refType `SpgCommission`/`ConsignmentCommission`/`ConsignmentCycle`/`TagihanTokoManual`/`KomisiSalesman`) is the only per-document proof of settlement. Changing/cancelling a decision is refused once a share has deductions (`SUDAH_ADA_POTONGAN`). Source hooks (`catatHilangDari*`) are fire-and-forget and **skipped under VITEST**. The phone (`/pwa/beban`, gaji page) shows only the person's own share. **Value basis default = `HARGA_TERIMA` ("harga toko terima", owner 2026-10-03: an SPG pays what the store received the piece at, e.g. 75.000):** PUTUS = net price on the latest delivery line to that store (`unitPrice − discountAmount/qty`, exact variant first, bonus lines skipped; the order-level delivery discount is NOT deducted); KONSI = the latest priced consignment document to that store — `ConsignmentShipmentItem` (no variant) or an approved non-bonus KONSI `FieldSalesOrderLine` (per variant, dated `approvedAt`) — whose IN-STORE price is converted to what Elorae receives with the store's CURRENT margin (`hargaTerimaKonsi`: 100.000 at 25% → 75.000, owner 2026-10-03; an invalid margin means the price was never grossed up, so it is used as is); else `Item.sellingPrice`, recorded in `metadata.hargaCadangan` of `STOCK_LOSS_OPENED` and badged on the office detail. Settings saved earlier with an explicit basis keep it. **Employee-discount relief:** a per-decision switch whose percent the office TYPES every time (owner 2026-10-03: no default; the old `diskonPegawaiPersen` setting was removed and is ignored if still stored); the action receives the shares BEFORE relief plus `keringananPersen`, and the SERVER runs `terapkanDiskonPegawai` (each SPG/SALES share floored to whole rupiah, the difference added to ELORAE) then `periksaBagian` again. Relief lives only in the decision note and `metadata.keringanan` of the latest DECIDED/CHANGED audit row — the office detail, the "Ubah" dialog (starts from the pre-relief split) and the phone (shows only "sudah termasuk keringanan X%", never the before-amount) read it via `bacaJejakKeringanan`; changing that audit shape means updating it.
- **Damage on delivery** (`StoreReceiptLine.qtyDamaged` + photo/decision, `/backoffice/spg-kiriman-rusak`) and the **damaged-goods warehouse** (`DamagedStockMovement`, `/backoffice/gudang-rusak`): NRI lines RUSAK/EXPIRED enter as MASUK_RETUR; decisions PERBAIKI (back to sellable stock via `STORE_RETURN_RECEIVE`), JUAL_OBRAL, MUSNAHKAN. NRT lines carrying `storeReceiptLineId`/`spgSaleReturnLineId` write no RETUR_KELUAR (`barisTanpaBukuToko`) — those pieces never entered the store book.
- **Reconciliation** (`lib/konsinyasi/pencocokan*.ts`, card on the cycle detail): sold per item from book / SPG notes / both reports / official; end-of-month stock = book vs titipan minus in-process (unreceived shipments, returns not yet counted, damaged not in the book) **minus lost goods already recorded from OPNAME/PENERIMAAN** (`hilangTercatat`) — MUTASI and MANUAL losses are excluded because they do not separate book from titipan.
- **Surat jalan** for transfers: `/pwa/spg/mutasi/<id>/surat-jalan` and `/backoffice/spg-transfers/<id>/surat-jalan` are route handlers returning HTML that **calls `window.print()` on load** — add `?pratinjau=1` to look at it (a headless/test browser hangs on the print dialog otherwise). Dates are Indonesian WIB (`tanggalSuratJalan`), unlike `fmtDocDate`'s English style. No prices.
- **Also built:** daily closing report (`SpgDailyClose`, `/pwa/spg/tutup`, `/backoffice/spg-tutup-hari`, 21:30 WIB reminder cron), shopper data with consent + "titip pesan" auto-closed by the sale that fulfils it (`StoreCustomer`/`CustomerRequest`, `/pwa/spg/pelanggan`, `/pwa/spg/titip`), shifts and swaps (`SpgShift`/`SpgShiftSwap`, `/backoffice/jadwal-spg`, `/pwa/spg/jadwal`), product info + size charts (`Item.material`/`careInstructions`/`sizeChartId`, `SizeChart`, `/pwa/spg/produk`), PUTUS commission (`SpgCommission`, `/backoffice/komisi-spg`), SPG performance (`/backoffice/kinerja-spg`), visitor counter (`SpgVisitorCount`, `/pwa/spg/pengunjung` + a +1 on the SPG home), barcode labels (`/backoffice/items/labels`, Code 128 in `lib/barcode/code128.ts`). Default style `ELORAE` copies the owner's existing label (2026-10-03): "STKCOD : <item SKU>", the bars, the code under them with "size" + the size in a black box, then the brand "Eloraé"; NO price (owner: "harga tidak di cetak di label"). The owner's barcode value looks like `0127000038PS` (= "01" + article `27000038P` + size `S`; "01" is a filler with no meaning, owner 2026-10-03) while their SKU is `<article>-<COLOUR>-<size>`. These barcodes ARE stored in Jubelio (owner, 2026-10-03), the catalog ingest copies them into `Item.variants[].barcode`, so labels encode that barcode and existing labels scan as they are; a variant without one falls back to its SKU. Style `RINCI` keeps the older layout (name, variant, optional price).
- **Photo cleaner registry:** every column that stores an `/api/foto/<id>` URL must be listed in `KOLOM_FOTO` (`lib/foto/pembersih.ts`) or the orphan cleaner deletes live photos; `pembersih-skema.test.ts` fails when a `*Url`/`*Urls`/photo-ish column is in neither `KOLOM_FOTO` nor `KOLOM_BUKAN_FOTO_DB`.
- **`isRetryableTxError`** (`lib/db/tx-retry.ts`) now also retries the Postgres adapter's `TransactionWriteConflict` / 40001 / 40P01 — before it only knew MariaDB 1213/P2034, so serializable races on local Postgres surfaced as errors instead of retries.
- **`parseItemVariants`** (`lib/items/variants.ts`) accepts a JSON string and normalises SKU/Barcode key case — `Item.variants` arrives in both shapes.

## Owner decisions from the 2026-10-04 audit (drive the fix phases)

The full-system audit (salesman app, SPG app, backoffice) scored the ERP 4.5/10: very broad features, but permission holes and money/stock bugs. Fixes run in phases (1 deploy blockers + security, 2 receivables money rules, 3 stock and cost, 4 workflow leaks, 5 salesman↔SPG connections + UX). Owner decisions that later phases MUST follow:
- **SPG till at a KONSI store records the IN-STORE price** (`computeStorePrice` KONSI gross-up, e.g. 75.000 at 25% margin → 100.000), not the PUTUS price. The SPG sale writer currently forces `termsType: "PUTUS"`.
- **Every putus nota tagihan becomes a `Receivable` automatically** (created with the delivery), so nothing is retyped and the credit check sees it.
- **A KONSI store is "another warehouse of mine":** goods leave main warehouse stock when they are SENT to the store and move into that store's stock; a return moves them back; sales at the store reduce the store's stock, never the main warehouse. Today konsi sends never leave the main warehouse while NRI returns add back — that double count is the bug to fix.

**Phase 1 (deploy blockers + security), done 2026-10-04 — rules it introduced:**
- **Case-insensitive search goes through `lib/db/cari-teks.ts` (`cariTeks`/`samaTeks`)**, never a literal `mode: "insensitive"`: Prisma supports `mode` only on PostgreSQL, prod is MariaDB (whose `utf8mb4_unicode_ci` collation is already case- and accent-insensitive), and the Docker build generates the MySQL client, so a literal `mode` breaks the build. `lib/db/cari-teks.penjaga.test.ts` scans all of apps/web for it.
- **Document numbers:** `generateDocNumber` (`lib/docNumber.ts`) now skips numbers already taken in the document's table via `KOLOM_NOMOR_DOKUMEN` — a THIRD map keyed by `DocType` (Partial, so the build does not break; `docNumber.test.ts` asserts its entries). Manual stock adjustments use the generator too (they used "max + 1", which collided with every other ADJ writer for the rest of the month).
- **PIN, password and user actions take the actor from `auth()`, never from a caller-supplied id.** Admin-only functions need `settings_security:manage`; only a system admin (`*`) may create/edit system-role users or grant a system role. `/api/sync`, GRN owner approve/decline (serializable, state-guarded), vendor-return process/complete, supplier bank decrypt/delete, UOM conversion, product push, PO/GRN read actions, dashboard actions, production read actions, inventory read actions and the fulfillment queue now check permissions. Receivables pages and their `/print` routes check `lib/receivables/izin.ts` helpers. `buatKlaimAction` refuses SPG and (with the own-store lock on) stores the reporter does not hold. SPG may open only its own store page.
- `KOLOM_FOTO` entries for required (non-`?`) columns carry `wajib: true` — Prisma rejects `not: null` on them; the schema test enforces it.

**Phase 1b + 2 (permissions left over + receivables money rules), done 2026-10-05 — rules it introduced:**
- **No `"use server"` under `lib/**`** (`lib/tanpa-use-server.test.ts`): every export of such a file is a callable endpoint, and `lib/inventory/costing.ts` exposed the `InventoryValue` writers that way. Client components read through guarded actions instead (`app/actions/nilai-persediaan.ts`, `app/actions/item-images.ts`).
- **`/api/foto/[id]` checks access:** the uploader, `*`, or a document that points to the photo and that the viewer may read (`PEMBACA_FOTO` in the route; salespeople only their own documents). A new photo column needs a reader rule there as well as its `KOLOM_FOTO` entry — a test checks every `KOLOM_FOTO` model has one. Item photos, display guides and avatars are open to any logged-in user.
- Supplier route: DELETE/decrypt need the permission AND a PIN through `verifyPinForAction` (the unlimited `verifyPin` is gone). `assignUserRole` follows Settings → Users (serializable, no own role, only `*` grants system roles, audited). Login records the attempt BEFORE bcrypt (5 parallel guesses max per process).
- **Receivables:** `Receivable.paidBeforeImport` holds what an imported invoice had already been paid; `hitungSisaPiutang` takes it as its third argument. One NRT can never be deducted beyond its value across live BKMs (`RETUR_TERPAKAI`, checked at create, verify and relink; a dangling NRT pointer is skipped, it is already held in full). The person who reported or recorded a BKM cannot decide it (`PEMERIKSA_SAMA`, admins included — prod has ONE admin, so a BKM the admin records from the office needs a second finance user). Verification re-reads each invoice balance inside the transaction (`MELEBIHI_SISA`, never clamped silently). Promise dates compare WIB days; BKM/TT numbers use the WIB year.
- **Every putus nota tagihan becomes a `Receivable`** (`source DELIVERY`, `deliveryId @unique`) inside `recordFieldSalesDelivery`'s transaction; a number already held by another receivable aborts the delivery (`NOMOR_PIUTANG_DIPAKAI`); 0-value notes make none. Date corrections move the receivable's dates too. Old notes: `scripts/backfill-piutang-nota.ts` (dry run by default; review its "dugaan ganda" list with the owner before `--apply` on prod).
- **Appealed final price = net per piece**: the line's promo discount is dropped (order-level promo still honor-at-create, so a total CAN go negative — owner decision pending).
- **Konsinyasi store payments** (`ConsignmentPayment`, finance records each payment after the money is seen, `journals:manage`): a cycle is "Selesai" only when the store's bill is paid (stage `bayar_toko`); unlock is refused once a nota or faktur number is recorded; `kunciAngka` deletes UNPAID commission rows of SPGs no longer assigned to the store.
- **Warehouse return count is blind** (`/gudang-retur`): the store's claim is shown only after the count is saved, and the counter cannot be the NRT's reporter. A charge decided against TOKO creates a `Receivable` for the store (link marker `[beban-retur:<id>]` in `note`), refused for KONSI stores (`TOKO_KONSI` → lost goods instead).
- Dashboard cards follow permissions (`app/backoffice/dashboard/kartu-beranda.ts`, queries for hidden cards are not run where the action allows it); the Piutang menu group is any-of per child; `app/pwa/stores/[id]/layout.tsx` sends an SPG away from other stores' sub-pages; PO audit ids come from the session.
- **Settlements:** a second upload of the same settlement (marketplace + seller + period + money released) is refused; old twins are flagged (`tandaiSettlementGanda`) and their journal button disabled. **Tax invoice numbers (NSFP)** are normalised and unique (`lib/tax-invoices/writer.ts`, `NomorFakturError`).
- Migrations: Postgres `29_dibayar_sebelum_impor`, `30_pembayaran_toko_konsi` (applied locally); MariaDB `20261005100000_receivable_paid_before_import`, `20261005120000_consignment_store_payments` (never run).

**Phase 3 (stock & cost), done 2026-10-06 — rules it introduced:**
- **A KONSI store is a second warehouse.** Goods leave main `InventoryValue` when SENT: a KONSI order at approve (`transferKonsiFieldSalesOrder` in `@elorae/db/reservation-writer`, releases the old reservation, `StockAdjustment` NEGATIVE `KONSI_TRANSFER`, idempotent per line) and a consignment shipment (`kirimTitipan`, per variant — `ConsignmentShipmentItem.variantSku`, refused `VARIAN_WAJIB` without one; typed-name lines move nothing). NRI returns add back once (atomic increment); store sales reduce only the store book/titipan. A transfer is never a sale. Old data: `apps/web/scripts/koreksi-stok-konsi.ts` (dry run by default; `--apply --confirm-prod-writes`, `--lepas-cadangan-opname` for lines an opname already overwrote).
- **SPG till at a KONSI store records the in-store price** (`hargaKasirSpg`, `lib/spg/harga-kasir.ts` — `computeStorePrice` KONSI gross-up rounded to whole rupiah); counter promos apply on it. SPG targets/ranking in rupiah at konsi stores rise by 1/(1 − margin).
- **No KONSI rupiah on the salesman phone, anywhere:** home "Bulan ini", Penjualan, Insentif, Peringkat, Target, the declining-store data and the route (`tokoMenurunHp`). Rupiah on the phone = putus + van; konsi shows as pcs. Guard: `lib/salesman/penjualan-hp.penjaga.test.ts`.
- **Opname:** an SPG store count compares with the book at the moment each line was counted (`umurHitungMs` → `StoreOpnameLine.countedAt`, also through the offline queue; offline sales/returns check the line's count time, not the opname's). The warehouse count is BLIND — `snapshotQty`/variance are not in the payload while CREATED/COUNTING (`angkaSistemTampil`); twin `null`/`""` rows are merged at approval.
- **Finished-goods cost:** each receipt carries material cost × received ÷ planned, so Σ receipts = the WO's material cost; receiving into a cancelled WO/PO is refused inside the transaction; after negative stock the new `avgCost` is the receipt price.
- **Every main-stock writer queues a Jubelio stock push after commit** (`lib/jubelio/antre-push-stok.ts`, best-effort; GRN, FG, adjustments, NRI, KONSI transfer, putus delivery, van, exchange, damaged-goods repair, vendor return, pre-order release). The api push skips a variant item's pooled row and sends its per-variant rows. **Echo guard:** the push records what Jubelio accepted in `JubelioOutbox.payload.terkirim`; a stock webhook whose `end_qty` equals the last push for that code within 10 minutes is skipped (`echo_of_push`). Full analysis + the prod verification query: `docs/jubelio-stok-gema.md`.
- **Webhook dedup key** (`apps/api/src/jubelio/webhooks/kunci-duplikat.ts`): `webhook-event-id` when sent; else for `stock`/`product` (notification-only bodies) body + minute; else body. Before 2026-10-06 the key was the body alone, so the SECOND stock/product change of a group was dropped forever as a "duplicate".
- **Stock card** reads every `StockAdjustment` source plus `StockMovement` (pairs matched, no double count); the adjustments list filters by source on the server (automatic sources hidden by default). Reconciliation compares Jubelio with AVAILABLE.
- **Purchasing:** PPN is computed on new POs (`ppnRatePercent`, 2 dp); a completed vendor return lowers the PO payable (`VendorReturn.poId`/`poCreditAmount`); "Tutup PO" (`purchase_orders:approve`, reason, audit, `closedAt`/`closedById`/`closeReason`) and a GRN into a PO closed short is refused (`PO_DITUTUP`); the supplier-payments register shows the payable after credits. PO cancel/edit and WO issue/material issue run in `runSerializable`; their screens get `{ ok:false, code }` (`batalkanPO`, `keluarkanBahan`, `terbitkanWorkOrder`).
- Migrations: Postgres `31_kiriman_konsi_varian`, `32_opname_waktu_hitung`, `33_pembelian_ppn_retur_tutup` (applied locally); MariaDB `20261005171500_consignment_shipment_variant`, `20261005190000_store_opname_line_counted_at`, `20261006093000_purchase_order_tax_close_return_credit` (never run).

**Phase 4 (workflow leaks), done 2026-10-06 — rules it introduced (no schema change, no new permission):**
- **"Did this visit have a transaction?" has ONE definition: `adaTransaksiKunjungan` (`lib/salesman/transaksi-kunjungan.ts`)**, used by online/offline check-out, the phone's reason check and the auto-close at the next check-in. Orders count only when not `REJECTED` (salesman-cancelled or office-rejected); BKM `DIBATALKAN`, NRT `BATAL` and tukar faktur `BATAL` do not count; a replaced order counts through its replacement (the writer copies `visitId`).
- **Error pages exist:** `app/global-error.tsx` (own `<html>`, fixed Indonesian, "Kembali ke beranda" → `/login/redirect`, "Coba lagi" = full reload), `app/error.tsx` (home picked from the path; none under `/toko/`), `app/backoffice/error.tsx`, `app/pwa/error.tsx` (phone style, offline wording, counts what is still queued on the phone and links to `/pwa/spg/antrean` for SPG sales, else `/pwa/orders/pending`), `app/not-found.tsx`, `app/pwa/not-found.tsx`. They never show the error message, only the digest. "Coba lagi" = `router.refresh()` + `reset()` — `reset()` alone does not recover a server error. Strings: namespace `halamanGalat`.
- **Van sale idempotency on the phone:** the cart, its idempotency key and the payment state live in `sessionStorage["elorae.van.keranjang.<userId>"]` (`app/pwa/van/keranjang.ts`, no buyer name/phone); the key is written BEFORE the send and dropped only once the sale is recorded; "terputus" (timeout/network) keeps the key and the cart, "ditolak" (server answered) is a different path; an emptied cart gets a new key. Logout clears the prefix (`lib/pwa/offline/bersihkan.ts`, tested).
- **A PUTUS good without a price (null, 0, NaN) can never be ordered:** `hargaSah`/`BarangTanpaHargaError` (`lib/field-sales/harga-wajib.ts`, client-safe). `createFieldSalesOrder` refuses inside its transaction (after the idempotency check), actions map it to `BARANG_TANPA_HARGA` (terminal in the offline queue), the reorder cart (`susunIsiUlang`) leaves such goods out and names them, the catalog shows "Harga belum diset", the store portal treats 0 as no price. A deliberately free good goes through a BONUS promo.
- **An order made from an SPG request / recommendation stays linked:** offline queue rows carry `sumber` and the server links on arrival; `tautkanSumberPesanan` refuses a dead order. `rapikanSumberPesananBatal` (`lib/field-sales/isi-keranjang-batal.ts`, never throws, `runSerializable`, audit `STOCK_REQUEST_ORDER_*`/`STORE_RECOMMENDATION_ORDER_*`) runs after a salesman cancel (`batalkanPesananSayaAction`) and after an edit: with a live replacement the link moves to it, otherwise the request goes back to BARU / the recommendation to DITERIMA and the SPG is told. An office rejection deliberately leaves them alone.
- **GRN needs signal.** The old offline GRN queue never synced (`/api/sync` did not know `GRN_CREATE`); it is gone (`/api/sync` answers `GRN_BUTUH_SINYAL`). Instead the whole form, photos included, is a per-user draft in Dexie (`drafGRN`, v6, `lib/offline/grn-draf.ts`), saved 400 ms after a change and on pagehide, restored on open with "Buang draf", deleted only after a successful save. Old queued GRNs show as a warning with "Muat ke formulir"/"Hapus". GRN has no idempotency key (see follow-ups).
- **The dashboard "Jurnal gagal" row clears itself:** `lib/finance/jurnal-tertunda.ts` — `hitungJurnalTertunda` counts a `JOURNAL_PENDING` only while unread AND its document still has no journal; every retry path, the sales sweep and the PO paid toggle call `tandaiJurnalTertundaSelesai`, which sets `AdminNotification.readAt` (never deletes — the van retry buttons ignore `readAt`). Metadata may be an object or JSON text. Supplier payments count as fixed only by a journal posted AFTER the flag. A successful unmark of payment also clears the "paid without journal" flag.
- **Reject confirmation per order kind** (`konfirmasi-tolak.ts`): putus "stock released", konsi its own sentence, pre-order "nothing was reserved". **BKM self-decision:** the verify/reject buttons (detail, list, giro list, `TombolGiro`) are disabled with the reason when the viewer reported or recorded the BKM (`pemeriksaSamaKlaim`, `app/backoffice/pelunasan/[id]/putusan-klaim.ts` — same rule as `putuskanKlaim`, a drift test reads the writer). MELEBIHI_SISA at verification has its own message. **Report tabs follow permissions** (`laporan/tab-izin.ts`). `getDashboardStats(kartu)` skips aggregates no visible card draws.
- **Production/purchasing return codes, not throws:** `updatePO`, `processReturn`/`completeReturn`, `issueAdditionalMaterials`, `updateWorkOrder` (DRAFT guard + writes in ONE `runSerializable`). Screen messages via `pesan-edit-po.ts`/`pesan-tolak-retur.ts`. **`keluarkanBahan` is idempotent** by a key stored in `AuditLog.metadata` of `MATERIAL_ISSUED` (written in the same transaction, matched in JS — no column). The actor comes from the session. Vendor-return lines are parsed by ONE helper (`lib/vendor-returns/baris-retur.ts`); WO reconciliation counts PROCESSED + COMPLETED returns, fabric/accessory lines only, all lines of an item.
- **A Prisma row sent from a server action to a client component goes through `decimalKeAngka` (`lib/db/decimal-ke-angka.ts`)**, which converts EVERY Decimal column by shape. The WO pages listed three Item Decimal fields by name and broke with "Decimal objects are not supported" once `Item.commissionPerPcs` existed (found in the phase 4 browser check).

**Phase 5 (salesman ↔ SPG connections + efficiency), done 2026-10-06 — rules it introduced (no schema change, no new permission code):**
- **Catalog cart** (`lib/field-sales/keranjang-katalog.ts`, `app/pwa/stores/[id]/catalog/QtyStepper.tsx`): quantities can be typed (clamped to the same cap as +, 0/empty removes the line only when the field is left); "+1 pack" in the variant sheet fills sizes from the global pack ratio (`putus.packRatio`, sizes read server-side by `ukuran-pack.ts`, one button per colour; short sizes clamped and named). The cart is saved in `sessionStorage["elorae.katalog.keranjang.<userId>.<storeId>"]` (`.inden.<window>` for pre-orders) with ONE idempotency key per cart, reused for every resend until the order is recorded or queued; not restored or saved under `?ganti=`/`?isi=`; expires after a day of no change; cleared on logout. KONSI saves no price.
- **SPG flow:** check-in at the SPG's own store lands on the SPG home (`CheckInButton` leaves `tujuanSesudahCheckIn`, `LanjutSetelahCheckIn` only on the SPG's own store page). SPG home order: month, attendance card, **Jual right under it**, tasks…, and the check-out card LAST. Tema/Keluar left the SPG home header (they are in Lainnya, like the salesman's).
- **Orders heading to an SPG store** (`lib/spg/pesanan-menuju-toko.ts`, section `#pesanan-toko` on `/pwa/spg/terima`, a task row on the SPG home): pending, approved-not-delivered putus and approved-not-received konsi orders per item × size, pcs only (the query selects no price column). **`KIRIMAN_BERANGKAT`** tells every SPG of the store when goods actually leave (putus delivery, konsi approve, consignment shipment), once per document (marker written before the push, called post-commit from the actions).
- **Receipt shortfall/damage reaches the salesman:** `KIRIMAN_DITERIMA_SELISIH` (`lib/spg/kabar-selisih-terima.ts`, computed inside the receipt transaction into a holder reset per attempt, sent after commit, only for a NEW receipt with a shortfall or damage; per-line shortfall, `lib/spg/selisih-terima.ts`). `/pwa/orders/[id]` shows "Diterima toko: N dari M pcs · rusak R" per delivery (`penerimaanToko`, pcs only).
- **Office dashboard queues:** 16 new rows defined in `lib/dashboard/antrean-kantor.ts` (`ANTREAN_KANTOR`, gated per viewer, query skipped when hidden); every row's href carries the destination filter and the count equals that filtered list (`tujuan-tautan.spec.ts`). New URL filters: pelunasan `?status=`/`?koreksi=1`, retur-toko `?koreksi=1`, kontrabon `?saring=belum`, biaya-perjalanan `?bulan=semua`, spg-requests `?pemegang=tanpa`, stores `?perubahan=menunggu`. Oversold counts per ITEM everywhere on the dashboard (`hitungBarangStokMinus`).
- **Bell:** paged by cursor, read-all marks every row, `getNotificationHref` covers the office categories and the office-side kabar types. **Menu:** new group **Pembelian** (PO, supplier payments, vendor returns); PO badges count under it (`nav-counts`), and Bantuan has the matching `navGroupPurchasing` group.
- **Salesman store page:** "Lapor pelunasan" (PUTUS only) and "Lapor retur" tiles open the forms with `?toko=`, honoured only when the store is in that form's own picker; "SPG hari ini" (`lib/spg/kehadiran-toko.ts`: at the store since / gone home / leave / day off by schedule / not yet, WIB day). `/pwa/retur` also lists `reportedBySpg` returns at stores the viewer holds (view only, badge "Dari SPG").
- **"Di rak: N" in the salesman catalog** for stores with a stock book (`listCatalogForPwa(storeId, { stokRak: true })`, only `/pwa/api/catalog` passes it; portal and WA catalogue never carry it — DB-tested).
- **SPG customer requests → stock requests:** "Minta ke salesman" on titip pesan and barang dicari prefills `/pwa/spg/stok/minta?tambah=…` with reasons `TITIP_PEMBELI`/`DICARI_PEMBELI`. A request for a store with NO holder writes `AdminNotification` `SPG_STOCK_REQUEST_UNHELD` (to `stores:manage`, link `/backoffice/spg-requests?pemegang=tanpa`); that page is now any-of `spg_sales:view` / `stores:manage` (removed from `ROUTE_PERMISSIONS`).

**Tahap A/B (security leftovers, anti-duplicates, owner decisions, office UI from the 176-finding audit), done 2026-10-07 — rules it introduced:**
- **Provisional owner decisions (2026-10-06: "ikuti usulan Claude dulu, ingatkan saya")** — each lives in ONE named constant so the owner can flip it; remind the owner until confirmed (memory `keputusan-sementara-pemilik`): `ATURAN_BEBAN_TOKO` + `bebanDitagihKeToko`/`batasPotongNota` (lib/receivables/rules.ts: a TOKO charge bills only what the store actually deducted beyond the counted goods), `RETUR_DITAHAN` (a BKM may not pay the part of an invoice held by a return; `BarisPiutang.bolehDilunasi`), `PESERTA_KOMISI_KUNCI_ULANG` (relock keeps unpaid commission of SPGs who moved), `batasiPromoPesanan` (order promo ≤ net subtotal, total never < 0), `betulkanHasilKunjunganDiTx` + `STATUS_BKM_BUKAN_TRANSAKSI` (cancel after check-out clears the TRANSAKSI outcome; finance-rejected BKM is not a transaction), `bukaSumberPesananDitolak` (office rejection reopens SPG request/recommendation), `DASAR_OMZET_KONSI = "LAKU_DI_TOKO"` (lib/salesman/penjualan-konsi.ts: konsi omzet = sold at the store at Elorae's price, owned by the store's holder at the time of sale; never the transfer value; phone still pcs only), `nilaiSpgElorae` (trend values SPG konsi receipts at Elorae's price), `LABEL_MENCETAK_HARGA = false`, `KATEGORI_NOTIF_RETUR_TANPA_PEMEGANG`, `batasNilaiKembali` (returned material after FG receipts is valued at most at the cost not yet carried).
- **Idempotency columns** (migration Postgres `34_kunci_kirim_hash_foto`, MariaDB `20261006140000_idempotency_keys_photo_hash` — never run; its `UPDATE Foto SET sha256 = SHA2(bytes,256)` reads every LONGBLOB, size the table first): `GRN.idempotencyKey` (one key per GRN form fill, kept in the Dexie draft; checked first and again inside `runSerializable`; a resend returns the first GRN with `ulang: true` and no second push/journal), `MaterialIssue.idempotencyKey` = `<woId>:<kunci>`, `ConsignmentPayment.idempotencyKey` = `<cycleId>:<kunci>` (checked BEFORE validation — a full settlement resent must not read as "exceeds balance"). A resend with EDITED fields returns the FIRST document (owner decision).
- **Photo fingerprint:** `Foto.sha256` (filled at upload, `lib/foto/sidik.ts`); `periksaFotoKembar` (`lib/foto/kembar.ts`) refuses `FOTO_SUDAH_DIPAKAI` for a byte-identical photo owned by someone else that another document already uses (pass `kecuali` when correcting the same document).
- **Security:** the JWT re-reads role/permissions/assignedStoreId from the DB when older than 60 s (a revoked permission dies within a minute; `proxy.ts` still reads the cookie). `aksesTokoHp` applies the own-store lock to `/pwa/api/catalog`. The store portal shares 30 requests per link per minute in process memory (`portal-toko-batas.ts`, `TERLALU_SERING`), answers stock shortfalls only as buckets in multiples of 6 (`KELIPATAN_STOK_PORTAL`), and re-counts the daily limit inside `createFieldSalesOrder`'s transaction. `app/actions/notifications.ts` is no longer `"use server"`. apps/api test setup points `DATABASE_URL` at port 1 — **never 3306, which is the SSH tunnel to PRODUCTION MariaDB on the laptop.**
- **Finance:** `/backoffice/finance/journals?status=tertunda` (`STATUS_TERTUNDA`) lists open `JOURNAL_PENDING` with document, Indonesian reason and repost link — same rows as `hitungJurnalTertunda`; one readiness banner (`SpandukBukuBesar`, `_kesiapan/kesiapan.ts`: 11 required roles + journals + start date) on Jurnal and the four reports, screen and print. "Cakupan data" comes from one list in `lib/finance/reports/disclosures.ts`.
- **Purchasing/production:** PO detail has "Terima barang" (→ `/backoffice/inventory/grn/new?poId=`) and its GRN list; `/backoffice/inventory/grn/[id]` is the GRN detail; supplier payments split payable (goods received) from PO commitment; old English PO history notes are translated on display (`catatan-riwayat.ts` — a new writer note needs its row there). "Kembalikan bahan" (`lib/production/kembali-bahan*.ts`, StockAdjustment source `WO_MATERIAL_RETURN`, key `woret:<wo>:<kunci>:<barang>`) and a WO can be cancelled once every issued material is back.
- **Receivables UI:** "Lewat jatuh tempo" (`lib/receivables/jatuh-tempo.ts`, WIB days, only invoices without kontrabon/pending BKM/held return) is distinct from "Lewat janji bayar"; office lists search + paginate on the SERVER (25/page, `?cari=`, `?halaman=`) and hide settled rows by default; the warehouse blind count strips claims/deductions/photos server-side for counters (`retur-toko/buta.ts`).
- **Navigation:** the menu now lives in `lib/nav/menu.ts` (not BackofficeShell); badge groups derive from the target route (`grupUntukRute`). Bell texts of known office notifications are translated at render (`lib/notifications/tampilan-lonceng.ts`, pattern-matched for old English rows — a writer that changes its sentence silently falls back to the stored text).
- **Light theme:** text/links in the brand colour use `--tautan` (`text-tautan`; `.text-primary` text is remapped in light mode by `globals.css`), ≥ 4.5:1 on every theme colour (`app/kontras-tautan.spec.ts`). Checkboxes use the foreground colour in light mode (lime was ±1.6:1). Buttons keep `bg-primary`.
- **Roles:** Settings → Peran & Izin explains every permission in Indonesian (`lib/rbac/penjelasan-izin.ts`: group by work area, label, one-line description, pages it opens; tested against `PERMISSIONS` and `ROUTE_PERMISSIONS`) — a new permission code needs its entry there. Ready-made office roles KEUANGAN, ADMIN_PENJUALAN, ADMIN_PAJAK: `seed-office-roles-permissions.sql` (both DBs; run on prod AFTER the receivables/consignment/tax/finance-report seeds).
- **CI:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (PRs to master): type-check api + web (`next typegen` first), lint web, DB-free guard tests with `DATABASE_URL` on port 1.

## Pulau status & gerak (motion), built 2026-10-07

Owner asked for in-app motion "seperti dynamic island". Rules:
- **One status island for the whole app**: `components/pulau/PulauStatus.tsx`, mounted ONCE in `app/layout.tsx` (`data-pulau-status`, plus two `sr-only` live regions). Every message goes through `lib/pulau/pulau.ts`: `tampilkanPesan`/`tutupPesan`, `mulaiProses` → `perbaruiProses` → `selesaiProses`/`gagalProses` for long jobs (resync, import), `kabarNotifikasi`, `setLuring`. The state machine is pure (`lib/pulau/keadaan.ts`, tested). **Existing `toast()` calls keep working** — `lib/pulau/jembatan-toast.ts` feeds sonner's toasts into the island via `useSonner`, so do not mount a second `<Toaster>` and do not render sonner's own stack. `lib/pulau/pemasangan.penjaga.test.ts` fails on a second island, a second Toaster, or a second offline/notification watcher.
- Watchers: offline in the office = `components/offline/OfflineIndicator.tsx` (headless, in `BackofficeShell`); on the phone `PemantauLuringHp` + `PemantauKabarHp` in `app/pwa/layout.tsx`. Tapping the island expands it.
- **`motion` (v14) is imported ONLY under `components/pulau`** through `LazyMotion` (`fitur-gerak.ts`). Everything else uses CSS: button press, dialog/sheet spring (`lib/gerak/kurva.ts` — `KURVA_PEGAS`, `KURVA_LEMBAR`), staggered list rows (≤ `MAKS_BARIS_BERURUTAN`), new/changed row highlight (`useSorotBaris` + `.sorot-baru`, `lib/gerak/sorot.ts`), numbers that count up (`components/gerak/AngkaNaik.tsx` — the ONLY counter; the old `components/dashboard/AngkaNaik` and `lib/dashboard/angka-antara` were removed), uniform list skeletons (`KerangkaDaftar`). `lib/gerak/gerak-statis.test.ts` checks every new animation has a `prefers-reduced-motion` antidote and that CSS numbers match `lib/gerak`.
- **Page transitions** = React `ViewTransition` in `components/gerak/TransisiHalaman.tsx`, mounted by `app/backoffice/template.tsx`: enter-only (`update="none"`, `exit="none"`), so filters, pagination and `router.refresh` never animate and the old page never paints over the header. If a browser misbehaves, delete `template.tsx` — nothing else depends on it.
- **SPG fixes in the same batch:** "laku" is NET everywhere through `lib/spg/laku-bersih.ts` (`gerakLakuBersih` / `jumlahLakuBersihSejak`: JUAL minus shopper returns, INCLUDING damaged returns that never re-enter the book, capped by the receipt's own JUAL) — store stock speed, consignment reconciliation, salesman absorption, store analysis and konsi omzet all read it; `saran-tawar` uses `qty − returnedQty`. A MANUAL lost-goods decision now writes store-book kind **`HILANG`** (Postgres `35_buku_toko_hilang`, MariaDB `20261007090000_store_stock_loss_kind`), so the later opname no longer opens a second loss; it has its badge colour and `stores.stockCard.kind.HILANG`. SPG home shows the DATE when the open check-in is from another day (`labelMasuk`/`labelMasukUntuk`, also on the offline visit card); "Kiriman menunggu" excludes receipts already held on the phone; an offline check-in recorded already-closed still writes attendance, and a corrected check-out clocks out that day; the dashboard queue lists last month's PUTUS stores whose SPG commission is not set (`/backoffice/komisi-spg?bulan=lalu`).
- `lib/offline/db.ts` (office offline queue): `getPendingOperations` filters `retries < BATAS_ULANG` in JS — `retries` is not indexed and `.where('retries')` threw a SchemaError every time the signal came back; the retry count now really increments (it was `retries || 0 + 1`).

## Jadwal produksi per tahap, like SSG (built 2026-10-07, uncommitted on `dev/timotius`)

Owner compared Plan Kerja with their SSG "PLAN PRODUKSI" sheet: Elorae had the data (plan, lead time) but not the flow — no dates per stage, no calendar, no follow-up, no PO tied to a batch. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-jadwal-produksi-design.md`. Migration Postgres `36_jadwal_produksi` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261007120000_production_schedule` (never run). **No new permission codes** — reads `production_planning:view`, writes `production_planning:manage`; the working-day calendar also opens to `lead_time:view|manage`.
- **Model:** `PlanStageStep` (ordered steps of one `PlanStage`: name, optional `processTemplateId`/`supplierId`, `durationDays`, `targetDate`, `doneDate`, status `BELUM|PROSES|SELESAI`); `PlanStage.scheduleStart`/`deadline`; `PurchaseOrder.planStageId` links a PO to the batch.
- **Dates are working days** (`lib/production/hari-kerja.ts`, pure): `SystemSetting["produksi.hariKerja"]` (default Mon–Fri, like SSG) and `["produksi.tanggalMerah"]` (typed per year; a button fills the five fixed-date national holidays — moving holidays are never guessed). The start day is NOT counted (a 1-day step starting Monday ends Tuesday). Changing working days or holidays re-schedules every open stage (confirm dialog on Lead Time → Hari kerja & libur).
- **Chain rule** (`lib/production/jadwal.ts`, pure): each step starts where the previous ended; a SELESAI step re-anchors on its real `doneDate` and moves every later step (earlier or later), PROSES moves nothing; backward mode finds the start from the deadline (`mulaiDariTenggat`). `doneDate` can never be after today. "Telat" only after the WIB day has passed.
- **Per-quantity steps are proportional, like SSG** (owner 2026-10-07: "di hitung sebanding dengan cara SSG saja"): `computeStepDays` = `max(1, ceil(qty × days ÷ rate))` — 12.000 pcs at 7 days/10.000 = 9 days. It used to round up to whole batches; every lead-time screen now uses the proportional rule.
- **Stage workspace** `/backoffice/production/planning/tahap/[id]`: pull steps from several suppliers' Lead Time chains in order (fabric mill → tailor → printer), forward or backward, add/edit/move/delete steps, change status (dialog previews the new finish date), set start, the stage's WO and POs, and material needs vs PO'd/received. Every preview in the browser uses the SAME pure functions as the writer (`ruang-kerja.ts` mirrors `jadwal-writer.ts` line by line — change both). Backward mode warns before saving (`peringatanJadwal`): finish after the deadline (done steps ran late) or the first unfinished step should already have started. **Only ACTIVE suppliers with a process chain are offered** — a supplier still awaiting approval does not appear.
- **Plan Kerja → Kalender** tab (`?tab=kalender&bulan=`): stages per month with schedule, WO and PO state. **Follow-up produksi** `/backoffice/production/follow-up` (Produksi menu): today / late / this week / all, list or calendar, per-supplier filter, WhatsApp to the supplier (message always Indonesian), status change in place; ONE filter definition `whereFollowUp` feeds the tabs, the counts and the two dashboard queue rows (`productionStepsOverdue`, `productionStepsDueToday`).
- **PO ↔ stage:** "Buat PO" on the workspace opens `/backoffice/purchase-orders/new?planStageId=`; `createPO` now returns `{ ok: true, id, docNumber } | { ok: false, code: "TAHAP_TIDAK_ADA" }` (it used to return the PO); `updatePO` treats `planStageId` undefined = untouched, null = detach. Stage writes (`createPlanStage`/`updatePlanStage`) return `KodeTolakTahap`, run in `runSerializable` with audit, and validate the variant and month — manual stages used to fail "Buat WO" with `TAHAP_TANPA_VARIAN`.
- Audit names in `JEJAK_JADWAL` (`lib/production/jadwal-jejak.ts`), codes in `KODE_GALAT_JADWAL` (each has `jadwalProduksi.server.err<KODE>` in both locales). Client screens may value-import only `jadwal.ts`, `hari-kerja.ts`, `jadwal-jejak.ts` and `lib/leadtime/calculations.ts`; `jadwal-queries/writer/pengaturan` are `import type` only.

**Supplier list views (same day, trimmed 2026-10-08):** Tabel / Galeri / Papan switcher like Notion or Airtable (`components/tampilan/PilihTampilan.tsx`, `lib/tampilan/tampilan.ts`: `?tampilan=` plus a per-user choice in localStorage). **The supplier list is MASTER DATA ONLY** (owner, 2026-10-08: a master list does not show how far each supplier's work has got): columns are name/code, type, contact (phone + email + WhatsApp button), tax (PKP status, PPh withholding, NPWP present), bank account and address — never open POs, running WOs, payables or "last order". The activity columns and `lib/suppliers/aktivitas*` were removed; progress lives on its own work screens (PO list, Follow-up produksi, Pembayaran pemasok, Umur utang). Same rule for every other master list (memory `data-master-tanpa-progres`). Board fetches up to `UKURAN_PAPAN_PEMASOK` = 100 and says so when cut. The type-chip row was removed at the owner's request (type is a Select again).

**SSG UI/UX gaps closed (same day, after a static read of the owner's SSG app — never run it):**
- **Drag-and-drop with pointer events (mouse + finger), never the HTML5 drag API:** `components/gerak/useSeretUrut.ts` (+ `HantuSeret.tsx`, pure math `lib/gerak/seret-urut.ts`) — drop target found with `elementFromPoint` + data attributes, only the grip is `touch-none`, a press under the threshold is still a click, Esc cancels. Used by Lead Time → Papan Pemasok (palette → card at a position, reorder in a card), SOP Rantai, and the stage workspace schedule (`pindahLangkahKe({ stepId, posisi, olehUserId })` in `jadwal-writer.ts`, `runSerializable`, preview dates via `pratinjauPindah`). The ↑/↓ buttons stay as the keyboard path.
- **"Centang pemasok…"** adds/removes one process across many suppliers in one serializable action (`lib/leadtime/rantai-pemasok-writer.ts`: `sisipkanLangkahPemasok`, `aturProsesBanyakPemasok`; sequences rewritten in two phases because `(supplierId, sequence)` is unique).
- **"Belum ada PO"** (amber row + badge) on Plan Kerja → Kalender and a **"Sudah ada PO"** figure on the dashboard (`lib/production/sudah-ada-po.ts`; null without `purchase_orders:view`).
- **PO from a stage, pre-filled** (`/backoffice/purchase-orders/new?planStageId=&isi=kurang[&bahan=]`, `lib/purchase-orders/isi-dari-tahap.ts` reuses `ruangKerjaTahap` — never a second shortfall formula) + "Kebutuhan vs tahap" 3-way calculator (`kebutuhan-tahap.ts`). **Colour per material line = a VARIANT of the material** (provisional owner decision 26): "Hitung warna" (`rincian-warna.ts`, split in cents so lines sum exactly), "+ Warna baru" adds `<SKU>-<WARNA>` to a non-FG item's `variants` with an optional TCX code under the reserved variant key `pantone` (`varian-warna-writer.ts`, refuses FINISHED_GOOD — their variants come from Jubelio), rolls = ceil(qty ÷ `Item.kgPerRoll`) for KG items. `kgPerRoll` is in `KOLOM_DECIMAL_ITEM` (a missing Decimal there breaks every page that loads items).
- **PO print letter:** opening/closing text in `SystemSetting["purchasing.suratPo"]` (card on Pengaturan → Pajak, `settings_tax:manage`, audited), editable per print in `purchase-orders/[id]/DialogCetakPo.tsx`; Warna/Roll columns print only when a line uses them.

## Ruang KOL — Kreatif rebuilt on the owner's KOL Manager (built 2026-10-07, uncommitted on `dev/timotius`)

Owner: "check UI dan UX untuk KOL … kemudian implementasikan". KOL Manager (owner's own app) was read statically (inventory in the session scratchpad, `uiux-kol.md`) and rebuilt the Elorae way; its bugs (double listeners, approved drafts vanishing, "mark paid" total of one page only, inline resi creating duplicate rows) were not copied. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-ruang-kol-dan-ssg-ui-design.md`. Migration Postgres `37_ruang_kol` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261007150000_kol_workspace` (never run). **No new permission codes** (`creative:view` / `creative:manage`). Creative was never on master, so no prod data is affected.
- **`KolBrief` IS the collaboration** (table name kept, screen says "Kolaborasi", number `BRIEF/YYYY/NNNN`). `status`/`KolBriefStatus`/the text `campaign` are gone; it has `tahap` (13 stages PROSPEK…SELESAI + exits DITOLAK/GHOSTING/BATAL), `campaignId` → `KolCampaign`, `jenis`, `picId`, payment fields. Stage rules in `lib/creative/tahap.ts`: forward free, backward/exit/reopen need a reason (audited). **Facts move the stage, never the user, through ONE seam: `terapkanMajuOtomatis(tx, …)` (`lib/creative/maju-otomatis.ts`) called inside the writer's transaction** — resi → SAMPEL_DIKIRIM, receipt date → SAMPEL_DITERIMA, draft link → DRAF, approved → DRAF_DISETUJUI, live link → TAYANG, metric on/after live + 7 WIB days → ANGKA_MASUK, LUNAS (after TAYANG) → DIBAYAR. First WhatsApp to a PROSPEK collaboration → DIHUBUNGI. Fee counts as expense from TAYANG.
- **One place for numbers: `angkaKolaborasi(ids, { periode })` / `jumlahAngkaKolaborasi` (`lib/creative/angka-queries.ts`).** Views = latest metric per content (metrics are NEVER overwritten — every entry is a `KolContentMetric` row); cost = fee + boost + shipping + sample HPP (departed shipments) + commission; ROAS = GMV ÷ cost. Hari ini, Kolaborasi, KOL, Campaign budget bar, Laporan and the rekap all read it — do not compute these on a screen.
- **Samples move stock** (`lib/creative/sampel-writer.ts`): per VARIANT, checked against available = on hand − reserved, cut ONCE when the shipment departs (resi saved; "tanpa resi" stores `-`), `StockAdjustment` NEGATIVE `KOL_SEEDING` keyed `kol-sampel:<lineId>`, avg cost copied to `KolShipmentLine.unitCost`, Jubelio push queued after commit with source `KOL_SAMPEL`. One unsent shipment per collaboration (inline resi updates it). Collaboration stage changes never move stock.
- **WhatsApp:** `components/kreatif/TombolWa.tsx` is the only WA button (templates filled server-side by `siapkanPesanWa`, wa.me opened synchronously on click, `catatKirimWa` records the contact; `templateAwal` highlights the template that fits the task). Templates/thresholds/identity are `SystemSetting` `kreatif.templateWa` / `kreatif.ambang` / `kreatif.identitas` (Kreatif → Pengaturan, code defaults).
- **Hari ini** = 13 task groups from pure `lib/creative/tugas.ts` + `tugas-queries.ts` (cached per request; the same count is the tab/menu badge via `lib/nav/lencana-menu.ts`). **Kalender** = derived deadlines (`tenggat.ts`, WIB, late only after the day passes) + events + personal agendas (`KolAgenda`).
- **Detail page panels** (async server components `{ kolaborasiId, bolehMengelola }`): `PanelPengiriman`, `PanelKontenKol`, `PanelPenjualan`, `PanelBayar`, `PanelCatatan` (timeline = manual `KolNote` + `AuditLog`; content/shipment/sale/payment audits are written against entity `KolBrief` with the sub-id in metadata so the collaboration timeline finds them).
- **Payments are per TERM with PPh (K3, built 2026-10-07/08, uncommitted until the K3 commit; migration Postgres `38_kol_pembayaran_pajak` applied locally / MariaDB `20261007180000_kol_payment_tax` never run).** `KolPaymentTerm` rows (label, %, trigger DEAL/TAYANG/TANGGAL + days, bruto/DPP/PPh/neto, `grossUp`, `bayarKe`, proof photo, bukti potong number, deposit date + NTPN). `KolBrief.modePajak`: DIPOTONG (fee = bruto, the KOL receives fee − PPh) or GROSS_UP (fee = what the KOL receives; bruto found by binary search for the smallest whole-rupiah gross, `hitungDariNeto`). PPh pure in `lib/creative/pph.ts` (client-safe): PPh 21 non-employee = Pasal 17 brackets on 50% of bruto, PPh 23 = 2%, +20%/+100% without NPWP, rounded DOWN to whole rupiah, computed per term; rates are `SystemSetting["kreatif.pajak"]` (Kreatif → Pengaturan, saving recomputes unpaid terms). `selaraskanTermin` is the ONLY writer of `KolBrief.payStatus/paidAmount/paidAt`. Money moves only through `termin-writer.ts` (all state guards in `runSerializable`, audit in the same transaction): pay one/many, cancel payment (refused once the PPh is deposited), bukti potong and deposit are finance only (`journals:manage`). Bank account/NIK/NPWP are AES-256-GCM encrypted per KOL+column with an HKDF key from `ENCRYPTION_KEY` (`data-sensitif.ts`, NOT the PIN-derived `lib/encryption.ts`); only last-4 digits are shown; the full view needs `journals:manage` + PIN and writes `KOL_SENSITIVE_VIEWED`. **Journals** (`jurnal-kol.ts`, roles `KOL_EXPENSE`, `KOL_PAYABLE`, `KOL_PREPAID`, `PPH21_PAYABLE`, `PPH23_PAYABLE`, optional in the readiness check): `KOL_FEE` recognises Σ bruto of live terms from TAYANG (posts only the difference, moves prepaid to payable), `KOL_PAYMENT` DR payable or prepaid (bruto) / CR Bank (neto) / CR PPh payable, `KOL_PAYMENT_REVERSAL` mirrors it, `KOL_SAMPLE` DR KOL expense / CR inventory per item type. No GL start-date gate (like GRN/van). Backfill: every fee > 0 got a "Pelunasan" term; history already paid or recognised has `jurnalOtomatis = false` and is never journaled. Finance page `/backoffice/finance/pph-dipotong` (`journals:view`). Cost and ROAS (`angka-queries.ts`) use Σ bruto of live terms, so gross-up PPh counts as cost; Hari ini/Kalender/Kolaborasi deadlines are one per unpaid term from its trigger (`TerminTenggat` in `tenggat.ts`, loaded via `termin-tenggat-queries.ts`), falling back to "live + N days" only for a collaboration with no terms. Sales are typed per collaboration (provisional 25); TikTok/Shopee file import waits for sample files.
- **Client import boundary:** client files may value-import only the pure `lib/creative/*.ts` (tahap, ambang, tenggat, tugas, angka-konten, wa, kembar, angka-masuk, rekap, aturan, *-aturan, *-param, kol-saring, laporan-*); `*-queries.ts`, `*-writer.ts`, `pengaturan.ts`, `maju-otomatis.ts` are `import type` only.
- `relationMode = "prisma"`: a collaboration can briefly point at a deleted KOL; list readers filter `c.kol != null` instead of crashing (`tugas-queries.ts`, `bayar-queries.ts`).
- **KOL monitoring through a Chrome extension (pilot, TikTok only, built 2026-10-08/09, committed locally bbf53b6a + b69328e0 + 7d74c60c).** The owner rejected the official TikTok/Instagram APIs and chose an automatic extension knowing the risks (both platforms' terms forbid automated collection → the checking account can be restricted; page structure changes break it; a computer must stay on). Design and the FIXED API contract: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-pantau-kol-ekstensi-design.md`. Migrations Postgres `42_pantau_kol` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261008200000_kol_monitoring` (never run). No new permission codes (`creative:view`/`creative:manage`).
  - **Extension** `extensions/elorae-kreatif/` — MV3, plain JS, deliberately OUTSIDE the pnpm workspace (no lockfile change), tests `node --test extensions/elorae-kreatif/test/`. Every decision is a pure function in `lib/aturan.js`; every TikTok page marker (captcha, login, not found, private) lives in ONE constant `POLA_HALAMAN` in `lib/pengurai.js` — that is the file to fix when TikTok changes. Numbers come from `<script id="__UNIVERSAL_DATA_FOR_REHYDRATION__">` (`webapp.user-detail` / `webapp.video-detail`) plus `/api/post/item_list/` responses captured by a MAIN-world fetch/XHR wrapper (`halaman-utama.js`). An unreadable number is never sent as 0 (field absent → `TIDAK_TERBACA`). Hosts allowed: `http://localhost:3000`, `https://elorae.cloud` (not `www.`). Installed by the owner via Load unpacked (README in Indonesian); never install it for them.
  - **Server** `lib/creative/pantau-*.ts`, endpoints `app/api/kreatif/ekstensi/{sambung,status,tugas,hasil,masalah,manual,putus}` OUTSIDE NextAuth (`/api/kreatif/ekstensi/` in the `proxy.ts` allow-list): bearer token = 32 random bytes, only its sha256 stored on `KolPerangkat`; pairing code 8 chars, 15 min, single use. The lease ANTRE→DIPINJAM runs in `runSerializable` with a race test (a plain `$transaction` failed it). A rejected payload costs one attempt per lease (3 → GAGAL); CAPTCHA/LOGIN pauses the DEVICE (`jedaOtomatisMenit`) and returns the job to the queue without an attempt. Results go through the same metric writer as typed numbers (`catatAngkaDiTx` in `konten-kol-writer.ts`) so `terapkanMajuOtomatis` moves the stage; snapshots carry `source "ekstensi"` + `perangkatId`.
  - **ER of an account** (`pantau-er.ts`): average per-post ER over ≤ 12 latest posts that are not pinned, ≥ 48 h old and have views; per-post ER = (likes + comments + shares + saves) ÷ views — the same formula as content ER (includes saves, so slightly above HypeAuditor benchmarks). < 3 valid posts → `null`, never 0, and the last ER is kept. Stored on `KolAccount.er/avgViews/erUpdatedAt` and each `KolFollowerSnapshot`; the manually typed `Kol.engagementRate` is NOT overwritten.
  - Queue (`pantau-antrean.ts`, cron 06:00 WIB + "Susun antrean sekarang"): content H+1/3/7/30 (only the latest due checkpoint; older ones DILEWATI, > 14 days late not queued), active KOL accounts every `hariAkunAktif` days, others every `hariAkunLain`, blocked KOLs never. Settings `SystemSetting["kreatif.pantau"]` (Kreatif → Pemantauan). "Hari ini" gained "Anomali akun" and "Perangkat pemantau berhenti".
  - When inserting test rows by SQL into these tables, write UTC (`now() at time zone 'utc'`): a WIB `now()` makes a job look 7 h in the future and `/tugas` answers ANTREAN_KOSONG.
  - **In-page panel on TikTok** (`konten.js`, redesigned 2026-10-09: dark card, big numbers, account ER, average views, 12-bar recent-views chart, minimised pill shows the ER). Its numbers come from the pure `lib/panel.js` (tests `test/panel.test.js`), whose `hitungErAkun` is a COPY of `apps/web/lib/creative/pantau-er.ts` — change both, or the panel shows a different ER from the one Elorae stores. Icons are built with `createElementNS`, never `innerHTML` (TikTok may enforce Trusted Types). Every module `konten.js` imports must be listed in the manifest's `web_accessible_resources`. Since v0.2.0 (2026-10-09, committed locally 19084032) the panel also has themes (otomatis = luminance of TikTok's page background, else the system; terang; gelap), accent presets + a custom colour, lengkap/ringkas, left/right — all pure in `lib/tampilan.js` (palettes contrast-tested), stored in `chrome.storage.local["tampilanPanel"]` and set from the popup, which the open panel follows live. Profile extras: median views (of the ER posts), views ÷ followers, videos per week, an upload-period comparison 7/30/90 days vs the previous equal window (`ringkasPeriode`, both windows END 48 h ago so young videos never read as a drop; "belum mencakup" when the captured list does not reach back — the extension now keeps up to 200 posts, the server limit), and a fair-price estimate = median views × an ASSUMED price per 1,000 views (Rp 30–60 rb, editable in the popup) with a typed asking price judged murah/wajar/mahal. The panel never redraws while the price field has focus (it would lose the typing), and key events in it stop propagating so TikTok shortcuts do not fire.
- **Paste-link video tracking + Performa video (built 2026-10-09, committed locally 2171bb45; first real run: the owner's @lystianovilda video was read by the extension 2 minutes after pasting).** `/backoffice/kreatif/performa` (tab after Konten KOL, `creative:view`; pasting needs `creative:manage`), no schema change, no new permission. "Tempel link" takes any text (WhatsApp chat included), one per line or mixed, up to 500 links / 200,000 characters, and a KOL-or-Afiliasi choice. Per video, ONE `runSerializable` (`lib/creative/tempel-link-writer.ts`, race-tested): already tracked by video id → skipped; TikTok account by handle → else a new KOL "@handle" (`simpanProfilKolDiTx`, no duplicate check); a running collaboration of the matching kind (Afiliasi = KOMISI only, KOL = anything but KOMISI) → else a new fee-0 one (`buatBriefDiTx`); a `KolContent` with `liveAt` decoded from the video id (`waktuTayangDariId`, clamped to now), which moves the stage to TAYANG through `terapkanMajuOtomatis`; a `KONTEN:<id>:SEGERA` task at priority 95. Short links (`vt./vm.tiktok.com`, `/t/`) are opened SERVER-side (`lib/creative/link-video-pendek.ts`, manual redirects, only tiktok.com hops followed, 5 s, 6 in parallel). The list is one portable SQL (`performa-queries.ts`, `sqlId` quoting) with filter/sort/paging on the server; average ER = mean of per-video ER, same formula as `engagementRate`. Queue (`pantau-antrean.ts`): after H+30 a video keeps a periodic check every `hariKontenBerkala` (7) days until `batasHariKonten` (90; 30 = off), counted from the last check; a reading on/after a checkpoint closes it; one open task per video. Writers `kol-writer`/`writer`/`konten-kol-writer` gained `*DiTx` variants with an optional `sumber` (audit metadata `sumber: "TEMPEL_LINK"`). Provisional decisions 74–81 in memory `keputusan-sementara-pemilik`.

## Akuntansi & pajak lengkap (built 2026-10-08, committed locally f50261f6 + 006a7f79 on `dev/timotius`, not pushed)

Owner: "masukkan [CMT ke HPP, PPN pembelian, tutup buku, gerbang settlement] … check jurnal/akuntansi/pajak, apakah ada fitur yang masih kurang … check perusahaan besar … tambahkan itu". Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-akuntansi-pajak-lengkap-design.md`; research notes in the session scratchpad. Migrations Postgres `39_akuntansi_lengkap` + `40_npwp_toko_pkp_pemasok` (applied locally) / MariaDB `20261008100000_full_accounting` + `20261008160000_store_tax_id_supplier_pkp` (never run). **No new permission codes** — reads `journals:view` / `finance_reports:view` / `supplier_payments:view`, writes `journals:manage` / `supplier_payments:create`. 32 new posting roles (54 total), all mapped by the chart template below. Every provisional rule is decision 34–50 in memory `keputusan-sementara-pemilik`.

- **Periods** (`packages/db/src/period-lock.ts`, `lib/finance/periode/**`, `/backoffice/finance/tutup-buku`, `/mulai-buku-besar`): `AccountingPeriod`/`AccountingYear`. `postJournal` takes `closedPeriod: "shift" | "refuse" | "allow"`; default is refuse for manual and DRAFT journals and SHIFT for everything else (dated the first day of the next open month, `originalDate` kept, description says so). Opening balances, depreciation and recurring journals pass `"refuse"` and handle `PERIOD_CLOSED`. Months close in order from the cutover month, only after they end; required checks are draft journals and documents without a journal; warnings (audited acknowledgement) are unknown-date failures, unmatched bank lines, missing depreciation, PPN recap not final (PKP), marketplace without a settlement. Year close (`YEAR_END_CLOSE`) needs 12 closed months; Trial balance / Income statement / Cash flow exclude it, the Balance sheet includes it. The cutover date is locked once a month is closed. **The settlement journal refuses `SEBELUM_TANGGAL_MULAI` when its whole period ends before the cutover**; a straddling settlement still posts.
- **Sweep + integrity** (`lib/finance/sapuan/{registri,semua,runner,sql}.ts`, cron 02:30 WIB, `/backoffice/finance/integritas`): every journaled source registers a finder (`cariTanpaJurnalSql`, portable NOT EXISTS) — **a new journaled source must register in `sapuan/semua.ts` and be resolvable by `journals/_sumber/info-dokumen.ts`**, or month close reports it as a document without a journal and integrity reports its journal as an orphan. Sources that post inside their own transaction (`PPN_MASA`, `TAX_PAYMENT`, `PPH_FINAL`) are not registered. The sweep posts as the first ADMIN user.
- **Chart template + opening balances** (`lib/finance/templat-akun/`, `lib/finance/saldo-awal/`, `/backoffice/finance/saldo-awal`): template "Dagang & produksi fesyen" maps all 54 roles (CASH 1101, BANK 1102, AP 2101 "Utang usaha", OPENING_EQUITY 3103); NOT installed on the local DB. Opening balances in five kinds (accounts, inventory, store AR — computed from the receivables module, marketplace AR, AP per supplier), one live import per kind, `sourceType "OPENING"`, dated the DAY BEFORE the cutover, counter-entry `OPENING_EQUITY`; re-posting mirrors the old journal (`<id>:balik`). Consignment stock and unpaid consignment bills need ACCOUNTS-kind entries or `INVENTORY_CONSIGNMENT`/`AR_CONSIGNMENT` go negative.
- **CMT in COGS** (`lib/production/biaya-jasa.ts`, `fg-receipt-journal.ts`, `hutang-jasa-queries.ts`): a finished-goods receipt costs material + WO step services per ACCEPTED piece (cumulative delta, Σ receipts = total) + `hppAdditionalCost`; `FGReceipt.serviceCost` is the tailor's bill incl. PPN (the AP amount). Journal `FG_RECEIPT`: DR `INVENTORY_FG` (+ `PPN_MASUKAN` if PKP) / CR `INVENTORY` (material) / CR `AP` (service, memo "Jasa produksi WO …") / CR `ACCRUED_EXPENSE`. **Selling price is MANUAL ONLY** (owner 2026-10-08: "harga jual biar saya yang mengisi sendiri … ini hak saya"): nothing writes `Item.sellingPrice` automatically any more — FG receipt, item save and Jubelio push defaults no longer call `recalcItemSellingPrice` (now callerless in `packages/db/src/item-price-writer.ts`), and the margin / additional-cost fields are gone from the item form and Jubelio settings (columns kept, never written). A manual price change still logs `ItemPriceChangeLog` `MANUAL_EDIT` and still pushes to Jubelio. The item form shows **HPP per pcs** read-only beside the price (`lib/items/hpp-barang.ts` pure + `hpp-barang-queries.ts` server): stock average Σ`totalValue` ÷ Σ`qtyOnHand`, else the latest material-bearing FG receipt, with that receipt's material/sewing/extras per piece (sewing = value − material − extras, never raw `serviceCost`, which includes PPN). Never suggest a price or compute a margin. Material issue is still not journaled (no WIP account): GL `INVENTORY` = raw stock card + `nilaiBahanDiProduksi()`. Readers of "material already charged" use `bagianBahanPenerimaan`, never `totalCostValue`. `Supplier.isPkp === false` → no service PPN; null = old behaviour + warning.
- **PPN & tax** (`lib/finance/pajak/**`, `/backoffice/finance/pajak` + `/ppn` `/setoran` `/pph-final` `/faktur-keluaran`, `/backoffice/finance/pph-dipotong`): settings `pajak.ppn` (`statusPkp` BELUM_DITETAPKAN by default = treated as non-PKP) and `pajak.profil` (legal form, PPh regime). `pisahPpn`: 12% × DPP 11/12, whole rupiah. **Every `PPN_KELUARAN`/`PPN_MASUKAN` line carries its DPP in the memo** — `memoDpp(dpp)` or, for the generational engines whose memo is the role name, `memoPeranDpp(role, dpp)` = `"ROLE · DPP n"`; readers use `dppDariMemo`/`peranDariMemo`. Channel never goes on PPN/PPh lines. GRN input VAT only for PO lines "Exclude PPN" with a rate; when NOT PKP that PPN is capitalized into stock value and AP (PO rate). PPh 22 marketplace (0.5% from 1 Oct 2026) is read from a settlement column named "PPh 22" → `PPH22_PREPAID`. PPN month recap from the ledger (finalised in order, overpayment carried forward), tax payments need a unique NTPN, PPh final UMKM rounded down with the individual Rp 500 jt exemption, Coretax XML export of outgoing invoices (`Store.npwp`/`nitku` fill the buyer; empty → `0000000000000000` + warning). **Elorae IS PKP** (PT LAJU ABADI CEMERLANG, since ±18 Feb 2026): the LOCAL DB holds `pajak.ppn` PKP `berlakuSejak 2026-02-18` and `pajak.profil` PT, rezim PPh still BELUM_DITETAPKAN (owner to answer) — written by `~/.elorae-local/setel-pkp-lokal.mjs` with the same audit rows as the Save button. Prod still has nothing; the owner/finance sets it in Settings → Pajak after deploy. **Under vitest `bacaPengaturanPpn`/`bacaProfilPajak` return the code defaults and never touch the DB** (`lib/finance/pajak/setelan-uji.ts`); a test that feeds its own setting through a fake client sets `UJI_PENGATURAN_PAJAK=setelan`, and PKP tests otherwise mock `ppn-pengaturan`. Trial journals posted before 2026-10-08 on the local bed were posted as non-PKP; new ones carry PPN.
- **SKB** (`Supplier.skbNumber`/`skbValidUntil`/`skbPhotoUrl`, form + amber "SKB habis" on the supplier list): ONE decider `tentukanPotongPemasok` (`lib/finance/pajak/pemotongan.ts`) is used by the payment form preview, `catatPembayaranPemasok`, `terapkanUangMuka`, and supplier cash vouchers. A payment dated (WIB day) on or before `skbValidUntil` withholds NOTHING whatever withholding type is picked; the exemption is stored in the payment/voucher audit metadata (`dibebaskanSkb`) and shows as a 0 row marked SKB on PPh dipotong. Owner practice: badan → PPh 23 2%; non-badan with SKB → none; without SKB → PPh 21 non-employee 2,5%. Changes write `SUPPLIER_SKB_CHANGED` in the same transaction. Elorae pays no down payments to tailors.
- **Consignment VAT is per store** (`Store.consignmentVat`, default false, switch on the KONSI store form, audit `STORE_CONSIGNMENT_VAT_CHANGED`) and is **frozen per cycle at lock** (`ConsignmentCycle.vatApplied`, cleared at unlock; cycles locked before the migration = true). The cycle-lock journal reads `vatApplied ?? store.consignmentVat` (`barisSiklusKonsi(…, pakaiPpn)`); a cycle without VAT skips stage `pajak` and refuses a faktur number (`TANPA_PPN`).
- **Field sales & receivables journals** (`lib/finance/lapangan/**`, `/backoffice/finance/rekonsiliasi-piutang`): `FIELD_DELIVERY` (nota tagihan, once, dated `invoiceDate`, revenue net of discounts + COGS), `FIELD_COLLECTION` (verified BKM; tunai → CASH, transfer/giro → BANK; deduction roles from `DeductionType.postingRole`), `STORE_RETURN` (per NRI, revenue reversed at min(physical, claim), damaged goods into `INVENTORY_DAMAGED` at avgCost frozen in the first journal), `STORE_EXCHANGE`. The generational engine `selaras.ts` nets existing journals per role (memo) + store + channel and posts only the DIFFERENCE as `<id>`, `<id>#2`, … in one `runSerializable` — reuse it for any source that can change; never invent `<source>_REVERSAL` types for those. Documents before the cutover are never journaled; with no cutover nothing posts.
- **Operational journals** (`lib/finance/operasional/**`, `/backoffice/finance/rekonsiliasi-persediaan`): 16 sourceTypes — consignment shipment/order (DR `INVENTORY_CONSIGNMENT` per store), consignment return, **cycle lock = consignment revenue** (DR `AR_CONSIGNMENT`, net Elorae price, PPN included only when the cycle's frozen `vatApplied` is on, dated the end of the cycle month; unlock reverses), SPG pay and putus SPG commission (expense / `COMMISSION_PAYABLE`, paid from BANK), store payments, lost goods (KONSI stores only), sales lost-goods deduction, damaged warehouse (MUSNAHKAN/PERBAIKI/JUAL_OBRAL — proceeds to CASH), manual stock adjustment vs `INVENTORY_VARIANCE` (UMKM opening import excluded), vendor return at COMPLETED (DR AP, CR `PPN_MASUKAN` when the GRN had PPN), travel claims (approve → payable, pay → chosen account or CASH). **SPG sales are not journaled on their own** (decision 37). Timing gap by design: the store book drops stock when the SPG sells, the GL when the cycle locks.
- **Post-commit hooks never throw and are OFF under vitest**: `lib/finance/lapangan/kait.ts` and `lib/finance/operasional/kait.ts` skip unless `JURNAL_LAPANGAN_KAIT_UJI` / `JURNAL_OPERASIONAL_KAIT_UJI` is `"1"` (the journal specs set it). Without the gate, action specs elsewhere posted real journals and `JOURNAL_PENDING` rows onto the shared bed.
- **Cash & bank** (`lib/finance/kas/**`, `lib/finance/bank/**`, `/backoffice/finance/kas` + `kas-kecil` + `kategori`, `/rekonsiliasi-bank`): vouchers BKK/ BKMK/ TRF/ (`CASH_VOUCHER`, posted in the same transaction as the document and audit row), petty cash with a fixed fund, expense categories mapped to accounts (deactivated, never deleted). **A voucher refuses lines on cash/bank, AP, `COMMISSION_PAYABLE`/`REIMBURSE_PAYABLE`/`KOL_PAYABLE` and tax payables** (`AKUN_*` codes) — those have their own "Tandai dibayar" flows, and a voucher with an expense category would book the expense twice. Bank statement import (`XLSX.read` with `raw:true`), auto-match = equal amount within ±3 days, 1:1; bank charges / interest from a statement line become a voucher. Cancel = mirror journal dated at the ORIGINAL date + releases matched statement lines. Three helpers say "is this a cash account" with the same rule (B2 `rekeningKasBank`, C2 `akunKasSah`, D1) — unify later.
- **Supplier payments** (`lib/purchasing/pembayaran-*`, `/backoffice/supplier-payments` + `/baru` + `/[id]` + `/umur-hutang`): real `SupplierPayment` + allocations to GRN / WO step (`TARGET_ALOKASI_LANGKAH_WO`) / opening AP, partial payments, one transfer for many documents, advances (= unallocated part; debit AP, no advance account), PPh 23/21/4(2) withheld (base = service before PPN; 4(2) gross), BYR/ numbers, `SUPPLIER_PAYMENT_DOC` journal. The AP subledger reads GRN journals' AP lines (PKP mode includes PPN) and nets VENDOR_RETURN journals — never `GRN.totalAmount`. The old PO "paid" toggle still works (`SUPPLIER_PAYMENT` `poId#gen`) and writes a mirror row `tandai-lunas:<poId>#<gen>` that the subledger skips; a PO with document payments refuses the toggle (`ADA_PEMBAYARAN_DOKUMEN`) and vice versa. Supplier tax fields (`isPkp`, `taxWithholding`, `hasNpwp`, `npwpEnc` encrypted with context `supplier:<id>:npwp`) — **`npwpEnc` must never reach a client payload** (masked in `maskBank`, the supplier API route and the create/update returns).
- **Fixed assets, recurring journals, maker-checker** (`lib/finance/aset/**`, `berulang/**`, `jurnal-manual/**`, `/backoffice/finance/aset-tetap`, `/jurnal-berulang`): Indonesian fiscal groups (PMK 72/2023) + "Komersial"; depreciation `<asset>/<YYYY-MM>` dated month end, cron 1st 01:00 WIB; disposal with gain/loss; values lock once depreciation exists. Recurring/accrual templates (cron daily 01:15 WIB, accrual auto-reverses on the 1st). **Manual journals and recurring templates need a second person** — "Ajukan" saves a DRAFT, someone other than the maker approves (admins included), approve calls `lockPeriodForPosting`; "Balik" creates a mirrored DRAFT with `reversalOfId`; posted journals are never edited or deleted. Prod has ONE admin → a second finance user with `journals:manage` is needed before any manual journal can post. Manual journal descriptions are capped at 191 characters (`Journal.description` is VARCHAR(191) on MariaDB).
- **Reports** (`lib/finance/reports/**`, `lib/finance/anggaran/**`): Trial balance shows opening / movement / closing with D/K sides (previous year-end closes in the opening); parent accounts are headings; zero accounts hidden by default; negatives in parentheses — new reports use `rupiahLaporan` + `barisPohon`, and show journal text through `teksKeteranganJurnal`/`memoJurnal`. New: comparison (same dates N months back / previous equal window / last year), ratios (current = cash-flow classification KAS/OPERASIONAL; DSO uses AR + AR_STORE + AR_CONSIGNMENT), profit per channel (`JournalLine.channel`/`storeId`, `KANAL_JURNAL`; old journals get their channel from the source document and the report says how many), budgets vs actuals (P&L leaves, normal sign, empty ≠ 0), ledger index. **New writers set `channel` themselves.** Report filter `saringJurnalLaporan` = POSTED only (`SARING_JURNAL_BERLAKU`). The "not yet journaled" disclosure now names only Jubelio stock changes and salesman commission (`SUMBER_BELUM_BERJURNAL` in `disclosures.ts`; English `coverageBody` keys updated by hand).
- **Fixes from the second accounting trial (2026-10-08, `scratchpad/uji-akuntansi-2/`):**
  - **Months before the ledger start lock once the start month is closed** (`packages/db/src/period-lock.ts`): manual/DRAFT/opening journals dated before the start are refused (`PERIOD_CLOSED`), automatic ones shift to the first open month; the month-close list shows them "Terkunci" (`terkunciSebelumBuku`). **Under vitest the DB start date is NOT read** — tests opt in with `UJI_TANGGAL_MULAI_BUKU="YYYY-MM-DD"` (or `setelan` to read the real setting); without it DB tests in 2000–2004 would be refused on a bed whose real start month is closed.
  - **Sweep "nothing to post" marks:** when the sweep gets `NOTHING_TO_POST` for a document it writes one `AuditLog` row `JOURNAL_SWEEP_NOTHING_TO_POST` (entityType = sourceType); month close and Integritas call `cariSemuaTanpaJurnal({ abaikanNihil: true })`, which skips marked documents unless they have an open `JOURNAL_PENDING`. **Every finder must apply `OpsiCariSapuan.ids` inside its query, before the row limit** (`sapuan/sql.ts` does it for SQL finders).
  - **Goods are billed to stores ONLY through delivery notes** (owner 2026-10-08: nota tagihan → piutang → faktur pajak queue, all automatic). "Faktur manual" and "Impor faktur" refuse invoices dated (WIB day) on or after the ledger start date (`SESUDAH_TANGGAL_MULAI`, `lib/receivables/gerbang-tanggal-mulai.ts`; import rows without an invoice date are judged by their due date); older invoices still move in for the opening balance. System charge invoices (`tulisPiutangManual`, `[beban-retur:…]`) are not gated. Under vitest the gate reads `UJI_TANGGAL_MULAI_BUKU` only. With no start date set, nothing is refused.
  - `STORE_INVOICE` journal (`lib/finance/lapangan/`, `sapuan-faktur.ts`, hook `kaitFakturPiutang`): manual and imported receivables dated on/after the start date post DR `AR_STORE` / CR `SALES_REVENUE` (channel PUTUS) / CR `PPN_KELUARAN` if PKP, NO COGS, generational through `selaras.ts`; excluded: delivery invoices (`source DELIVERY`/`deliveryId`), charge invoices `[beban-retur:…]` (already netted in the return journal) and KONSI stores. Cancelling an import reverses on the cancel date.
  - **Store return COGS = the COGS of the latest earlier PUTUS delivery of that item × variant to that store** (`lib/receivables/hpp-retur.ts` `hppJualTerakhir`, current average only when none); the difference to the stock-card value goes to `INVENTORY_VARIANCE`; damaged goods enter the damaged warehouse at that COGS; frozen at the first journal. NRI stock adjustments are keyed per line (`nri-masuk:<lineId>`).
  - **Withholding on advances** (`lib/purchasing/pembayaran-uang-muka.ts`): total PPh on a document = rate × its base whatever the order of advances and payments. An advance applied to an unpaid document is withheld at the next payment to it; an advance that settles a document is withheld at application through a net-0 "potongan" `SupplierPayment` (key `potong-uang-muka:<advanceId>:<n>`, DR AP / CR PPh payable), cancelled with the advance. State is read from payment audit metadata (`uangMukaDipotong`/`uangMukaTerpotong`), no columns.
  - **Stock lookups that a supplier return or GRN decline reaches are null/"" tolerant** — `cariBarisStok` (`lib/inventory/costing.ts`, exported; findFirst, twins never summed) in `reverseInventoryValue`, `reverseMovingAverage`, `getInventoryValue`, the vendor-return actions and the GRN owner-decline check. The vendor-return stock movement records the value that actually left the card.
  - **Document numbers by document date:** `generateDocNumber(type, tx, { tanggal })` numbers by the document's WIB month (BKK/BKMK/TRF vouchers, BYR payments); re-entering a period continues from the highest number used.
