  - Cash flow moves the gain/loss AND the written-off accumulated depreciation of `ASSET_DISPOSAL`/`_REVERSAL` journals from operating to investing ("Penyesuaian pelepasan aset tetap"). Depreciation of a month that has not ended is refused (`BULAN_BELUM_BERAKHIR`). Bank matching never uses a cancelled voucher/payment or a reversed journal (`lib/finance/bank/jurnal-batal.ts`, `JURNAL_DIBATALKAN`).
  - `Item.commissionPerPcs` is edited on the item form ("Komisi SPG per pcs", finished goods only, audit `ITEM_SPG_COMMISSION_CHANGED`). New "materai/meterai" deduction types default to `BANK_CHARGES`; the deduction-type master shows each type's journal account. The consignment "Kunci angka" dialog now carries the item picked in the SPG/store reports (`bandingkanLaporan` keeps `itemId`) and warns about rows without an item or priced 0.
- **i18n changes to EXISTING values** go through `~/.elorae-local/i18n-ubah.mjs <fragment.json>` (refuses missing keys, writes nothing on any error, keeps the 2-space JSON format byte-for-byte). The session-scratchpad `i18n-merge.mjs`/`i18n-ubah.mjs` were lost when the laptop rebooted on 2026-10-08 (`/private/tmp` is wiped on boot) — **never keep a tool other sessions rely on in the scratchpad**; its "add keys" twin is `~/.elorae-local/i18n-tambah.mjs` (refuses existing or one-language keys); both share the lock `apps/web/lib/i18n/messages/.kunci-i18n`. Other durable local tools in `~/.elorae-local/`: `erp.sh` (start/stop/status/`akun` prints test logins), `db.sh`, `tsc-scoped.sh <name> <files…>`, `terapkan-satu.mjs <postgres migration folder>`, `buat-akun-keuangan.mjs` (second finance user `keuangan@elorae.com`, role KEUANGAN, password in `akun-uji-keuangan.txt`), `setel-pkp-lokal.mjs`. Indonesian uses **"utang"**, not "hutang", in every displayed string (45 values + code literals normalised 2026-10-08); keys and routes keep `hutang` (`/umur-hutang`, `saldoAwal.hutang.*`).
- **Password minimum is 8 everywhere** — `SANDI_MIN` in `lib/users/validasi.ts`; Settings → Keamanan's change-password reuses it (it used to say 6).

## Bantuan — explanations live in a help panel, not on screen (2026-10-05)

Owner: "saya tidak suka banyak keterangan … masukkan ke dalam sidebar seperti wikipedia sehingga bisa saya check sendiri, namun tidak tampil di UI". Every page and card explanation moved out of the screens:
- **One entry per route** in `apps/web/lib/bantuan/daftar/<group>.ts` (groups = sidebar groups + `pwaBersama`/`pwaSalesman`/`pwaSpg`), type in `lib/bantuan/jenis.ts`: `rute` (Next route pattern), `judul`, `isi` (paragraphs), `kartu` (card explanations). Text is plain Indonesian or `{ kunci }` of an existing i18n key **without `{placeholders}`** (`daftar.test.ts` checks both locales).
- **A new page needs an entry** — `lib/bantuan/cakupan.test.ts` fails otherwise (allow-list with reasons for redirect/print/offline pages). Phone screens must also show the ⓘ (`app/pwa/tombol-bantuan-hp.spec.ts`).
- **ⓘ** = `components/bantuan/TombolBantuan.tsx`: loads the registry lazily on open and shows the current route's entry. Under `/pwa` it opens from the bottom and links to `/pwa/bantuan`, elsewhere from the right and `/backoffice/bantuan` — automatically, so office components reused on the phone (the BKM form) behave. `PageHeader` renders it beside every title; phone screens use `app/pwa/TombolBantuanHp.tsx`.
- **`PageHeader` never prints `description`** (it is only the ⓘ fallback for a page without an entry). **Data about the record goes in `meta`**, which always shows.
- Wiki pages: `/backoffice/bantuan` (all office + phone entries, table of contents, search; sidebar entry "Bantuan" with no permission) and `/pwa/bantuan` (filtered by role: SPG sees shared + SPG, salesman shared + salesman; "Bantuan" tile in both Lainnya menus).
- **What stays on screen:** field hints, warnings, errors, empty states, numbers/status/data, money rules next to a submit button (e.g. "BKM belum mengurangi piutang"), instructions needed at the moment of work (cashier note in the SPG cart).

## Follow-ups / tech debt (checklist)

Canonical home for follow-ups + known debt. **New follow-ups go HERE** as `- [ ]` — not buried in PR bodies (they die on merge) or per-slice decomposition prose. When an item ships, flip it to `- [x]` and append the PR #. Feature names only (no EPIC labels — shared artifact). Cross-cutting code landmines also get a `memory/` note; this list is the actionable index.

Roadmap slices (not debt) live in the decomposition tables + the GitHub board, NOT here. **Every item below was verified STILL-OPEN against current code on 2026-07-25** (full merged-PR + board sweep, then per-item code check). *Dropped as already-fixed — do not re-add:* P2002 index-name check (`stock-writer.ts:86` robust regex, #73), canvassing van-load forbidden-vs-not-found (no conflation, #148), oversold-only view (now server-side, #133), `docker builder prune -af`/`.next` cache (uses GHA layer cache, not aggressive prune, #99), `@types/node-cron` ("dead" but node-cron is actively used), fulfillment e2e (prod-live since #8).

### Audit fix phase 3 — stock & cost (2026-10-06)
- [ ] **⚠ Before deploying phase 3: verify on prod whether Jubelio echoes our stock PUTs** — the read-only query and how to read it are in `docs/jubelio-stok-gema.md` §3. Phase 3 makes pushes automatic and fixes the variant code they were sent under, so echo damage (each push→echo cycle lowers `qtyOnHand` by `reservedQty`) becomes real if Jubelio echoes. The 10-minute echo guard is in, but a legitimate Jubelio-side stock webhook is still written in "available" units (Usulan A in the doc, after the live questions are answered).
- [ ] Prod hand-run after deploy: `scripts/koreksi-stok-konsi.ts` — dry run, review AKAN_DIPOTONG / TERTIMPA (with source) / VARIAN_TIDAK_PASTI / returns without a shipment with the owner, then `--apply --confirm-prod-writes`; `--lepas-cadangan-opname` only for lines confirmed overwritten by an opname.
- [ ] Owner decisions (konsi omzet = laku, trend at Elorae price, RINCI label without price and the material-return flow were settled on 2026-10-06 — see "Tahap A/B"): gross-up rounding is to whole rupiah (no shelf-price rounding rule); label style RINCI prints the putus price; PUTUS stock pooled in a variant item's `null` row can never be shipped/pushed per size (data work); a WO with issued material cannot be cancelled (no material-return flow).
- [ ] Consignment shipment: the idempotency key lives in `AuditLog.metadata.kunciKirim` (no column — `ConsignmentShipment.idempotencyKey @unique` is the proper fix); after a lost response, an edited resubmit returns the FIRST shipment.
- [ ] Opname: one line's count time is its LAST change (a sale between two scans of the same line can be mis-attributed); the warehouse count stores no per-line time, so approval still compares with stock at approval (drift modal warns); a counted item with no `InventoryValue` row is skipped at approval; `components/inventory/OpnameCountRows.tsx` is now unused.
- [ ] FG receipts recorded before this fix each carry the WO's whole material cost — `avgCost`/`sellingPrice` of those goods may be inflated (no backfill). Cost still excludes CMT service, `hppAdditionalCost` and vendor returns.
- [x] (phase 4: codes, `keluarkanBahan` idempotency key in `AuditLog`, `updateWorkOrder` in one transaction, reconciliation parses the JSON lines and counts PROCESSED + COMPLETED) Still thrown, not returned as codes (masked in prod): `updatePO` (edit flow), `completeReturn`/`processReturn`, `issueAdditionalMaterials`. `keluarkanBahan` has no idempotency key (a post-commit failure invites a double issue). `updateWorkOrder` checks DRAFT outside a transaction. `lib/production/reconciliation.ts` treats `VendorReturn.lines` as an array (it is JSON text) and reads only PROCESSED returns.
- [ ] Old vendor returns have no PO credit (no backfill); the supplier-payment receipt prints `grandTotal` as paid.
- [ ] PO has no discount column (the "PPN after discount" rule is only unit-tested); PPN Masukan is not modelled in the GL.
- [ ] Stock card by type loads every adjustment of every FG item in the range (watch the size on prod); a per-variant card cannot include `StockAdjustment` (no `variantSku` column).
- [ ] Push labels: several writers pass `"ADJUSTMENT"` as `SumberPushStok` (the real source is in `ref`); BOUNDARY §3.1 still says the outbox row is written in the same transaction (it is after commit, best-effort).

### Audit fix phase 4 — workflow leaks (2026-10-06)
- [ ] Owner decisions: (a) a salesman can still create an order, check out (TRANSAKSI) and THEN cancel it — the visit keeps `outcome = TRANSAKSI` (fix in `cancelFieldSalesOrderBySalesman` or derive the outcome on read); (b) a BKM finance REJECTED (`DITOLAK`) still counts as a visit transaction, while office-rejected orders do not; (c) editing an order during a later visit keeps the OLD visit's `visitId`, so the later visit still needs a reason; (d) a PUTUS good priced 0 on purpose can no longer be ordered, even with a price appeal (free goods = BONUS promo); (e) an office rejection leaves the SPG request DIPROSES / recommendation SELESAI pointing at the rejected order; (f) emptying the van cart after "terputus" gives a new key, so refilling the same goods for the same buyer can record twice (keeping the key risks the next buyer's sale returning the old receipt); (g) a successful "unmark paid" also clears the "paid without journal" flag; (h) an owner-declined GRN whose receipt journal failed keeps its flag with no correct exit.
- [x] (2026-10-06, `GRN.idempotencyKey`) GRN has no idempotency key (needs a column + `createGRN` in `runSerializable`): a lost response then a re-save records the receipt twice — softened only by the "check Penerimaan Barang first" message and fresh "already received" numbers on a restored draft. Opening the GRN page offline from the SW cache never knows the session, so no draft is restored or saved there.
- [ ] `BackofficeShell` treats a session that fails to refetch while offline as a logout (spinner + `router.replace('/login')`), so ANY office form vanishes when the tab regains focus without signal. Drafts survive, but the honest "needs signal" GRN screen is only visible until the tab switches. Fix: keep the last session while `!navigator.onLine`, or `refetchOnWindowFocus={false}` in the office.
- [x] (2026-10-06, `MaterialIssue.idempotencyKey` = `<woId>:<kunci>`, old AuditLog keys still recognised) `keluarkanBahan`'s key lives in `AuditLog.metadata` (no `MaterialIssue.idempotencyKey @unique`); proven on Postgres with `Promise.all`, untested on MariaDB. A resend with EDITED lines after a lost response returns the first issue. `issueAdditionalMaterials` still checks IN_PRODUCTION outside the transaction. `STOK_KURANG`/`VARIAN_TIDAK_VALID` are still recognised from other libraries' error TEXT (locked by tests, but they should throw coded errors).
- [ ] Linking an SPG request: a second order from the same request while the first is still in the phone queue is not prevented (the server links only the first); if the process dies between commit and tidy, a replacement that is then APPROVED keeps the link on the old order. `StoreRecommendation.fieldSalesOrderId` has no index (a serializable scan on MariaDB). The race guard is only provable on MariaDB.
- [ ] `getActionItemCounts` (also the sidebar badge on every navigation) now loads every unread `JOURNAL_PENDING` with metadata plus up to 2 journal queries instead of one COUNT — cheap today, revisit if van sales keep writing flags while `CASH` is unmapped. Counts are still per notification, not per document. Bell items (`NotificationQueue`) are not marked read when their flag resolves.
- [ ] Not seen in a browser as a phone user (the local session is an admin, whom `/pwa` redirects): the phone error page, the van "terputus" path, the reorder "no price" toast, the Rencana kirim refusal. `notFound()` on an office page falls back to the root not-found, outside the office shell (there is no `app/backoffice/not-found.tsx`).

### Audit fix phase 5 — salesman ↔ SPG connections + efficiency (2026-10-06)
- [ ] Owner decisions: (a) Tema/Keluar were removed from the SPG home header (now only in Lainnya); (b) an SPG checking in from "Toko saya" is also taken to the home, so a check-in meant for photos/checklist needs "Toko saya" again; (c) a check-in left open from another day no longer offers Jual on the home (the sale screen and server still allow it); (d) the "SPG hari ini" card tells the salesman Izin/Sakit/Cuti — maybe only "Izin hari ini"; (e) SPG returns at a store with no holder are seen by no salesman; (f) the shortfall kabar writes only the non-zero parts ("kurang 2 pcs"); (g) after an uncertain send, an EDITED cart resent with the same key returns the first order if it was recorded.

Local DB has no `putus.packRatio`, no sized goods except one, and no `StoreStockMovement` rows, so "+1 pack" and "Di rak" need seeded data to look at.

### Tahap A/B — security leftovers, anti-duplicates, owner decisions, office UI (2026-10-07)
- [ ] **Provisional owner decisions — remind the owner** (memory `keputusan-sementara-pemilik`; constants listed in the "Tahap A/B" rules). Plus decisions the agents took the same way: an edited resend with the same key returns the FIRST document; key scope GRN global / material issue per WO / store payment per cycle; a supplier-payment date may be back-dated but not after today (no lower bound — a payment dated before its GRN makes payables negative in an earlier period); "Lewat jatuh tempo" only for invoices without kontrabon/pending BKM/held return; returned material after FG receipts valued at most at the cost not yet carried; nameEn defaults to nameId; barcode quick format assumes the attribute is called `Size`.

Make the CI check "Type-check + lint" a required status check in GitHub branch protection (otherwise it only reports); apps/api has no eslint config.
- [ ] Security residue: `proxy.ts` still trusts permissions in the cookie (pages/actions that call `auth()` refresh within 60 s); `User.isActive` does not exist (only a deleted account invalidates a session); the portal limiter is per process (N processes → N × 30/min) and binary search through previews still narrows stock to multiples of 6; the web→api HMAC has no timestamp/nonce (replayable) and does not sign the query string; resync is not idempotent (double click = new batch); simultaneous portal submits with the same key both send a kabar.

### Finance — Journal & CoA
- [ ] **⛔ GL CUTOVER IS BLOCKED ON A BUSINESS ANSWER, NOT CODE — the purchase side of the business was never entered.** Measured on PROD 2026-08-08: **0 purchase orders, 0 GRNs**, and **0 of 9,389** eligible sales orders carry any COGS (15,852 lines: 9,116 NULL, 6,736 explicitly `0`, none positive). `InventoryValue` holds 51,944 units at `SUM(totalValue) = 0.00`. The zeros are RECORDED, not missing — `consumeOrder` ran 9,213 times and correctly stamped `cogs = 0` because `avgCost` was 0 every time. The fulfilment pipeline works; it is faithfully recording that the goods cost nothing. **Both previously-proposed remedies are dead:** a COGS backfill has nothing to backfill FROM (GRNs establish moving-average cost and there are none), and relaxing the sweep predicate would post revenue with no cost forever — a permanent 100% gross margin, turning a visible stall into an invisible falsehood. **Do not seed the CoA, map roles, or arm the cutover.** The open question is where purchases are recorded today and whether that data can enter the system; written up for the client in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-08-gl-blocker-client-note.md`. Everything else (ingestion, fulfilment, stock, field sales, canvassing, settlement reconciliation) is unaffected.
- [ ] Sweep-stall mechanic, still true and worth keeping even after the above is resolved: `postPendingSalesJournals` selects on `NOT (EXISTS revenue_journal AND EXISTS cogs_journal)` and takes the oldest 50, so an order whose Σ `SalesOrderItem.cogs` is permanently 0 can never satisfy the COGS half and keeps its slot in that window forever — tick 1 posts revenue for the oldest 50, every later tick re-selects the same 50, increments nothing (`pending` counts only non-`NOTHING_TO_POST` failures) and logs nothing, so order 51 onward is never journaled with no error anywhere. `set-gl-cutover-date.sql` pre-flight query 5 measures it per environment and the file refuses to be run until it reads 0 (PR #222).
- [x] (2026-10-08, period work) Settlement journals are now gated: a settlement whose WHOLE period ends before the cutover is refused (`SEBELUM_TANGGAL_MULAI`). Residual: a settlement straddling the cutover still posts in full, so its pre-cutover orders still credit AR that this ledger never debited — the store/marketplace opening AR (Saldo awal → piutang marketplace) is what absorbs it.
- [ ] `apps/api/src/jubelio/handlers/salesorder.handler.ts` stamps `shippedAt = completed_date ?? last_modified ?? new Date()` with `reportsShipped` keyed on `wms_status` alone, so re-ingesting a historical order after go-live (the settlement resync path does exactly this) with no `completed_date` stamps `shippedAt = now`, clearing the cutover floor and journaling a pre-cutover sale into an open period (PR #222).
- [ ] `finance.glCutoverDate` has no Settings card, no notification and no UI of any kind — it is set only by hand-run SQL, and the sole signal that the sweep is inert is a 5-minute `console.warn` into an unretained container log (PR #222).
- [x] Account-mapping UI: unmap/clear control (X button per mapped role) — PR #162.
- [ ] Prod's chart of accounts was NEVER BUILT — it is the bare 10-account SAK-EMKM skeleton with no postable detail leaves, zero `JournalAccountMapping` rows and zero journals (verified 2026-08-08). The `13 Hutang` mistyping this item used to describe was DEV, not prod. Nothing to audit until a chart exists, which is gated on the GL blocker above. When it is built: every posting role must map to a LEAF — `postJournal` rejects an account with children and only checks at write time, so the mapping page shows a broken mapping as healthy.
- [x] (2026-10-08) The chart template maps `CASH` → 1101 and `BANK` → 1102 (with `INVENTORY_VAN` and the 32 new roles). It is NOT installed on prod — installing it is a post-deploy step; the in-flight-van hazard below still applies at that moment.
- [x] `fix-hutang-account-type.sql` — run on dev 2026-08-06 (`affectedRows = 1`; `13 Hutang` ASET → `2101` LIABILITAS under `21`, all 4 journal lines intact, AP mapping followed by id). **It is a DEV-ONLY artifact and must never be run on prod**, where there is no `13` account and no AP mapping — it would no-op and tell you nothing.
- [ ] In-flight-van hazard: any van already holding stock when the van cash journals deploy was loaded BEFORE the load journal existed, so its value was never debited to `INVENTORY_VAN`. Its next sale credits `INVENTORY_VAN` against an account never debited, and its reconcile debits `INVENTORY` for a return that was never credited — driving `INVENTORY_VAN` negative and overstating `Persediaan`. Check `VanStock WHERE qty > 0` before mapping the `CASH`/`INVENTORY_VAN` roles; remedy is either (a) reconcile every van to empty first, or (b) post one manual opening journal (DR `INVENTORY_VAN` / CR `INVENTORY`) for the in-flight van book value.
- [ ] `VanStock.avgCost` is `Decimal(15,2)`, so a second load into a non-empty van re-blends at 2dp and leaves cent-scale residue in `INVENTORY_VAN` (bounded ≈ qty × 0.005 per blend; sub-rupiah in IDR).
- [x] (2026-10-08, `lib/finance/journals/peran-persediaan.ts`, found by the accounting trial) Finished goods were credited to `INVENTORY` while FG receipts debit `INVENTORY_FG`, so with separate accounts raw-material inventory went negative by the COGS. Now EVERY journal that moves finished goods uses `peranPersediaan(item.type)` / `nilaiPerPeranPersediaan` (sales COGS, sales return, GRN split per line, opname by scope, van load/reconcile, KOL samples). A new stock journal must do the same.
- [ ] **Accounting trial data is LEFT in the local Postgres on purpose** (2026-10-07, report + exports in the session scratchpad `uji-akuntansi/`): 35 `UJIAKUN` accounts, all 22 posting roles mapped to them, `finance.glCutoverDate = 2026-08-01`, Aug–Sep purchases/production/marketplace sales/settlements. Consequences while it stays: the dev server's 5-minute sales sweep is ARMED for shipped orders after 1 Aug, and every DB test runs against a fully mapped GL. Its cleanup scripts were lost with the scratchpad on 2026-10-08 (see the trial-2 item above).
- [ ] Trial findings still open: sewing/CMT cost is booked as an expense when paid, not into HPP per piece (August shows a loss); `issueMaterials` values the issue at the typed/PO price while stock leaves at average cost (GL can drift from the stock card); purchase PPN is not separated (PPN Masukan).
- [ ] `postJournal`'s findUnique-then-create means two concurrent posts for the same source can file a `JOURNAL_PENDING` with `reason: "ERROR"` on a P2002 even though the journal did post — a false alarm, no duplicate and no double-count.
- [ ] Van "Post journal" retry visibility (`lib/canvassing/journal-pending.ts`) reads every `JOURNAL_PENDING` row and matches `metadata.docId`/`kind` in JS, because JSON-path filtering is unreliable on this adapter. Uncapped by choice — a `take` window would hide a genuinely failed older post — and cheap today (6 such rows on dev). Revisit if `AdminNotification` grows, since nothing prunes it.
- [ ] Van journal retry is gated on a `JOURNAL_PENDING` notification existing (`hasPostableJournal`), but `postVanJournalSafely`'s own `notify` write is itself best-effort — if the journal post fails AND the notification write also fails (or the process dies after the stock transaction commits but before `notify` runs), the document has no journal, no notification, and no retry button: permanently unpostable except by hand. We can't add a durable attempt marker without a migration, so the real fix is a van equivalent of `lib/finance/sales/sweep.ts`'s `postPendingSalesJournals` — a sweep over van load/sale/reconcile documents that have no journal, independent of whether a notification was ever written. Must be scoped to specific document ids or a date window, per this file's own rule that a test-bed spec must never invoke a DB-wide sweep (see "What NOT to do").
- [x] (2026-10-08) Chart template "Dagang & produksi fesyen" (`lib/finance/templat-akun/`) creates the postable leaves and maps every role, keeping existing mappings; installed from Bagan akun (`/backoffice/finance/coa`, `KartuTemplatAkun`).
- [x] (2026-10-08) `/backoffice/finance/integritas`: unbalanced journals, orphans, documents without a journal (silent vs pending), reversed-sign balances, journals in closed months.
- [ ] Settlement journal `UNBALANCED` when the excel summary itself doesn't satisfy `Dilepas + Pengeluaran = Pendapatan`. The `MARKETPLACE_FEE_OTHER` residual absorbs fee-level gaps but is computed from `totalPengeluaran`, so it cannot absorb a summary-level mismatch (`settlement/journal.ts`) (PR #157, #17).
- [ ] Settlement `SP-`+orderNo match coverage is low for pre-2026-06-14 settlements — revisit if old-period reconciliation matters.
- [ ] Shopee fee itemization is coarse: `SettlementLine` persists 4 of ~10 charge columns on the Income sheet (captured: Komisi AMS, Administrasi, Layanan, Proses Pesanan; not itemized: Premi, Biaya Transaksi, Biaya Kampanye, Bea Masuk/PPN/PPh, Biaya Program Hemat Biaya Kirim, Biaya Isi Saldo Otomatis). Not a correctness gap — the journal totals from Summary's `Total Pengeluaran` with `MARKETPLACE_FEE_OTHER` absorbing the rest, and per-line profit uses `Total Penghasilan`, already net of every fee — so this only limits Laba Rugi's fee breakdown granularity. Verified against `reference/finance/Income.sudah dilepas.id.20260601_20260630.xlsx` (43 columns) on 2026-08-05. Adding columns means a migration + parser + journal-role work.
- [ ] `Biaya Layanan` is itemized per order on Shopee's Seller Fee sheet (Gratis Ongkir XTRA kategori F/G, Promo XTRA, Shopee Live XTRA) but only stored as raw JSON in `Settlement.sellerFeesRaw` — never parsed into per-line columns. Same granularity theme as the item above.
- [ ] Field-sales promo: order-level discount not pro-rated across `SalesHistory` lines — line totals sum > order total (`field-sales/writer.ts:194`), per D7 (PR #111).
- [x] Auto-journal supplier payments (DR Hutang / CR Bank) — the PO paid toggle now posts DR AP / CR BANK for the payable its receipts actually booked to the GL, and unmarking posts the mirror (PR #220).
- [ ] A PO marked paid with nothing booked raises `JOURNAL_PENDING` and posts nothing — the payment direction notifies on `NOTHING_TO_POST`, while the unmark direction stays silent because nothing to reverse is a genuine no-op. After the GRN-journal precondition below, the only causes left for that code are a genuine advance payment (no receipts), no receipt that could ever book a payable (each one sub-cent or owner-declined), or a payable already cleared by reversals; a receipt still owed with no journal returns `GRN_JOURNALS_INCOMPLETE` and one still awaiting the owner's decision returns `GRN_APPROVAL_PENDING` instead. Correct today, but advances have no representation in the GL at all until a real payment model exists.
- [ ] Supplier payment now REFUSES (`GRN_JOURNALS_INCOMPLETE`, raises `JOURNAL_PENDING`, posts nothing) while any receipt of the PO that is STILL OWED is missing its GRN journal — one of several or every last one — correct, because paying only the journaled subset under-pays and the rest reappears in payables the moment that GRN journal is retried. Owner-declined receipts are exempt (same as sub-cent ones: nothing was ever booked for them), so a declined receipt whose journal never posted no longer blocks its siblings and no longer gets told to post a journal for goods inventory already reversed — that PO now reports `NOTHING_TO_POST` when the declined receipt is its only one. The consequence is a second downstream effect for a stuck GRN `JOURNAL_PENDING`: it now blocks that PO's payment journal too, not just the receipt's own posting. Remedy is per-PO (post the missing GRN journal, then unmark + re-mark payment); a GRN journal sweep would clear both at once.
- [ ] Supplier payment also REFUSES (`GRN_REVERSAL_MISSING`, raises `JOURNAL_PENDING`, posts nothing) while an owner-declined receipt still carries its GRN journal with no `GRN_REVERSAL` — the declined payable is otherwise netted into the payment and over-pays payables and bank by that amount. Same downstream shape as the incomplete-journal refusal above, but a separate code because the remedy is the declined GRN's own reversal retry, not the receipt-journal one. Remedy: post the reversal from the GRN row, then unmark + re-mark payment.
- [ ] Supplier payment also REFUSES (`GRN_APPROVAL_PENDING`, raises `JOURNAL_PENDING`, posts nothing) while any receipt of the PO is an over-receive still awaiting the owner's approve-or-decline. An over-receive credits payables in full at receive time and only then waits, so paying before the decision and declining after leaves the bank cash-out standing while the decline debits payables a second time — payables negative, supplier overpaid on the books, and nothing adjusts the payment. Condition mirrors `declineGRNByOwner`'s own guard (`requiresOwnerApproval` set, neither `ownerApprovedAt` nor `ownerDeclinedAt` stamped), checked FIRST of the three preconditions because the decision is a prerequisite of the other two remedies. Remedy: owner approves or declines the pending receipt, then unmark + re-mark payment. Consequence: an over-receive left undecided now blocks its PO's payment journal, not just its own approval state.
- [ ] The `paidAt` toggle and its journal now run in ONE serializable transaction (`runSerializable`, retried on deadlock), so a mark and a concurrent unmark can no longer interleave — the old compare-and-swap only ordered the two `paidAt` writes, leaving a window where an unmark reversed nothing (silent `NOTHING_TO_POST`) and the mark's journal committed after it, ending unpaid with DR payables / CR bank standing. Residual by design: a journal failure still leaves the toggle committed (the post's error is caught inside the transaction and carried out), so the `JOURNAL_PENDING` notification — written after the transaction, never inside it — remains the only recovery signal, and the recovery is still unmark + re-mark. Both post-commit writes are hardened around that: the notification is written BEFORE the `POStatusHistory` row (a bookkeeping write must not be able to skip the durable flag) and the history insert is itself try/caught-and-logged (its failure must not reject the action and replace the operator's warning toast with a generic error).
- [ ] Supplier payment also REFUSES (`PAYMENT_SUPERSEDED`, raises `JOURNAL_PENDING`, posts nothing new) when a payment journal is already standing at the current generation for a DIFFERENT amount than the payable now reads. The generation counts reversals, so a reversal that failed to post freezes it while `paidAt` keeps toggling: mark (posts `poId#1`), unmark (reversal fails, `#1` still standing), journal another receipt, re-mark → `postJournal` matches `#1` and would have reported `created: true`-shaped success while the ledger cleared only the old, smaller amount. The refusal compares the standing journal's own posted total against the fresh payable in cents. Unlike every other code, payables and bank are NOT untouched here — they hold the earlier payment — and the message says so. Remedy: confirm the reversal actually posted, then re-mark. Residual: if the reversal keeps failing (e.g. an unmapped role), the PO stays stuck at the stale amount until that is fixed — by design, since the alternative is a second payment journal on the same generation. **Note the re-mark leg of that sequence is now blocked before it reaches this code** — `setPOPaidAt`'s `STANDING_PAYMENT_JOURNAL` guard refuses the mark while the reversal is still missing, and the reversal is posted from its own control on the PO detail page rather than by toggling (PR #220).
- [ ] BACKFILL GAP: every PO already marked paid before the supplier payment journal deploys has no `SUPPLIER_PAYMENT` journal and nothing creates one, so prod payables overstate by the total those receipts booked and supplier cash-out is missing for the whole pre-feature history. The only backfill today is per-PO unmark + re-mark (the unmark returns `NOTHING_TO_POST` and posts nothing, the re-mark posts at generation 1). The durable fix is a scoped sweep over `PurchaseOrder WHERE paidAt IS NOT NULL` with no payment journal — scoped to explicit ids or a date window, per this file's rule that a test-bed spec must never invoke a DB-wide sweep.
- [ ] `postGrnReversalJournal` dates its reversal `new Date()` instead of the GRN/journal it reverses (`inventory/grn-journal.ts`), so declining an over-receive GRN in a later period misstates both periods — the same defect the supplier payment reversal now avoids by dating to the payment journal's own date. Out of scope on the supplier-payment branch.
- [x] (2026-10-08) Document supplier payments choose the cash/bank account. The old toggle still assumes BANK.
- [x] (2026-10-08) Real `SupplierPayment` + allocations (partial, many documents per transfer, advances, PPh withheld). The toggle and its refusal codes remain for old POs; a PO cannot mix the toggle with document payments. Old toggle payments have no mirror row until the PO is toggled again (the subledger still reads them from journals; a backfill script was not written).
- [ ] DECISION NEEDED — should a document-state refusal ABORT the paid toggle instead of committing `paidAt`? Now scoped to THREE codes, not four: the `PAYMENT_SUPERSEDED` case was settled the other way late in PR #220, by a separate pre-CAS guard in `setPOPaidAt` that returns its own `refusal: "STANDING_PAYMENT_JOURNAL"` whenever the PO reads unpaid with a payment journal still standing. That guard preempts the writer's `PAYMENT_SUPERSEDED` rather than being it — the writer code is unchanged and still refuses post-CAS if ever reached directly. Committing that state through destroys its own recovery banner, and both UIs already withheld the mark, so enforcing it server-side was consistency rather than a new policy. The open three are `GRN_APPROVAL_PENDING`, `GRN_JOURNALS_INCOMPLETE`, `GRN_REVERSAL_MISSING`, which still leave `paidAt` set — as do the infrastructure codes (`UNMAPPED_ROLE`, `UNBALANCED`, `ERROR`), correctly, since a Finance misconfiguration must not block ops. For the three the refusal means "this PO is not payable yet, fix that document first", and committing `paidAt` manufactures the ledger/UI divergence the feature fights. The post runs inside the toggle's transaction, so aborting is a small change per code. Blocked on an owner call: whether ops may record cash leaving the bank while a receipt is still unapproved. **Unlike the superseded case, these three refuse in states where the UI still OFFERS the button — aborting removes operator capability, which is why it stayed a product decision** (PR #220).
- [ ] A PO left marked paid with NO payment journal is invisible after the page reloads — the mirror state (unpaid + standing journal) got a detector, a banner, and a withheld mark on both surfaces; this one has only the transient toast plus an `AdminNotification` nothing renders. A naive "paid without a payment journal" banner would false-positive permanently on the legitimate cases (advance payment, all-sub-cent, all-declined) with no dismiss path. So the fix is a payment-journal health panel on the PO detail (re-run `poBookedPayable`, show the code + the toast's own remedy sentence + a retry), not a one-line detector — or nothing, if the abort decision above is taken, since that removes most of what the panel would flag. The superseded half of this item is closed: the mark that used to create it is now refused (PR #220).
- [x] `/backoffice/supplier-payments` standing-payment signal — the register now disables Mark paid on exactly the POs the detail page does, off the same condition, with the reason rendered inline; `listPOs` gained an opt-in `withStandingPaymentJournal` extra backed by `poIdsWithStandingPaymentJournalWhileUnpaid` (fixed 3 queries per page, not 3 per row) (PR #220).
- [ ] Recovery still lives ONLY on the PO detail page — the register names the standing-payment state but carries no "Post reversal journal" control, so clearing it always means opening the PO. Narrowed, not closed, by the item above (PR #220).
- [ ] The list-row standing-payment flag is OPTIONAL on the row type and opt-in per caller, so a future third paid-toggle surface that forgets `withStandingPaymentJournal` silently gates nothing — fail-open. Optional on purpose (a caller that never checked must not be handed `false`, which reads as "safe to mark"), but only two surfaces exist today to keep it honest (PR #220).
- [ ] `poIdsWithStandingPaymentJournalWhileUnpaid`'s reversal read is one query with an `OR` term per unpaid id: round trips are constant, predicate width is not. Fine for a paginated caller; an unpaginated one asking for the flag is the thing to review (PR #220).
- [ ] `PAYMENT_SUPERSEDED` is now close to unreachable from the UI, since `setPOPaidAt`'s `STANDING_PAYMENT_JOURNAL` guard refuses the mark before the CAS that would reach it. Kept as the writer-level backstop (`postSupplierPaymentJournal` is independently callable), but it is exercised almost entirely by its spec now — don't mistake it for dead code (PR #220).
- [ ] The PO detail page hides both paid-toggle buttons for `DRAFT`/`CANCELLED` while the supplier-payments register shows them for every row, so a cancelled PO's paid mark is editable from one surface and not the other. Never a GL question — a payable needs a GRN and cancellation is blocked once any receipt exists — but the two surfaces should agree (PR #220).
- [ ] `getGrnJournalState.hasPostableReceiptJournal` is `hasValue && receipt == null` with no declined check, so the inventory UI offers "Post journal" on an owner-DECLINED GRN; taking the offer books a payable for goods inventory already reversed and pushes that PO into `GRN_REVERSAL_MISSING` (recoverable from the declined GRN's own reversal button). Pre-existing GRN-side wart, surfaced by the supplier payment audit, untouched by that branch.
- [ ] Van journal `JOURNAL_PENDING` dedup (`lib/canvassing/post-van-journal-safely.ts` `alreadyFlagged`) matches only `(docId, kind)`, so a van document that fails one way, gets fixed, and then fails a DIFFERENT way writes no new row — the operator is left with the stale first reason. Same weakness the supplier payment dedup just fixed by adding `reason` to the match; exposure is smaller because the van flow has only three distinct reasons (`UNMAPPED_ROLE`, `UNBALANCED`, `ERROR`) and they share one remedy family (map the account, then retry), whereas supplier payment has a longer list of codes with genuinely different remedies. Fix is the same one-line predicate change, deliberately left out of the supplier payment branch. Note `hasPostableJournal` gates the van retry button on ANY matching row, so the button survives either way — this is a wrong-message bug, not a lost-retry one.
- [ ] Supplier payment `JOURNAL_PENDING` dedup matches `(docId, kind, reason)`, so at most one unread row per distinct reason per PO and direction. Two residuals: an earlier reason's row lingers unread after the failure mode changes (nothing marks it superseded, so a feed would show both — the newest is correct, the older is history), and every `ERROR` collapses to one row regardless of the `detail` message. Marking read clears the first; both wait on the `AdminNotification` feed follow-up.
- [ ] Van journal outcome is invisible at the moment of the action: `loadVanAction`/`recordVanSaleAction`/`recordVanReconcileAction` call `postVanJournalSafely` and then return `{ok:true}` regardless, so `LoadVanForm`/`ReconcileVanForm`/`VanSellShell` always `toast.success` — the operator is told the document succeeded even when nothing posted to the GL. Same shape the supplier payment toggle just fixed by returning the journal failure and branching to a warning toast (`supplierPayments.journal.err.*` + `supplierPaymentJournalErrorKey`). Exposure is smaller than supplier payment's was, because the van doc detail pages DO render a `JOURNAL_PENDING` badge + "Post journal" retry, so the signal exists — it is just not where the operator is looking. Fix mirrors the supplier payment one: return the failure code from the three actions, add `canvassing`/`vanSale` `journal.err.*` messages, warn instead of confirming. Deliberately out of scope of the supplier payment branch.
- [ ] `/backoffice/supplier-payments` and the PO detail page are otherwise 100% hardcoded English with no i18n hook; the new supplier-payment journal warning toasts are the first translated strings on them, so an `id`-locale operator now sees an Indonesian warning beside English buttons. Net gain over no warning at all, but those two pages want a proper i18n pass.
- [ ] `SUPPLIER_PAYMENT` / `SUPPLIER_PAYMENT_REVERSAL` journals key `sourceId` as `poId#generation` (generation = reversals so far + 1), not a bare PO id — any future consumer looking up a PO's payment journal must prefix-match `poId#`. Nothing consumes it today.
- [x] (2026-10-08) Putus nota tagihan, BKM, store returns and goods exchange are journaled (`lib/finance/lapangan`); consignment revenue posts at cycle lock (`lib/finance/operasional`). Salesman commission is still not journaled (no payroll/commission payable exists).
- [x] (2026-10-08, provisional decision 37) SPG sales are not journaled on their own: putus-store sales are already revenue at the nota, konsi-store sales become revenue at cycle lock. Named in the disclosure note.
- [ ] Itemize TikTok fees from `escrow_list` instead of dumping them into `MARKETPLACE_FEE_OTHER`.
- [x] (2026-10-08) Month and year close (`YEAR_END_CLOSE` moves P&L to retained earnings); the injected unclosed-earnings line now shows only for years not yet closed and only when non-zero.
- [ ] Settlements journaled before the fee split keep their single lumped `MARKETPLACE_FEE` line — no reverse-and-repost migration was run.
- [ ] Settlement journal's bank and AR lines still throw an uncaught `BAD_LINE` when `totalDilepas` or `totalPendapatan` is zero (a both-zero journal line), which is realistic for a fully-refunded settlement period — same bug class as the fee-sign issue fixed in PR #215; `NON_POSTABLE_ACCOUNT` and `UNBALANCED` are handled, this case is not.
- [x] `NON_POSTABLE_ACCOUNT` settlement error string + toast case — done (PR #215); both locales name the cause (inactive or non-leaf mapped account) and the fix (Finance → Pemetaan Akun).
- [ ] Laba Rugi has no page-level empty state — it relies on per-section `emptyLabel` rows, unlike the Trial Balance sibling, so a period with no journals renders an all-zero skeleton instead of an empty state.
- [ ] Neraca's balanced/unbalanced note is on screen only — the print view and the Excel export omit it, unlike Trial Balance's tripwire note and Neraca's own opening-balance warning (both now exported). `BalanceSheet.isBalanced` is already available at both call sites; ~2 lines each.
- [ ] Statement disclosure copy is duplicated: `apps/web/lib/finance/reports/disclosures.ts` (used by the print views and Excel exports, which have no locale provider) restates the `financeReports.*` note strings in `id.json` verbatim, kept in step only by a header comment. A copy-editor touching the locale file will not know to update the other.
- [ ] Statement print views use a named `@page statement` for portrait geometry, which is Chromium-only — Firefox still prints them on the shared landscape sheet (`print.css`). Cosmetic (nothing clipped); fixing it properly needs the shared `@page` default split per print view.
- [ ] No test covers the mixed-sign settlement fee shape (positive summary `totalPengeluaran` with negative per-line fee columns) — hand-traced as safe for totals today, but a net-positive fee category would post mis-signed with the `MARKETPLACE_FEE_OTHER` residual silently absorbing the difference.
- [x] Settlement journal reported role `MARKETPLACE_FEE` when BOTH a category role and the legacy fallback were unmapped — `resolveFeeAccount` now rethrows the category role the operator actually hit, so the `UNMAPPED_ROLE` toast names the right mapping. Covered by a `journal.test.ts` case.
- [ ] Up to ten sequential account-mapping lookups run before `postJournal`'s idempotency short-circuit, so re-posting an already-posted settlement pays the full resolve cost (`settlement/journal.ts`).
- [ ] `MarketplaceFeeRole` in `finance/settlement/fee-split.ts` hand-duplicates five `POSTING_ROLES` entries — ``Extract<PostingRole, `MARKETPLACE_FEE_${string}`>`` would remove the drift risk (the compiler catches renames today, not additions).
- [ ] Laba Rugi renders every active revenue/HPP/expense account even at zero for the period, with no "show zero accounts" toggle like the Trial Balance has.
- [ ] `apps/web/lib/finance/settlement/tiktok-settlement-parser.ts`'s top-of-file comment is stale — it says the settlement journal posts `totalPengeluaran` as a single straight debit line, which stopped being true when the per-category fee split landed. Its `Math.abs()` rationale still stands.
- [ ] Cash flow print view is single-period — the previous-period comparison appears on screen and in Excel but not in the PDF, because three amount columns do not fit the portrait statement sheet. A landscape variant would fix it.
- [ ] Cash flow section classification has no bulk edit: clearing a large unclassified bucket is one account at a time. Fine for a small chart of accounts, painful for a real one.
- [x] The Klasifikasi Arus Kas page listed every balance-sheet account including non-postable parents — `listAccountSections` now returns LEAVES only, which was never cosmetic: tagging a PARENT as KAS silenced its own leaves out of the cash bucket while contributing a cash delta of zero, so the statement reported zero cash start and end under the green reconciled note. Inactive leaves are deliberately kept (not "postable leaves"), matching `getAccountBalances`, so a deactivated cash account still holding a balance stays in `cashAccountIds` and `kasAwal`; the row is dimmed on the page instead (feat/finance-cash-flow-statement).
- [ ] `getCashFlowReport` runs `getAccountBalances` twice when a comparison is shown (once per window) plus one opening-balance query. Acceptable for a report, but it is the heaviest read in the finance module.
- [ ] Cash flow has no DB-touching spec — `listAccountSections`, `getSectionByAccountId` and `getCashOpeningBalance` are covered only by reading. The pure engine, classifier and comparison are fully unit-tested.
- [ ] **KAS is now restricted to ASET at all three chokepoints** — `setCashFlowSectionAction` refuses with `KAS_REQUIRES_ASET`, the classification combobox disables the option off ASET, and `listAccountSections` only ever offers leaves. The earlier note here had the diagnosis BACKWARDS: `debit − credit` is the orientation `netChange` and `getCashOpeningBalance` already shared, and it was the ENGINE's `row.signed` that diverged, so a LIABILITAS account marked KAS (a bank overdraft, legitimate under IAS 7) read its movement with the sign flipped and doubled the cash side — a permanent false "data jurnal rusak" alarm on a balanced ledger, not a mislabelled opening balance. The engine now accumulates `row.debit - row.credit`, so all three paths state one orientation. Residual: an override stored BEFORE this branch can still hold KAS on a non-ASET account (nothing migrates existing rows, and the option stays selectable-looking in the trigger label so it is at least visible); and supporting overdraft-in-cash properly means orienting the whole cash path off `AccountType`, not relaxing the check (feat/finance-cash-flow-statement).
- [ ] Non-cash investing and financing transactions produce phantom GROSS flows in the section subtotals: buying a vehicle wholly on credit shows investing −50.000.000 and financing +50.000.000 though no cash moved. Inherent to deriving sections from balance deltas under the indirect method — only the NET is a measured figure, and it is correct. Needs a disclosure sentence saying the section subtotals may include non-cash transactions, not code.
- [ ] `kasAkhirActual` is documented as the "measured" closing cash figure but is itself derived (`kasAwal + Σ cash-account deltas`), and it is rendered on no surface — screen, print and Excel all show `kasAkhir`. Either surface it beside `kasAkhir` as the corroborating figure it is meant to be, or drop it and let `isReconciled` be the only consumer.
- [ ] A quiet period (`hasMovement === false`) hides `kasAwal`/`kasAkhir` entirely on the cash flow screen, while the print view has no such guard and renders the full statement at zero. The two surfaces disagree about what an empty period looks like, and the screen is arguably the wrong one — opening and closing cash are the figures a reader most wants from a quiet month.
- [ ] Cash flow `to < from` produces an inverted previous window and renders the "empty period" state rather than a validation message, so an operator who transposes the dates is told there are no journals instead of being told the range is backwards.
- [ ] The cash flow print view silently omits the previous-period comparison that both the screen and the Excel show. Deliberate (three amount columns do not fit the portrait sheet) but undisclosed — the PDF should at least say it is single-period. Related to the landscape-variant item above.
- [ ] Neither `setCashFlowSectionAction` nor `clearCashFlowSectionAction` has a try/catch, so an infrastructure failure or a P2025 on a concurrently-deleted account throws out of the server action and no toast fires at all — the operator sees the row simply not change. Both already return typed result codes precisely to avoid digest-masked throws; the uncaught path defeats that.
- [ ] `loadCashFlowReport` awaits the current and previous windows sequentially though they are independent — `Promise.all` would roughly halve the latency of the heaviest read in the finance module. Same call site as the double-`getAccountBalances` item above.
- [x] (fixed 2026-10-05 with the help sweep) `INCOME_STATEMENT_COVERAGE_BODY` carries the SAME staleness the cash flow coverage note was just fixed for: it still names "penjualan kanvas" and "pembayaran ke pemasok" as un-journaled, but van sales post `DR CASH / CR SALES_REVENUE` (`lib/canvassing/van-journal.ts`) and supplier payments post `DR AP / CR BANK` (`lib/purchasing/supplier-payment-journal.ts`) on master today. Mirrored in `financeReports.incomeStatement.coverageBody` in both locales. Left alone on the cash flow branch because it belongs to a different report.
- [ ] The cash flow comparison table has no `min-w-*`, so on a phone the 4-column layout compresses and wraps rather than scrolling inside its `overflow-x-auto`. Matches `TrialBalanceClient`'s existing house style, so fixing it is a sweep across the finance report clients rather than a one-file change.

### Production — Palet Warna & warna sablon
- [ ] The palette lives at `/backoffice/production/palette` (moved out of Creative on 2026-09-17 at the owner's request — it is production work). Viewing reuses `production_colors:view`; saving palettes and the team base-ink list needs the NEW `production_colors:manage`. The seed row exists only as Postgres SQL (`packages/db/prisma/postgres/seed-production-palette-permissions.sql`, ADMIN + PRODUCTION) and in `seed.ts` — **there is no MariaDB seed; run the equivalent surgical SQL on prod post-merge** or non-admin production users can open the page but every save returns `TANPA_AKSES`.
- [ ] Both `ColorPalette` MariaDB migrations (`20260917090000_color_palette`, `20260917120000_color_palette_fabric`) were written but never executed on any MariaDB — this branch runs on PostgreSQL.
- [ ] Base inks are a `SystemSetting` row keyed `production.print.baseInks` (renamed from `creative.sablon.baseInks` before anything was ever saved under the old key — nothing to migrate).
- [ ] Mix recipes are an uncalibrated Kubelka-Munk estimate from screen colour (`lib/production-colors/sablon/campur.ts`); mixes with white come out lighter in reality than predicted. Calibrating needs a handful of real sample measurements.
- [ ] Colour recommendations are sales-driven when data exists: `SalesHistory.color` (the marketplace VARIANT colour name, i.e. the garment colour, imported via Forecast → Import) is aggregated over the last 90 days vs the 90 before (`ambilPenjualanWarna`, completed orders only), names are resolved by a hand-written Indonesian market dictionary (`sablon/nama-warna.ts`, ~40 colours with approximate hex — "mocca" means slightly different colours to different stores) plus team mappings in `SystemSetting["production.print.colorNames"]`, and the result feeds a best-sellers card (use as fabric). Local dev has 0 `SalesHistory` rows, so this was verified with sample props only — check it against a real import. Known gaps: no province filter yet (the column exists), and `kenaliWarna` picks one colour for two-tone names ("Hitam Putih").
- [ ] Uploading a design reads its composition automatically (`bacaKomposisiDesain` → `KartuKomposisiDesain`, all in the browser) and offers it with other accents (`variasiDesain`). The "same design on other fabrics" gallery was removed at the owner's request on 2026-09-18: the fabric is the fixed reference. With the fabric LOCKED (padlock in the fabric card, `kainTerkunci` in `PaletClient`), the design is adapted to that fabric instead of bringing its own, and nothing else can change the fabric — fabric editor (replaced by a read-only view), the on/off switch, "Jadikan kain" from best sellers and the image picker, loading a saved palette with another fabric, and the design card. In fabric mode the contrast card shows only each ink on the fabric, on the sablon readability scale; the full WCAG ink-on-ink grid remains for free palettes only. The image picker's "Tebak komposisi" (fabric mode) runs the same reader, so its count means INKS, the design's fabric is its own row and never sent as an ink, and each ink shows its share of the printed area — the shares "Jadikan tinta sablon" then sets (pinned by a `keadaan.test.ts` case). Those inks are the image's exact colours with the nearest Pantone beside them; the design card's button snaps to Pantone codes instead. It treats the LARGEST colour as the fabric, which is wrong for an all-over print. Anti-aliased letter edges are merged into their ink before the 1% crumb threshold (`gabungTepian`): a colour of ≤3% lying on the straight Lab line from the fabric toward a farther ink (t 0.2–1, ≤6 off the line) counts as edge — on small designs thin black text is otherwise lost, because its edges outnumber its core. The 0.2 bound is calibrated on one real design (edges at t 0.24–0.73, its tone-on-tone at 0.13–0.16), so a genuine tone-on-tone ink under 3% that sits more than ~16 L* from the fabric toward black will be read as edge. Blends BETWEEN two inks (black outline on white) are not merged.
- [ ] "Keterbacaan Pantone C di kain ini" (`KartuKeterbacaanPantone`, logic in `sablon/keterbacaan-katalog.ts`) shows the WHOLE C book as "Aa" tiles on the current fabric with each colour's contrast ratio and sablon readability label, filterable by readability / colour family / code and sortable by contrast — the owner's own idea, so the team reads the numbers themselves instead of waiting for a guessed suggestion. Picking a tile previews large, headline and small lettering on the fabric and can apply it to any unlocked ink (with Undo). It shows metallic and neon too, with a switch to hide them, because the owner asked for "semua warna Pantone C"; the readability number is a screen-colour contrast, so a metallic's real print will differ.
- [ ] Automatic suggestions never round to a metallic (871–877, 8001–8999, 10101–10999 C) or neon (801–814, 901–942 C) Pantone (`pantoneKhusus` in `palet/pantone.ts`, skipped in `komposisi.ts` `bulatkan` and `saran.ts`): on screen they look like flat colours, but they print as special shimmering/fluorescent inks. Until 2026-09-18 they were suggested freely (687 of the 3,219 C entries), so palettes saved before then may carry one chosen automatically. Search and exact-code lookups still include them — metallic gold/silver are real sablon inks — so a metallic the user locks keeps its code.
- [ ] REMOVED at the owner's request on 2026-09-21: the composition recommendation gallery (`KartuRekomendasi`, `galeriKomposisi`/`lengkapiKunci`), the per-ink rows in the print-ink card (portion input, "Ganti", "Warna lain" with its kind/rule options in `sablon/pengganti.ts`, per-ink padlocks, per-ink recipe panels), and ink locks with them. Inks are now chosen from the Pantone C readability book; the print-ink card keeps the tee preview, the read-only shares and the copied summary with recipes. `keadaan.ts` still accepts optional `terkunci` indices (tested, currently passed by no caller). Recover any of it from the commit before the removal (`refactor(web): remove the composition gallery…`) if the owner wants it back. `batasKromaTinta` (loud-colour cap by ink share on muted fabrics) went with `pengganti.ts`.
